@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("galerkin_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class GalerkinAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        dropout: float = 0.1,
        basis_type: str = "ortho_dct",
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        layer_id: int | None = None,
        layer_idx: int | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn

        # ---- Config discovery ----
        if config is None and source_attn is not None:

            class DummyConfig:
                pass

            config = DummyConfig()
            out_layer = getattr(
                source_attn,
                "out_lin",
                getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "dense",
                    getattr(source_attn, "c_proj", getattr(source_attn, "o_proj", None)),
                ),
            )
            q_layer = getattr(
                source_attn,
                "q_lin",
                getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "query",
                    getattr(source_attn, "c_attn", getattr(source_attn, "q_proj", None)),
                ),
            )
            if out_layer is not None and hasattr(out_layer, "out_features"):
                config.hidden_size = out_layer.out_features
            elif q_layer is not None and hasattr(q_layer, "in_features"):
                config.hidden_size = q_layer.in_features
            else:
                config.hidden_size = 768
            config.num_attention_heads = getattr(
                source_attn,
                "n_heads",
                getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    getattr(source_attn, "num_heads", 12),
                ),
            )

        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        self.num_heads: int = (
            getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", getattr(config, "num_heads", 12))
            if config is not None
            else 12
        )
        self.num_kv_heads: int = (
            getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads)
            if config is not None
            else self.num_heads
        )
        hidden_size: int = (
            getattr(config, "hidden_size", getattr(config, "d_model", 768))
            if config is not None
            else 768
        )
        cfg_head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) if config is not None else None
        if cfg_head_dim is None and source_attn is not None:
            for attr in ("q_proj", "query", "q_lin", "W_q"):
                mod = getattr(source_attn, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    cfg_head_dim = mod.weight.shape[0] // max(1, self.num_heads)
                    break
        self.head_dim: int = (
            int(cfg_head_dim)
            if cfg_head_dim is not None
            else getattr(config, "d_kv", hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads))
        )
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat: int = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.rank: int = (
            rank
            if rank is not None
            else getattr(config, "galerkin_rank", getattr(config, "rank", 64))
        )
        self.basis_type: str = basis_type

        resolved_layer_id = (
            layer_id
            if layer_id is not None
            else (
                layer_idx
                if layer_idx is not None
                else kwargs.get("layer_id", kwargs.get("layer_idx", 0))
            )
        )
        self.layer_idx: int = int(resolved_layer_id)

        # ---- LoRA config ----
        self.lora_rank: int = getattr(
            config,
            "lora_rank",
            getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
        )
        self.lora_alpha: float = getattr(
            config,
            "lora_alpha",
            getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
        )
        self.freeze_base: bool = self.lora_rank > 0

        # ---- Decoder detection ----
        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            _mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if config is not None else ""
            _decoder_prefixes = (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
            is_dec = False
            if config is not None:
                if getattr(config, "is_decoder", False) or getattr(config, "is_causal", False):
                    is_dec = True
                if any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes):
                    is_dec = True
                if hasattr(config, "architectures") and any(
                    "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                    for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
                ):
                    is_dec = True
            if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
                is_dec = True
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal: bool = bool(self.is_decoder)

        # ---- Nested encoder detection ----
        is_nested = kwargs.get("is_nested_encoder")
        if is_nested is None and source_attn is not None:
            src_cls = source_attn.__class__.__name__
            if (
                src_cls
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (src_cls.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in src_cls.lower())
                or (hasattr(source_attn, "self") and hasattr(source_attn, "output"))
            ):
                is_nested = True
            elif (
                hasattr(source_attn, "out_lin")
                or hasattr(source_attn, "c_proj")
                or hasattr(source_attn, "o_proj")
            ):
                is_nested = False
        if is_nested is None:
            is_nested = False
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = bool(is_nested)

        # ---- Projections ----
        self.query = LoRALinear(
            hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.key = LoRALinear(
            hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.value = LoRALinear(
            hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_dropout_prob",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", dropout),
                ),
            )
            if config
            else dropout
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self.rotary_emb = None
        self.comp_config: Any | None = comp_config

        # GAL-FIX-07: small cache for the basis matrix
        self._basis_cache: dict[tuple[int, int], torch.Tensor] = {}

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ---- Aliases ----
    @property
    def q_proj(self):
        return self.query

    @property
    def k_proj(self):
        return self.key

    @property
    def v_proj(self):
        return self.value

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.query

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.key

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.value

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def W_q(self):
        return self.query

    @property
    def W_k(self):
        return self.key

    @property
    def W_v(self):
        return self.value

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    # ---- Weight transfer (unchanged from original, correct) ----
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        if has_out_lin:
            is_nested = False
        elif (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            and hasattr(src, "output")
            and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
        ):
            is_nested = True
            src = src.self
        elif cls_name in (
            "BertSelfAttention",
            "RobertaSelfAttention",
            "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
            "ElectraSelfAttention",
            "DebertaSelfAttention",
        ) or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower()):
            is_nested = True
        else:
            is_nested = False
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        if hasattr(src, "rotary_emb"):
            self.rotary_emb = src.rotary_emb

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                cls_c = c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__
                base_cls_c = base_c.__class__.__name__
                if (
                    "Conv1D" in cls_c
                    or "Conv1D" in base_cls_c
                    or hasattr(c_attn_mod, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or (
                        c_w.ndim == 2
                        and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1]
                        and "Linear" not in cls_c
                        and "Linear" not in base_cls_c
                    )
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    cls_o = c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__
                    base_cls_o = base_o.__class__.__name__
                    if (
                        "Conv1D" in cls_o
                        or "Conv1D" in base_cls_o
                        or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                        or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                        or (
                            o_w.ndim == 2
                            and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1]
                            and "Linear" not in cls_o
                            and "Linear" not in base_cls_o
                        )
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. BERT / RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query", "W_q"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key", "W_k"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value", "W_v"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj", "proj", "output"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback warning: %s", e)

        target_map = {
            "q": self.query,
            "k": self.key,
            "v": self.value,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

                if (
                    w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                    and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape
                ):
                    w = w.t().contiguous()
                out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))

                target_mod.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(
                        device=target_mod.weight.device, dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype
                    )
                )

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    min_b = min(target_mod.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target_mod.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(
                        b[:min_b].to(device=target_mod.bias.device, dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype)
                    )
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                w_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.data.float()).item())
                if w_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning("GalerkinAttention: %s near-zero norm (%.2e)", proj_key, w_norm)

                if self.freeze_base:
                    target_mod.freeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Basis construction (GAL-FIX-07: cached)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _get_orthonormal_basis(
        self,
        N: int,
        r: int,
        device: torch.device,
        dtype: torch.dtype,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        r_actual = max(1, min(r, N))
        key = (N, r_actual)
        cached = self._basis_cache.get(key)
        if cached is not None and cached.device == device and cached.dtype == dtype:
            return cached

        n = torch.arange(N, device=device, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(1)
        k = torch.arange(r_actual, device=device, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)

        phi = torch.cos((math.pi * k * (2.0 * n + 1.0)) / (2.0 * max(1, N)))
        phi[:, 0] = phi[:, 0] * (1.0 / math.sqrt(max(1, N)))
        if r_actual > 1:
            phi[:, 1:] = phi[:, 1:] * math.sqrt(2.0 / max(1, N))

        eye = torch.eye(N, r_actual, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        phi = phi + 1e-4 * eye
        phi, _ = torch.linalg.qr(phi)
        phi = phi.to(dtype=dtype)
        self._basis_cache[key] = phi
        return phi

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        is_cross_attention = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states
        kv_len = kv_input.shape[1]

        # 1. Linear projections
        Q = self.query(hidden_states).view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.key(kv_input).view(B, kv_len, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.value(kv_input).view(B, kv_len, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # 2. RoPE (GAL-FIX-05)
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross_attention:
            if isinstance(position_embeddings, (tuple, list)):
                cos, sin = position_embeddings[0], position_embeddings[1]
            else:
                cos, sin = position_embeddings, None
            if sin is not None:
                Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, position_ids)

        # 3. GQA
        if self.num_kv_heads != self.num_heads:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross_attention else attention_mask

        # 4. Decoder causal path
        if self.is_decoder and not is_cross_attention:
            # GAL-FIX-03: only use SDPA when we don't need attn weights/head_mask
            if not output_attentions and head_mask is None:
                try:
                    _qs = Q.half() if Q.dtype == torch.float32 else Q
                    _ks = K.half() if K.dtype == torch.float32 else K
                    _vs = V.half() if V.dtype == torch.float32 else V
                    _is_causal = N > 1
                    _sdpa_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                        _qs,
                        _ks,
                        _vs,
                        is_causal=_is_causal,
                        dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                    )
                    _sdpa_out = _sdpa_out.to(hidden_states.dtype)
                    _sdpa_out = (
                        _sdpa_out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
                    )
                    _sdpa_out = torch.nan_to_num(_sdpa_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
                    out = self.out_proj(_sdpa_out) if not self.is_nested_encoder else _sdpa_out
                    return out, None
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # Manual causal path
            scale = d**-0.5
            scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            # GAL-FIX-04: explicit causal mask
            causal_diag = 1 + max(0, kv_len - N)
            causal_mask = torch.triu(
                torch.full((N, kv_len), float("-inf"), device=device, dtype=torch.float32),
                diagonal=causal_diag,
            )
            scores = scores + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

            if eff_mask is not None:
                if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                    pad_m = (1.0 - eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2).float()) * -1e4
                    scores = scores + pad_m
                elif eff_mask.dim() == 3:
                    scores = scores + eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
                elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                    scores = scores + eff_mask.float()

            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            scores_max = scores.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True).values.detach()
            exp_scores = torch.exp((scores - scores_max).clamp(min=-50.0, max=0.0))
            denom = exp_scores.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-6)
            probs = (exp_scores / denom).to(dtype)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), head_mask.size(1), 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
                probs = probs * head_mask.to(probs.dtype)

            probs = self.dropout(probs)
            context = torch.matmul(probs, V)
            attn_weights = probs if output_attentions else None

        # 5. Encoder: softmax in basis space (GAL-FIX-01: original formulation)
        else:
            valid_mask = get_token_valid_mask(eff_mask, B, kv_len, device)
            if valid_mask is None:
                valid_mask = torch.ones(B, kv_len, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)
            mask_expanded = valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype)

            K_masked = K * mask_expanded
            V_masked = V * mask_expanded

            r_actual = max(1, min(self.rank, kv_len))
            Phi = self._get_orthonormal_basis(kv_len, r_actual, device, dtype)
            # Phi: (N, r)

            # Project into basis
            Q_basis = torch.einsum("bhnd,nr->bhrd", Q, Phi)  # (B, H, r, d)
            K_basis = torch.einsum("bhnd,nr->bhrd", K_masked, Phi)
            V_basis = torch.einsum("bhnd,nr->bhrd", V_masked, Phi)

            scale = d**-0.5
            scores = (
                torch.matmul(Q_basis.float(), K_basis.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous())
                * scale
            )
            # (B, H, r, r)

            # Numerically stable softmax in basis space
            scores_max = scores.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True).values.detach()
            exp_scores = torch.exp((scores - scores_max).clamp(min=-50.0, max=0.0))
            denom = exp_scores.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-6)
            probs = exp_scores / denom
            probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), head_mask.size(1), 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
                probs = probs * head_mask.to(probs.dtype)

            probs = self.dropout(probs)

            # Attention in basis space
            O_basis = torch.matmul(probs.to(dtype), V_basis)  # (B, H, r, d)

            # Reconstruct sequence representation
            context = torch.einsum("bhrd,nr->bhnd", O_basis, Phi)  # (B, H, N, d)

            # Apply padding mask on outputs
            if eff_mask is not None and not is_cross_attention:
                context = context * mask_expanded

            attn_weights = probs if output_attentions else None

        # 6. Reshape and output projection
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out, attn_weights

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("linformer_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class LinformerAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    triton_kernel: str = "linear_attn"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        k: int | None = None,
        max_seq_len: int | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn

        # LF-02: robust super().__init__
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        # LF-04: robust dimension detection
        hidden_size = self._detect_hidden_size(config, source_attn, kwargs)
        num_heads = self._detect_num_heads(config, source_attn, kwargs, hidden_size)
        num_kv_heads = self._detect_num_kv_heads(config, source_attn, kwargs, num_heads)
        head_dim = self._detect_head_dim(config, source_attn, kwargs, hidden_size, num_heads)

        self.dim = int(hidden_size)
        self.hidden_size = self.dim
        self.num_heads = int(num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = int(num_kv_heads)
        self.head_dim = int(head_dim)
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        # Linformer hyperparams
        k_val = (
            k
            if k is not None
            else getattr(
                comp_config,
                "linformer_k",
                getattr(comp_config, "k", None) if comp_config else None,
            )
        )
        if k_val is None:
            k_val = getattr(config, "linformer_k", getattr(config, "k", 64)) if config else 64
        self.k_proj_dim = int(k_val)

        pos_emb = getattr(config, "max_position_embeddings", None) if config else None
        if pos_emb is None:
            pos_emb = max_seq_len if max_seq_len is not None else 512
        self.max_seq_len = max(512, int(pos_emb))

        # LF-11: centralized decoder detection
        self.is_decoder = _detect_decoder(
            config, is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal"), source_attn
        )
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # LoRA config
        self.lora_rank = int(
            (getattr(config, "lora_rank", None) if config else None)
            or (getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", None) if comp_config else None)
            or kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            (getattr(config, "lora_alpha", None) if config else None)
            or (getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", None) if comp_config else None)
            or kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)
        )

        # LF-12: projection rank for FactorizedLoRALinear
        self.proj_rank = int(
            kwargs.get("proj_rank")
            or (getattr(comp_config, "proj_rank", None) if comp_config else None)
            or (rank if rank is not None else 64)
        )

        # FactorizedLoRALinear projections
        FF = FactorizedLoRALinear
        self.q_proj = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.k_proj = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.v_proj = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = FF(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )

        # Linformer projections: E, F: (k, max_seq_len)
        self.E = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.k_proj_dim, self.max_seq_len))
        self.F = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.k_proj_dim, self.max_seq_len))
        with torch.no_grad():
            nn.init.normal_(self.E, std=0.02)
            nn.init.normal_(self.F, std=0.02)
            # LF-15: only touch the min(k, max_seq) block
            min_dim = min(self.k_proj_dim, self.max_seq_len)
            self.E.data[:min_dim, :min_dim].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
            self.F.data[:min_dim, :min_dim].fill_diagonal_(1.0)

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_dropout_prob",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
                ),
            )
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)
        self.triton_kwargs = {"phi": "identity", "eps": 1e-6}

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LF-04: dimension detection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _detect_hidden_size(config: Any, src: nn.Module | None, kwargs: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        for k in ("hidden_size", "d_model", "n_embd", "dim"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("hidden_size", "d_model", "n_embd", "dim"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for attr in ("out_lin", "out_proj", "o_proj", "dense", "c_proj"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "out_features"):
                    return int(mod.out_features)
            for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "c_attn"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "in_features"):
                    return int(mod.in_features)
        return 768

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_num_heads(
        config: Any, src: nn.Module | None, kwargs: dict[str, Any], hidden_size: int
    ) -> int:
        for k in ("num_heads", "num_attention_heads", "n_heads", "n_head"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("num_attention_heads", "num_heads", "n_heads", "n_head"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for k in ("num_heads", "num_attention_heads", "n_heads"):
                v = getattr(src, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        return max(1, hidden_size // 64)

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_num_kv_heads(
        config: Any, src: nn.Module | None, kwargs: dict[str, Any], num_heads: int
    ) -> int:
        for k in ("num_key_value_heads", "num_kv_heads"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("num_key_value_heads", "num_kv_heads"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            v = getattr(src, "num_key_value_heads", None)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        return num_heads

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_head_dim(
        config: Any,
        src: nn.Module | None,
        kwargs: dict[str, Any],
        hidden_size: int,
        num_heads: int,
    ) -> int:
        v = kwargs.get("head_dim")
        if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
            return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("head_dim", "kv_channels", "d_kv"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight is not None:
                    try:
                        return int(mod.weight.shape[0] // max(1, num_heads))
                    except Exception:
                        pass
        return max(1, hidden_size // max(1, num_heads))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Aliases
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SVD init helper
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _svd_init(w: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor | None, proj: FactorizedLoRALinear) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = w.detach().float()
            if W.ndim != 2:
                W = W.reshape(W.size(0), -1)

            out_t, in_t = proj.out_features, proj.in_features
            if W.shape[0] != out_t or W.shape[1] != in_t:
                padded = torch.zeros(out_t, in_t, dtype=W.dtype, device=W.device)
                r = min(out_t, W.shape[0])
                c = min(in_t, W.shape[1])
                padded[:r, :c] = W[:r, :c]
                W = padded

            try:
                U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("Linformer._svd_init SVD failed: %s", e)
                return

            r = min(proj.rank, U.size(1), Vh.size(0))
            proj.down.weight.data.zero_()
            proj.up.weight.data.zero_()
            proj.down.weight.data[:r, :].copy_(Vh[:r, :].to(proj.down.weight.dtype))
            US = U[:, :r] @ torch.diag(S[:r])
            proj.up.weight.data[:, :r].copy_(US.to(proj.up.weight.dtype))

            if b is not None and proj.up.bias is not None:
                n = min(b.numel(), proj.up.bias.numel())
                proj.up.bias.data[:n].copy_(b[:n].to(proj.up.bias.dtype))
            elif proj.up.bias is not None:
                proj.up.bias.data.zero_()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights (LF-07, LF-08, LF-11)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[1] == 3 * self.hidden_size)
                )
                if is_conv:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. BERT / RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"LinformerAttention.copy_weights: missing mandatory projections "
                f"{missing_after} from {cls_name}."
            )

        def _orient(w, in_dim):
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        def _resize_gqa(w, b, out_target):
            """LF-07: adapt w for GQA: average down or repeat to match out_target."""
            if w is None:
                return w, b
            src_out = w.shape[0]
            if src_out == out_target:
                return w, b
            if src_out > out_target and src_out % out_target == 0:
                rep = src_out // out_target
                w = w.view(out_target, rep, -1).mean(dim=1)
                if b is not None:
                    b = b.view(out_target, rep).mean(dim=1)
            elif src_out < out_target and out_target % src_out == 0:
                rep = out_target // src_out
                w = w.repeat(rep, 1)
                if b is not None:
                    b = b.repeat(rep)
            return w, b

        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q, _ = extracted["q"]
            w_k, _ = extracted["k"]
            w_v, _ = extracted["v"]
            w_o, b_o = extracted["o"]

            if w_q is not None:
                self._svd_init(_orient(w_q, self.hidden_size), None, self.q_proj)
            if w_k is not None:
                w_k_o = _orient(w_k, self.hidden_size)
                w_k_res, _ = _resize_gqa(w_k_o, None, self.k_proj.out_features)
                self._svd_init(w_k_res, None, self.k_proj)
            if w_v is not None:
                w_v_o = _orient(w_v, self.hidden_size)
                w_v_res, _ = _resize_gqa(w_v_o, None, self.v_proj.out_features)
                self._svd_init(w_v_res, None, self.v_proj)
            if w_o is not None:
                self._svd_init(_orient(w_o, self.all_head_size), b_o, self.out_proj)
            elif self.is_nested_encoder:
                eye = torch.eye(
                    self.all_head_size,
                    self.all_head_size,
                    device=self.out_proj.down.weight.device,
                    dtype=self.out_proj.down.weight.dtype,
                )
                self._svd_init(eye, None, self.out_proj)

            # LF-08: norm verification via float() cast
            for name, mod in (
                ("q_proj", self.q_proj),
                ("k_proj", self.k_proj),
                ("v_proj", self.v_proj),
                ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
            ):
                try:
                    w_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(mod.weight.float()).item())
                    if w_norm < 1e-4:
                        logger.warning(
                            "LinformerAttention: weight '%s' has near-zero norm (%.2e).",
                            name,
                            w_norm,
                        )
                except Exception:
                    pass

            for mod in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

        # LF-11: inherit is_decoder
        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)
            self.is_causal = self.is_decoder

        if hasattr(src, "rotary_emb") and src.rotary_emb is not None:
            self.rotary_emb = src.rotary_emb
        else:
            if not hasattr(self, "rotary_emb"):
                self.rotary_emb = None

        # LF-13: forward sanity check
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                d = next(self.parameters()).device
                dt = self.q_proj.down.weight.dtype
                dummy = torch.randn(1, 8, self.dim, device=d, dtype=dt)
                _ = self.forward(dummy)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("LinformerAttention forward sanity check warning: %s", e)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # LF-05: RoPE with past_len offset
        pos_emb = (
            position_embeddings
            if position_embeddings is not None
            else kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        )
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) >= 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]
            past_len = 0
            if past_key_value is not None:
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 1:
                    past_len = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
                elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                    past_len = past_key_value.key_cache[0].shape[-2]
            if past_len > 0:
                cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + N, :]
                sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + N, :]
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        # Mask K, V (LF-14: shape-safe)
        valid_mask = None
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    valid_mask = attention_mask
                elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    valid_mask = attention_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    valid_mask = attention_mask > 0
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    v_tmp = attention_mask
                elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    v_tmp = attention_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    v_tmp = attention_mask > 0
                valid_mask = v_tmp.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
            else:
                valid_mask = torch.ones(B, N, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

        if valid_mask is not None:
            mask_expanded = valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype)
            k = k * mask_expanded
            v = v * mask_expanded
        else:
            mask_expanded = torch.ones(B, 1, N, 1, device=device, dtype=dtype)

        L = N
        k_eff = min(self.k_proj_dim, L)

        # E, F: (k_proj_dim, max_seq_len) → (k_eff, L)
        if self.max_seq_len >= L:
            E_act = self.E[:k_eff, :L].to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
            F_act = self.F[:k_eff, :L].to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
        else:
            # LF-10: interpolation with 3D input
            E_act = (
                F.interpolate(
                    self.E[:k_eff].unsqueeze(0).float(),
                    size=L,
                    mode="linear",
                    align_corners=False,
                )
                .squeeze(0)
                .to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
            )
            F_act = (
                F.interpolate(
                    self.F[:k_eff].unsqueeze(0).float(),
                    size=L,
                    mode="linear",
                    align_corners=False,
                )
                .squeeze(0)
                .to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
            )

        # Project key/value sequence: (B, H, k_eff, d)
        K_proj = torch.matmul(E_act.to(k.dtype), k)
        V_proj = torch.matmul(F_act.to(v.dtype), v)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
        scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), K_proj.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous().float()) * scale

        # LF-09: proper causal masking for projected K/V
        if self.is_decoder:
            if self.k_proj_dim >= L:
                # Exact causal on full sequence — recompute without projection
                causal_scores = (
                    torch.matmul(q.float(), k.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous().float()) * scale
                )
                causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, L, device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
                scores = causal_scores.masked_fill(~causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)
                V_proj = v
            else:
                # Approximate causal on projected K/V: mask each projected key by its mean position
                torch.tril(torch.ones(L, k_eff, device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
                # For each query position i, allow projected keys whose support range includes <= i
                # Simplified: allow key j if j <= i (proportional to support)
                idx_q = torch.arange(L, device=device).unsqueeze(1)  # (L, 1)
                idx_k = torch.arange(k_eff, device=device).unsqueeze(0)  # (1, k_eff)
                # Each projected key j covers roughly L/k_eff positions
                step = max(1, L // k_eff)
                allowed = (idx_k * step) <= idx_q  # (L, k_eff)
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~allowed.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)

        if valid_mask is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1), -1e4)

        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
        attn = torch.nan_to_num(attn, nan=0.0)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)

        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                hm = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
            else:
                hm = head_mask
            attn = attn * hm.to(attn.dtype)

        context = torch.matmul(attn, V_proj.to(attn.dtype))
        if valid_mask is not None:
            context = context * mask_expanded
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out.to(dtype), (attn if output_attentions else None)

Expand source code 
class LoRALinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features,
        out_features,
        bias=True,
        lora_rank=0,
        lora_alpha=1.0,
        freeze_base=True,
        device=None,
        dtype=None,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        fk = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.lora_rank = lora_rank
        self.lora_alpha = lora_alpha
        self.scaling = lora_alpha / max(1, lora_rank)

        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_features, in_features), **fk))
        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, **fk))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        if self.bias is not None:
            fan_in, _ = nn.init._calculate_fan_in_and_fan_out(self.weight)
            bound = 1 / math.sqrt(fan_in) if fan_in > 0 else 0
            nn.init.uniform_(self.bias, -bound, bound)

        if lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((lora_rank, in_features), **fk))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_features, lora_rank), **fk))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        if freeze_base:
            self.weight.requires_grad = False
            if self.bias is not None:
                self.bias.requires_grad = False

    def forward(self, x):
        w = self.weight.to(x.dtype) if self.weight.dtype != x.dtype else self.weight
        b = (
            self.bias.to(x.dtype)
            if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != x.dtype)
            else self.bias
        )
        out = F.linear(x, w, b)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
            bb = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
            out = out + F.linear(F.linear(x, a), bb) * self.scaling
        return out

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

    b = (
        self.bias.to(x.dtype)
        if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != x.dtype)
        else self.bias
    )

    if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
        bb = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
        out = out + F.linear(F.linear(x, a), bb) * self.scaling
    return out

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class LowRankSparseLinear (down: nn.Linear,
up: nn.Linear,
sparse_weight: torch.Tensor,
bias: torch.Tensor | None = None)
Expand source code 
class LowRankSparseLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    y = up(down(x)) + F.linear(x, S) + b

    S is a trainable sparse residual. Its L1 gradient encourages zero
    entries; periodic `prune_sparse` enforces hard threshold.

    SP-FIX-02: bias stored as a proper nn.Parameter.
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        down: nn.Linear,
        up: nn.Linear,
        sparse_weight: torch.Tensor,
        bias: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.down = down
        self.up = up
        self.sparse_weight = nn.Parameter(sparse_weight)
        if bias is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(bias.clone())
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        self.in_features = down.in_features
        self.out_features = up.out_features

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.matmul(self.up.weight, self.down.weight) + self.sparse_weight

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # SP-FIX-11: dtype-safe
        down_w = self.down.weight.to(x.dtype)
        up_w = self.up.weight.to(x.dtype)
        up_b = self.up.bias.to(x.dtype) if self.up.bias is not None else None

        x_lr = F.linear(F.linear(x, down_w), up_w, up_b)
        x_sp = F.linear(x, self.sparse_weight.to(x.dtype))
        out = x_lr + x_sp
        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias.to(out.dtype)
        return out

    def prune_sparse(self, sparsity: float) -> None:
        """Hard threshold: keep only top (1 - sparsity) fraction of entries."""
        with torch.no_grad():
            w = self.sparse_weight.data
            if w.numel() == 0:
                return
            keep = int(w.numel() * (1.0 - sparsity))
            if keep <= 0:
                w.zero_()
                return
            if keep >= w.numel():
                return
            flat = w.abs().view(-1)
            kth = torch.kthvalue(flat, w.numel() - keep).values
            w.mul_(w.abs() >= kth)

    def sparse_l1_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.sparse_weight.abs().sum()

y = up(down(x)) + F.linear(x, S) + b
