class NASCAttention (config: Any,
rank: int | None = 16,
window_size: int = 64,
block_size: int = 32,
top_k: int = 2,
rff_features: int = 64,
pretrained_attn: torch.nn.modules.module.Module | None = None,
lora_rank: int = 16,
lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
is_decoder: bool | None = None,
**kwargs: Any)
Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("nasc_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class NASCAttention(BaseAttention):
    r"""NASC: NEXUS-AEGIS Spectral Cascade Attention.
    Hardened for multi-architecture deployment across DistilBERT, RoBERTa, GPT-2, and Qwen3.5.
    """

    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any,
        rank: int | None = 16,
        window_size: int = 64,
        block_size: int = 32,
        top_k: int = 2,
        rff_features: int = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.hidden_size: int = getattr(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        self.num_heads: int = getattr(
            config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 4)
        )

        cfg_head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) if config else None
        if cfg_head_dim is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(cfg_head_dim)
        elif kwargs.get("head_dim") is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(kwargs["head_dim"])
        elif self.num_heads > 0:
            self.head_dim = self.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        else:
            self.head_dim = 64
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim

        cfg_kv = (
            getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
            if config
            else None
        )
        if cfg_kv is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv)
        elif kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(kwargs["num_kv_heads"])
        else:
            self.num_kv_heads = self.num_heads

        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))
        self.kv_repeat: int = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.window_size: int = window_size
        self.block_size: int = block_size
        self.top_k: int = top_k
        self.rff_features: int = rff_features
        self.lora_rank: int = lora_rank
        self.lora_alpha: float = lora_alpha
        self.enable_cascade_on_encoders: bool = bool(
            kwargs.get(
                "enable_cascade_on_encoders",
                getattr(config, "enable_cascade_on_encoders", True),
            )
        )

        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            m_type = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "")).lower() if config else ""
            self.is_decoder = (
                getattr(config, "is_decoder", False)
                or getattr(config, "causal", False)
                or any(
                    m_type.startswith(p)
                    for p in (
                        "gpt",
                        "qwen",
                        "llama",
                        "mistral",
                        "gemma",
                        "phi",
                        "opt",
                        "bloom",
                        "falcon",
                        "olmo",
                        "mpt",
                        "stablelm",
                    )
                )
                if config
                else False
            )

        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )

        rff_init = torch.empty(self.head_dim, rff_features)
        nn.init.orthogonal_(rff_init)
        self.rff_proj = nn.Parameter(rff_init, requires_grad=False)
        self.gate_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, 3)

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    @property
    def query(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.v_proj

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. UniversalAdapter extraction
        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter extraction fallback for %s: %s", proj, e)

        # 2. GPT-2 Fused c_attn Conv1D handling
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if weight is not None:
                weight = weight.data.clone()
                bias = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                bias_data = bias.data.clone() if bias is not None else None
                c_cls = getattr(base_c, "__class__", type(base_c)).__name__
                if (
                    ("Conv1D" in c_cls)
                    or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or ("Conv1D" in getattr(c_attn_mod, "__class__", type(c_attn_mod)).__name__)
                    or hasattr(c_attn_mod, "nf")
                    or (weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[1] == 3 * self.hidden_size)
                ):
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
                q_w = weight[:split_size, :]
                k_w = weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :]
                v_w = weight[2 * split_size :, :]
                q_b = bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
                k_b = bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
                v_b = bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (q_w, q_b)
                extracted["k"] = (k_w, k_b)
                extracted["v"] = (v_w, v_b)

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                b_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                p_cls = getattr(base_o, "__class__", type(base_o)).__name__
                if (
                    ("Conv1D" in p_cls)
                    or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                    or ("Conv1D" in getattr(c_proj_mod, "__class__", type(c_proj_mod)).__name__)
                    or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                    or (w_o.ndim == 2 and w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1])
                ):
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                extracted["o"] = (w_o, b_o.data.clone() if b_o is not None else None)

        # 3. RoBERTa / BERT Nested Encoder handling
        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query"):
                extracted["q"] = (s.query.weight.data, getattr(s.query, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "key"):
                extracted["k"] = (s.key.weight.data, getattr(s.key, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "value"):
                extracted["v"] = (s.value.weight.data, getattr(s.value, "bias", None))

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            dense = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (dense.weight.data, getattr(dense, "bias", None))

        # 4. Modern Decoders (Qwen3.5, LLaMA, Mistral) & DistilBERT
        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        if hasattr(layer, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                layer.weight.data,
                                getattr(layer, "bias", None),
                            )
                            break

        # Detect nested encoder architecture
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, attr)
            for attr in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        if hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = False
        elif (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
            )
            and not has_out_proj
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
        else:
            self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # Check required weights
        missing = [k for k in ["q", "k", "v"] if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.warning("Module copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights.")

        # Frobenius norm validation
        for proj_key in ["q", "k", "v"]:
            w = extracted[proj_key][0]
            if w is not None:
                norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float())
                if norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"NASCAttention extracted weight for {proj_key} has near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm.item():.6f})"
                    )

        if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
            norm_o = torch.linalg.norm(extracted["o"][0].float())
            if norm_o < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"NASCAttention extracted weight for output projection has near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm_o.item():.6f})"
                )

        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        # Copy into projection modules
        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in [
                ("q", self.q_proj),
                ("k", self.k_proj),
                ("v", self.v_proj),
                ("o", self.out_proj),
            ]:
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    in_dim = self.all_head_size if proj_key == "o" else self.hidden_size
                    w_oriented = _orient(w, in_dim)
                    target_w = target.weight
                    out_d, in_d = target_w.shape
                    src_out, src_in = w_oriented.shape

                    if proj_key in ("k", "v"):
                        if src_out == out_d:
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented)
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b)
                        elif src_out > out_d and src_out % out_d == 0:
                            rep = src_out // out_d
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented.view(out_d, rep, -1).mean(dim=1))
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b.view(out_d, rep).mean(dim=1))
                        elif src_out < out_d and out_d % src_out == 0:
                            rep = out_d // src_out
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented.repeat(rep, 1))
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b.repeat(rep))
                        else:
                            target_w.data[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)].copy_(
                                w_oriented[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)]
                            )
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data[: min(len(target.bias), len(b))].copy_(
                                    b[: min(len(target.bias), len(b))]
                                )
                    else:
                        target_w.data[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)].copy_(
                            w_oriented[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)]
                        )
                        if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                            b_val = b.data if isinstance(b, nn.Parameter) else b
                            target.bias.data[: min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                                b_val[: min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))]
                            )
                elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    min_d = min(target.weight.shape[0], target.weight.shape[1])
                    target.weight.data.zero_()
                    target.weight.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    if target.bias is not None:
                        target.bias.data.zero_()

                target.freeze_base_weights()

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    def _local_window_attention(self, Q, K, V, valid_mask, is_causal):
        B, H, L, d = Q.shape
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
        scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        idx = torch.arange(L, device=Q.device)
        dist = (idx.unsqueeze(0) - idx.unsqueeze(1)).abs()
        mask = dist > self.window_size
        if is_causal:
            # Query i attending to key j: future keys (j > i) must be masked
            mask = mask | (idx.unsqueeze(0) > idx.unsqueeze(1))
        scores = scores.masked_fill(mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -10000.0)

        # Padding mask
        pad_k = (~valid_mask).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
        scores = scores.masked_fill(pad_k, -10000.0)
        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(Q.dtype)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)
        return torch.matmul(attn, V)

    def _sparse_block_attention(self, Q, K, V, valid_mask, is_causal):
        B, H, L, d = Q.shape
        pad_len = (self.block_size - L % self.block_size) % self.block_size
        if pad_len > 0:
            Q_pad = F.pad(Q, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))
            K_pad = F.pad(K, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))
            V_pad = F.pad(V, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))
            valid_pad = F.pad(valid_mask, (0, pad_len), value=False)
        else:
            Q_pad, K_pad, V_pad = Q, K, V
            valid_pad = valid_mask
        Lp = L + pad_len
        nb = Lp // self.block_size

        Qb = Q_pad.view(B, H, nb, self.block_size, d)
        Kb = K_pad.view(B, H, nb, self.block_size, d)
        Vb = V_pad.view(B, H, nb, self.block_size, d)
        valid_b = valid_pad.view(B, nb, self.block_size)

        Q_mean = Qb.mean(dim=3)
        K_mean = Kb.mean(dim=3)
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
        blk_scores = (
            torch.matmul(Q_mean.float(), K_mean.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
        )

        if is_causal:
            causal_blk_mask = torch.triu(
                torch.full((nb, nb), float("-inf"), device=Q.device, dtype=torch.float32),
                diagonal=1,
            )
            blk_scores = blk_scores + causal_blk_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        top_k = min(self.top_k, nb)
        _, top_idx = blk_scores.topk(top_k, dim=-1)
        if is_causal:
            for i in range(nb):
                top_idx[:, :, i, 0] = i
        else:
            top_idx[:, :, :, 0] = 0

        out_blocks = torch.zeros_like(Qb)
        for i in range(nb):
            idx = top_idx[:, :, i, :]
            idx_exp = idx.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, self.block_size, d)
            K_sel = torch.gather(Kb, 2, idx_exp).reshape(B, H, top_k * self.block_size, d)
            V_sel = torch.gather(Vb, 2, idx_exp).reshape(B, H, top_k * self.block_size, d)

            scores_i = (
                torch.matmul(Qb[:, :, i].float(), K_sel.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous())
                * scale
            )

            if is_causal:
                q_pos = (i * self.block_size + torch.arange(self.block_size, device=Q.device)).view(
                    1, 1, self.block_size, 1
                )
                k_pos = (
                    idx.unsqueeze(-1) * self.block_size
                    + torch.arange(self.block_size, device=Q.device).view(1, 1, 1, self.block_size)
                ).view(B, H, 1, top_k * self.block_size)
                scores_i = scores_i.masked_fill(k_pos > q_pos, -10000.0)

            valid_b_exp = valid_b.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, H, nb, self.block_size)
            idx_v = idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, self.block_size)
            valid_sel = torch.gather(valid_b_exp, 2, idx_v).reshape(B, H, top_k * self.block_size)
            pad_sel = ~valid_sel.unsqueeze(2)
            scores_i = scores_i.masked_fill(pad_sel, -10000.0)
            scores_i = torch.nan_to_num(scores_i, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

            attn_i = softpick(scores_i, dim=-1).to(Q.dtype)
            out_blocks[:, :, i] = torch.matmul(attn_i, V_sel)

        out = out_blocks.view(B, H, Lp, d)[:, :, :L, :]
        return out

    def _global_spectral_rff_attention(self, Q, K, V, valid_mask, is_causal):
        B, H, L, d = Q.shape
        pad = (~valid_mask).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)

        Qf = torch.matmul(Q.float(), self.rff_proj.float())
        Kf = torch.matmul(K.float(), self.rff_proj.float())
        norm = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.rff_features)
        phi_Q = torch.cat([torch.cos(Qf), torch.sin(Qf)], dim=-1) * norm
        phi_K = torch.cat([torch.cos(Kf), torch.sin(Kf)], dim=-1) * norm

        phi_K = phi_K.masked_fill(pad, 0.0)
        V_masked = V.float().masked_fill(pad, 0.0)

        if is_causal:
            # Causal linear recurrence via cumulative prefix sums
            KV = phi_K.unsqueeze(-1) * V_masked.unsqueeze(-2)  # [B, H, L, 2F, d]
            KV_cumsum = torch.cumsum(KV, dim=2)
            num = torch.matmul(phi_Q.unsqueeze(-2), KV_cumsum).squeeze(-2)  # [B, H, L, d]
            Z = torch.cumsum(phi_K, dim=2)
            den = (phi_Q * Z).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-5
            return (num / den).to(Q.dtype)
        else:
            KV = torch.matmul(phi_K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous(), V_masked)
            Z = phi_K.sum(dim=-2, keepdim=True)
            num = torch.matmul(phi_Q, KV)
            den = torch.matmul(phi_Q, Z.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) + 1e-5
            return (num / den).to(Q.dtype)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        L_kv = kv_input.shape[1]

        is_causal = bool(self.is_decoder and not is_cross and L > 1)

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        if eff_mask is None:
            valid_mask = torch.ones(B, L_kv, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=torch.bool)
        elif eff_mask.dim() == 2:
            valid_mask = eff_mask.bool() if eff_mask.dtype == torch.bool else eff_mask > 0
        elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
            valid_mask = (
                eff_mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -10000.0
                if eff_mask.min() < -1000
                else eff_mask[:, 0, 0, :] > 0
            )
        else:
            valid_mask = torch.ones(B, L_kv, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=torch.bool)

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        K = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, L_kv, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, L_kv, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        if self.num_kv_heads < self.num_heads:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        use_dense = bool(is_cross or (not self.is_decoder and not self.enable_cascade_on_encoders))

        if not hasattr(self, "_logged_once"):
            logger.debug(
                "NASCAttention: L=%d, L_kv=%d, is_causal=%s, head_dim=%d, all_head_size=%d, path=%s",
                L,
                L_kv,
                is_causal,
                self.head_dim,
                self.all_head_size,
                "dense" if use_dense else "cascade",
            )
            self._logged_once = True

        if use_dense:
            # Fall back to standard scaled dot product attention for short/cross sequences or encoders
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
            scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            if is_causal:
                causal_mask = (
                    torch.triu(
                        torch.full(
                            (L, L_kv),
                            float("-inf"),
                            device=hidden_states.device,
                            dtype=torch.float32,
                        ),
                        diagonal=1,
                    )
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                )
                scores = scores + causal_mask

            if eff_mask is not None:
                if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                    scores = scores + (eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2).float() - 1.0) * 10000.0
                elif eff_mask.dim() == 3:
                    scores = scores + eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
                elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                    scores = scores + eff_mask.float()

            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(Q.dtype)
            attn = self.dropout(attn)
            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
                attn = attn * head_mask
            O = torch.matmul(attn, V)
        else:
            if is_causal:
                gates = F.softmax(self.gate_proj(hidden_states), dim=-1)  # [B, L, 3]
                g0 = gates[:, :, 0].unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)  # [B, 1, L, 1]
                g1 = gates[:, :, 1].unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
                g2 = gates[:, :, 2].unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
            else:
                summary = hidden_states.mean(dim=1)
                gates = F.softmax(self.gate_proj(summary), dim=-1)  # [B, 3]
                g0 = gates[:, 0].view(B, 1, 1, 1)
                g1 = gates[:, 1].view(B, 1, 1, 1)
                g2 = gates[:, 2].view(B, 1, 1, 1)

            O_loc = self._local_window_attention(Q, K, V, valid_mask, is_causal)
            O_sp = self._sparse_block_attention(Q, K, V, valid_mask, is_causal)
            O_glb = self._global_spectral_rff_attention(Q, K, V, valid_mask, is_causal)

            O = g0 * O_loc + g1 * O_sp + g2 * O_glb

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
                else:
                    hm = head_mask
                O = O * hm

        context = O.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out, None

NASC: NEXUS-AEGIS Spectral Cascade Attention. Hardened for multi-architecture deployment across DistilBERT, RoBERTa, GPT-2, and Qwen3.5.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "nystrom", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "nystrom_attention",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
class NystromAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    # NOTE: No Triton kernel — uses pure PyTorch.

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        num_landmarks: int | None = None,
        pinv_iterations: int = 3,
        chunk_size: int = 256,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size")
        num_heads = kwargs.get("num_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
        num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads") or kwargs.get("num_kv_heads")

        if hidden_size is None:
            if config is not None:
                hidden_size = getattr(config, "hidden_size", getattr(config, "dim", 768))
            elif source_attn is not None:
                out_layer = getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "out_lin",
                    getattr(
                        source_attn,
                        "dense",
                        getattr(source_attn, "c_proj", getattr(source_attn, "o_proj", None)),
                    ),
                )
                hidden_size = (
                    getattr(out_layer, "out_features", 768) if out_layer is not None else 768
                )
            else:
                hidden_size = 768

        if num_heads is None:
            if config is not None:
                num_heads = getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 12))
            elif source_attn is not None:
                num_heads = getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    getattr(source_attn, "n_heads", 12),
                )
            else:
                num_heads = 12

        if num_kv_heads is None:
            if config is not None:
                num_kv_heads = getattr(
                    config,
                    "num_key_value_heads",
                    getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", num_heads),
                )
            elif source_attn is not None:
                num_kv_heads = getattr(source_attn, "num_key_value_heads", num_heads)
            else:
                num_kv_heads = num_heads

        self.dim = int(hidden_size)
        self.hidden_size = self.dim
        self.num_heads = int(num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = int(num_kv_heads)
        self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        landmarks_cfg = (
            (
                num_landmarks
                if num_landmarks is not None
                else getattr(
                    config,
                    "num_landmarks",
                    getattr(config, "nystrom_landmarks", rank if rank is not None else 32),
                )
            )
            if config is not None
            else (num_landmarks if num_landmarks is not None else 32)
        )
        self.num_landmarks = min(32, int(landmarks_cfg))
        self.pinv_iterations = pinv_iterations

        # Chunk size for causal Nyström
        self.chunk_size = int(
            kwargs.get(
                "chunk_size",
                getattr(comp_config, "nystrom_chunk_size", chunk_size),
            )
        )

        # Decoder detection
        model_type = getattr(config, "model_type", "").lower() if config else ""
        is_dec = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "is_decoder",
                getattr(config, "is_causal", kwargs.get("is_causal", False)),
            )
            if config
            else False
        )
        _mt = (model_type or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi",
            "opt", "bloom", "falcon", "olmo", "mpt", "stablelm",
        )
        if (
            any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            or hasattr(config, "architectures")
            and any(
                "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # LoRA config
        self.lora_rank = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if config
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # Projections
        self.query = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.all_head_size, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.key = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.value = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size, self.dim, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # --- Aliases ---
    @property
    def q_proj(self): return self.query
    @property
    def k_proj(self): return self.key
    @property
    def v_proj(self): return self.value
    @property
    def q_lin(self): return self.query
    @property
    def k_lin(self): return self.key
    @property
    def v_lin(self): return self.value
    @property
    def out_lin(self): return self.out_proj
    @property
    def dense(self): return self.out_proj
    @property
    def o_proj(self): return self.out_proj
    @property
    def c_proj(self): return self.out_proj

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__
        has_out = (
            hasattr(src, "dense") or hasattr(src, "out_proj") or hasattr(src, "out_lin")
            or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        )
        is_nested = False
        if cls_name in [
            "BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention", "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
            "ElectraSelfAttention", "DebertaSelfAttention",
        ] or (hasattr(src, "query") and not has_out):
            is_nested = True
        if hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src, "output"):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None), "k": (None, None), "v": (None, None), "o": (None, None),
        }

        # GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv1d = (
                    "Conv1D" in c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__
                    or "Conv1D" in base_c.__class__.__name__
                    or hasattr(c_attn_mod, "nf") or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv1d:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (c_w[:split_size, :], c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (c_w[split_size:2*split_size, :], c_b[split_size:2*split_size] if c_b is not None else None)
                extracted["v"] = (c_w[2*split_size:, :], c_b[2*split_size:] if c_b is not None else None)

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv1d = (
                        "Conv1D" in c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__
                        or "Conv1D" in base_o.__class__.__name__
                        or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf") or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv1d:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (o_w, bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None)

        # RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # Flat
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception:
                pass

        target_map = {"q": self.query, "k": self.key, "v": self.value, "o": self.out_proj}

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)
                if w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape:
                    w = w.t().contiguous()
                out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
                target_mod.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(device=target_mod.weight.device, dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype)
                )
                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    min_b = min(target_mod.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target_mod.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(
                        b[:min_b].to(device=target_mod.bias.device, dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype)
                    )
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                w_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.data.float()).item())
                if w_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"NystromAttention weight verification failed for '{proj_key}': "
                        f"norm too small ({w_norm:.2e})"
                    )
                if self.freeze_base:
                    target_mod.freeze_base_weights()

    # ==================================================================
    # Core helpers
    # ==================================================================
    def _iterative_pinv(self, A: torch.Tensor, iters: int = 3) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Stable iterative pseudoinverse (Schultz method from Nyströmformer paper)."""
        norm_1 = A.abs().sum(dim=-2).amax(dim=-1, keepdim=True).unsqueeze(-1)
        norm_inf = A.abs().sum(dim=-1).amax(dim=-1, keepdim=True).unsqueeze(-1)
        alpha = 1.0 / (norm_1 * norm_inf + 1e-5)
        V = alpha * A.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
        I = torch.eye(A.size(-1), device=A.device, dtype=A.dtype).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
        for _ in range(iters):
            V = torch.matmul(V, 2.0 * I - torch.matmul(A, V))
        return V

    def _dense_attention(
        self,
        Q: torch.Tensor,
        K: torch.Tensor,
        V: torch.Tensor,
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor,
        dtype: torch.dtype,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Dense (non-causal) attention for encoder fallback."""
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        try:
            if valid_mask.all():
                return F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    Q, K, V,
                    is_causal=False,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                ).to(dtype)
            else:
                attn_mask = valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                return F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    Q, K, V,
                    attn_mask=attn_mask,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                ).to(dtype)
        except Exception:
            pass

        scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)
        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
        attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)
        return torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)

    def _pool_landmarks(self, X: torch.Tensor, m: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Adaptive average pooling to extract `m` landmarks from X.

        Args:
            X: (B, H, N, d)
            m: number of landmarks
        Returns:
            (B, H, m, d)
        """
        B, H, N, d = X.shape
        if m >= N:
            return X
        X_flat = X.reshape(B * H, N, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()  # (B*H, d, N)
        pooled = F.adaptive_avg_pool1d(X_flat, m)                      # (B*H, d, m)
        return pooled.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().reshape(B, H, m, d)

    # ==================================================================
    # Chunked Causal Nyström (NEW — for decoders)
    # ==================================================================
    def _chunked_causal_nystrom(
        self,
        Q: torch.Tensor,  # (B, H, N, d)
        K: torch.Tensor,  # (B, H, N, d)
        V: torch.Tensor,  # (B, H, N, d)
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor,  # (B, N)
        chunk_size: int = 256,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Chunked Causal Nyström attention.

        For each chunk c:
          - Compute landmarks from chunks 0..c (cumulative)
          - Within-chunk: dense causal attention
          - Across-chunks: Nyström with cumulative landmarks

        Complexity: O(N * C) where C = chunk_size (not O(N²))
        """
        B, H, N, d = Q.shape
        device = Q.device
        dtype = Q.dtype
        m = min(self.num_landmarks, N)

        # Split into chunks
        num_chunks = (N + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size
        outputs = []

        for c_idx in range(num_chunks):
            start = c_idx * chunk_size
            end = min(start + chunk_size, N)
            C_len = end - start

            Q_c = Q[:, :, start:end, :]   # (B, H, C_len, d)
            K_c = K[:, :, start:end, :]
            V_c = V[:, :, start:end, :]
            mask_c = valid_mask[:, start:end]

            # 1. Within-chunk dense causal attention
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
            scores_intra = torch.matmul(Q_c.float(), K_c.float().transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
            causal = torch.tril(torch.ones(C_len, C_len, device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
            scores_intra = scores_intra.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)
            scores_intra = scores_intra.masked_fill(
                ~mask_c.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4
            )
            attn_intra = F.softmax(scores_intra, dim=-1).to(dtype)
            attn_intra = torch.nan_to_num(attn_intra, nan=0.0)
            out_intra = torch.matmul(attn_intra, V_c)  # (B, H, C_len, d)

            # 2. Across-chunks Nyström (if c_idx > 0)
            if c_idx == 0:
                outputs.append(out_intra)
                continue

            # Landmarks from all previous chunks (0..c_idx-1)
            K_prev = K[:, :, :start, :]  # (B, H, start, d)
            V_prev = V[:, :, :start, :]
            mask_prev = valid_mask[:, :start]

            # Compute landmarks via adaptive pooling
            m_prev = min(m, start)
            K_flat = K_prev.reshape(B * H, start, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
            K_landmarks = (
                F.adaptive_avg_pool1d(K_flat, m_prev)
                .transpose(1, 2)
                .contiguous()
                .reshape(B, H, m_prev, d)
            )

            # Nyström approximation for cross-chunk
            # F1 = softmax(Q_c @ K_landmarks^T)  → (B, H, C_len, m_prev)
            scores1 = torch.matmul(Q_c.float(), K_landmarks.float().transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
            F1 = F.softmax(scores1, dim=-1).to(dtype)

            # F2 = softmax(Q_landmarks @ K_landmarks^T)  → (B, H, m_prev, m_prev)
            Q_flat = Q_c.reshape(B * H, C_len, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
            Q_landmarks = (
                F.adaptive_avg_pool1d(Q_flat, m_prev)
                .transpose(1, 2)
                .contiguous()
                .reshape(B, H, m_prev, d)
            )
            scores2 = torch.matmul(
                Q_landmarks.float(), K_landmarks.float().transpose(-2, -1)
            ) * scale
            F2 = F.softmax(scores2, dim=-1).to(dtype)

            # F3 = softmax(Q_landmarks @ K_prev^T)  → (B, H, m_prev, start)
            scores3 = torch.matmul(Q_landmarks.float(), K_prev.float().transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
            scores3 = scores3.masked_fill(~mask_prev.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)
            F3 = F.softmax(scores3, dim=-1).to(dtype)

            # Pseudoinverse of F2
            F2_pinv = self._iterative_pinv(F2, iters=self.pinv_iterations)

            # Cross-chunk output: F1 @ F2_pinv @ F3 @ V_prev
            step1 = torch.matmul(F3, V_prev)      # (B, H, m_prev, d)
            step2 = torch.matmul(F2_pinv, step1)  # (B, H, m_prev, d)
            out_cross = torch.matmul(F1, step2)   # (B, H, C_len, d)
            out_cross = torch.nan_to_num(out_cross, nan=0.0)

            # 3. Combine intra and cross
            out_c = out_intra + out_cross
            outputs.append(out_c)

        # Concatenate all chunks
        context = torch.cat(outputs, dim=2)  # (B, H, N, d)
        return context

    # ==================================================================
    # Forward
    # ==================================================================
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if kwargs.get("encoder_hidden_states") is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("Cross-attention is not implemented for NystromAttention")

        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        Q = self.query(hidden_states).view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.key(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.value(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        valid_mask = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, device)

        # ============================================================
        # Causal (decoder) → Chunked Causal Nyström
        # ============================================================
        if self.is_decoder or self.is_causal:
            # Use chunked causal Nyström
            context = self._chunked_causal_nystrom(
                Q, K, V, valid_mask, chunk_size=256
            )
            context = self.dropout(context)

        # ============================================================
        # Encoder path
        # ============================================================
        elif max(64, 2 * self.num_landmarks) >= N:
            # Very short sequence — dense attention
            context = self._dense_attention(Q, K, V, valid_mask, dtype)
        else:
            # Standard Nyström for encoder
            m = min(self.num_landmarks, N)

            Q_landmarks = self._pool_landmarks(Q, m)
            K_landmarks = self._pool_landmarks(K, m)

            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)

            # F1: (B, H, N, m)
            scores1 = torch.matmul(
                Q.float(), K_landmarks.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
            ) * scale
            F1 = F.softmax(scores1, dim=-1).to(dtype)

            # F2: (B, H, m, m)
            scores2 = torch.matmul(
                Q_landmarks.float(), K_landmarks.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
            ) * scale
            F2 = F.softmax(scores2, dim=-1).to(dtype)

            # F3: (B, H, m, N)
            scores3 = torch.matmul(
                Q_landmarks.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
            ) * scale
            scores3 = scores3.masked_fill(
                ~valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4
            )
            F3 = F.softmax(scores3, dim=-1).to(dtype)

            F2_pinv = self._iterative_pinv(F2, iters=self.pinv_iterations)

            # (B, H, m, d) ← F3 @ V
            step1 = torch.matmul(F3, V)
            # (B, H, m, d) ← F2_pinv @ step1
            step2 = torch.matmul(F2_pinv, step1)
            # (B, H, N, d) ← F1 @ step2
            context = torch.matmul(F1, step2)
            context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0)
            context = self.dropout(context)

        # Head mask
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                hm = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), -1, 1, 1)
            else:
                hm = head_mask
            context = context * hm.to(context.dtype)

        # Zero-out padded positions
        context = context * valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype)

        # Merge heads
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out, None

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.
