@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "performer",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "performer_attention",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
class PerformerAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    triton_kernel = "linear_attn"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        eps: float = 1e-6,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        is_nested_encoder: bool | None = None,  # FIX 4: explicit override
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        # ---------------- Hidden size & heads ----------------
        hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size", kwargs.get("dim", kwargs.get("d_model")))
        num_heads = kwargs.get(
            "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_heads", kwargs.get("n_heads"))
        )
        num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", kwargs.get("num_kv_heads"))

        if hidden_size is None or num_heads is None:
            if config is not None:
                if hidden_size is None:
                    hidden_size = _cfg_get(
                        config,
                        "hidden_size",
                        _cfg_get(config, "dim", _cfg_get(config, "d_model", 768)),
                    )
                if num_heads is None:
                    num_heads = _cfg_get(
                        config,
                        "num_attention_heads",
                        _cfg_get(config, "n_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_heads", 12)),
                    )
                if num_kv_heads is None:
                    num_kv_heads = _cfg_get(
                        config,
                        "num_key_value_heads",
                        _cfg_get(config, "num_kv_heads", num_heads),
                    )
            elif source_attn is not None:
                if hidden_size is None:
                    out_layer = getattr(
                        source_attn,
                        "out_lin",
                        getattr(
                            source_attn,
                            "dense",
                            getattr(
                                source_attn,
                                "c_proj",
                                getattr(source_attn, "o_proj", None),
                            ),
                        ),
                    )
                    hidden_size = getattr(
                        out_layer,
                        "out_features",
                        getattr(
                            source_attn,
                            "hidden_size",
                            getattr(source_attn, "embed_dim", 768),
                        ),
                    )
                if num_heads is None:
                    num_heads = getattr(
                        source_attn,
                        "num_attention_heads",
                        getattr(
                            source_attn,
                            "n_heads",
                            getattr(source_attn, "num_heads", 12),
                        ),
                    )
                if num_kv_heads is None:
                    num_kv_heads = getattr(
                        source_attn,
                        "num_key_value_heads",
                        getattr(source_attn, "num_kv_heads", num_heads),
                    )
            else:
                hidden_size = hidden_size if hidden_size is not None else 768
                num_heads = num_heads if num_heads is not None else 12
                num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads if num_kv_heads is not None else num_heads

        if num_kv_heads is None:
            num_kv_heads = num_heads

        # FIX 8: validate divisibility
        if num_heads <= 0 or num_kv_heads <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid head counts: num_heads={num_heads}, num_kv_heads={num_kv_heads}"
            )
        if num_heads % num_kv_heads != 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"num_heads ({num_heads}) must be divisible by num_kv_heads ({num_kv_heads})"
            )

        self.dim = int(hidden_size)
        self.hidden_size = self.dim
        self.num_heads = int(num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = int(num_kv_heads)
        self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True
        self.eps = eps

        # ---------------- num_features (FIX 7) ----------------
        num_features_default = rank if rank is not None else 64
        self.num_features = _resolve_config_value(
            config,
            comp_config,
            kwargs,
            names=("performer_rank", "rank", "num_features"),
            default=num_features_default,
        )
        # FIX 14: ensure at least 1
        self.num_features = max(1, int(self.num_features))

        # ---------------- kernel_type ----------------
        self.kernel_type = _resolve_config_value(
            config,
            comp_config,
            kwargs,
            names=("performer_kernel_type", "kernel_type"),
            default="favor_plus",
        )

        # ---------------- Decoder detection ----------------
        model_type = getattr(config, "model_type", "").lower() if config else ""
        is_dec = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "is_decoder",
                getattr(config, "is_causal", kwargs.get("is_causal", False)),
            )
            if config
            else False
        )
        _mt = (model_type or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if (
            any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            or hasattr(config, "architectures")
            and any(
                "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)

        # ---------------- LoRA rank & alpha (FIX 7) ----------------
        self.lora_rank = int(
            _resolve_config_value(
                config,
                comp_config,
                kwargs,
                names=("lora_rank",),
                default=16,
            )
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            _resolve_config_value(
                config,
                comp_config,
                kwargs,
                names=("lora_alpha",),
                default=32.0,
            )
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # ---------------- Nested encoder detection (FIX 4) ----------------
        # If explicit value passed, use it. Otherwise infer from source_attn.
        if is_nested_encoder is not None:
            self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested_encoder)
        else:
            self.is_nested_encoder = False  # will be updated by copy_weights

        # ---------------- Projections (FIX 2) ----------------
        # out_proj gets LoRA only if NOT nested encoder (since nested encoder
        # skips out_proj in forward pass).
        # We determine nested-ness AFTER copy_weights, but we need to build
        # modules before. So: build with lora on out_proj tentatively; if
        # copy_weights reveals nested, we zero-out / disable it.
        self.query = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.key = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.value = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        # out_proj LoRA is created conditionally. We start with lora_rank,
        # and if copy_weights detects nested, we disable it.
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # Aliases (no longer registered as parameters, only attribute access)
        self.q_proj = self.query
        self.k_proj = self.key
        self.v_proj = self.value

        # ---------------- Omega matrix ----------------
        omega = self._build_orthogonal_matrix(self.head_dim, self.num_features)
        self.register_buffer("omega", omega, persistent=True)

        # ---------------- Dropout (FIX 10) ----------------
        if config is not None:
            dropout_p = getattr(
                config,
                "attention_probs_dropout_prob",
                getattr(config, "dropout", 0.1),
            )
        else:
            dropout_p = 0.1
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)
        self.rotary_emb = getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None)
        self.triton_kwargs = {
            "phi": "identity",
            "eps": self.eps,
            "cos_weight": None,
            "sin_weight": None,
        }

        # ---------------- Copy weights from source ----------------
        if source_attn is not None:
            success = self.copy_weights(source_attn)
            if not success:
                logger.warning(
                    "PerformerAttention: copy_weights failed. Proceeding with initialized weights."
                )

        # FIX 2: after is_nested_encoder is known, disable LoRA on out_proj
        # if nested encoder (since out_proj is not used in forward).
        if self.is_nested_encoder and self.lora_rank > 0:
            # Move out_proj LoRA params to a "dummy" state so they don't
            # participate in optimization. We freeze them and zero-init.
            if self.out_proj.lora_A is not None:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    self.out_proj.lora_A.zero_()
                self.out_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = False
            if self.out_proj.lora_B is not None:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    self.out_proj.lora_B.zero_()
                self.out_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = False
            logger.info(
                "PerformerAttention: is_nested_encoder=True, disabling LoRA "
                "on out_proj (out_proj is bypassed in forward pass)."
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def _build_orthogonal_matrix(self, d: int, m: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        blocks = []
        remaining = m
        while remaining > 0:
            block_size = min(d, remaining)
            g = torch.randn(d, block_size)
            q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(g)
            blocks.append(q)
            remaining -= block_size
        W = torch.cat(blocks, dim=1)
        W = W * math.sqrt(d)
        return W.t().contiguous()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def _feature_map(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """FAVOR+ feature map.

        FIX 1: removed per-token max_proj subtraction (bias).
        FIX 6: removed redundant +eps.
        FIX 11: removed .contiguous() (matmul handles non-contiguous).
        FIX 12: removed dead clamp(max=10.0).
        """
        # x: [B, H, N, d]
        # omega: [M, d]
        omega_t = self.omega.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device).t()  # [d, M]
        proj = torch.matmul(x, omega_t)  # [B, H, N, M]

        if self.kernel_type in ("favor_plus", "exp", "favor"):
            diag_sq = torch.sum(x**2, dim=-1, keepdim=True) * 0.5  # [B,H,N,1]
            # FIXED: Added max clamp to prevent FP16 overflow (which causes NaN collapse)
            diff = torch.clamp(proj - diag_sq, min=-30.0, max=11.0)
            phi = torch.exp(diff)  # FIX 6: no +eps
            phi = phi / math.sqrt(self.num_features)
        elif self.kernel_type == "elu":
            phi = (F.elu(proj) + 1.0) / math.sqrt(self.num_features)
        else:
            phi = (F.relu(proj) + self.eps) / math.sqrt(self.num_features)
        return phi

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> bool:
        """Copy weights from source attention module.

        FIX 13: returns bool (was None).
        FIX 4: sets is_nested_encoder robustly.
        """
        if src is None:
            return False

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")

        # FIX 4: robust nested detection
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True

        # Only override if we didn't get explicit is_nested_encoder in __init__
        # We know we didn't if current value is False (default) and no source
        # was given... but actually we should always trust the source.
        # Let's just set it (unless caller explicitly passed one, in which
        # case we don't override — but we can't detect that here, so we
        # assume the source is authoritative).
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # ---- 1. GPT-2 fused Conv1D ----
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # ---- 2. RoBERTa/BERT nested ----
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # ---- 3. Flat modules ----
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # ---- 4. Fallback via UniversalAdapter ----
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (
                            w.clone(),
                            b.clone() if b is not None else None,
                        )
            except Exception:
                pass

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                "PerformerAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections %s from %s.",
                missing_after,
                src.__class__.__name__,
            )
            return False

        # ---- 5. Copy into target modules ----
        target_map = {
            "q": self.query,
            "k": self.key,
            "v": self.value,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

                if (
                    w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                    and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape
                ):
                    w = w.t().contiguous()

                out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))

                target_mod.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(
                        device=target_mod.weight.device,
                        dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype,
                    )
                )

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    min_b = min(target_mod.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target_mod.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(
                        b[:min_b].to(
                            device=target_mod.bias.device,
                            dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype,
                        )
                    )
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                w_norm = float(torch.norm(target_mod.weight.data).item())
                if w_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "PerformerAttention weight verification notice for %s: "
                        "norm too small (%.2e)",
                        proj_key,
                        w_norm,
                    )

        # ---- 6. Ensure base weights frozen, LoRA A/B trainable ----
        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.query.freeze_base_weights()
            self.key.freeze_base_weights()
            self.value.freeze_base_weights()
            if not self.is_nested_encoder:
                self.out_proj.freeze_base_weights()
            # If nested encoder, out_proj LoRA was already disabled

        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        # FIX 5: explicit error instead of silent None
        if output_attentions:
            raise NotImplementedError(
                "PerformerAttention does not materialize attention matrices. "
                "FAVOR+ computes attention implicitly and does not store the "
                "O(N^2) attention scores."
            )

        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            # Cross-attention not supported
            return hidden_states, None

        while isinstance(hidden_states, (tuple, list)):
            hidden_states = hidden_states[0]

        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        scale = d**-0.25
        Q = self.query(hidden_states).view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous() * scale
        K = (
            self.key(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
            * scale
        )
        V = self.value(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # ---- RoPE ----
        if (
            position_embeddings is not None
            and isinstance(position_embeddings, (tuple, list))
            and len(position_embeddings) >= 2
        ):
            cos, sin = position_embeddings[0], position_embeddings[1]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
            if pos_ids is None:
                pos_ids = torch.arange(N, device=device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
            try:
                cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(V, pos_ids)
                Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, pos_ids)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("RoPE application failed: %s", e)

        # ---- GQA expansion ----
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        # ---- Valid token mask ----
        valid_mask = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, device)
        if valid_mask is not None:
            mask_expanded = valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype)
        else:
            mask_expanded = torch.ones(B, 1, N, 1, device=device, dtype=dtype)

        # ---- Feature maps ----
        # FIX 3: mask Q_feat in BOTH branches
        Q_feat = self._feature_map(Q) * mask_expanded
        K_feat = self._feature_map(K) * mask_expanded

        # ---- Attention via shared Triton dispatcher ----
        context = triton_dispatch(
            self.triton_kernel,
            Q_feat,
            K_feat,
            V,
            is_causal=self.is_decoder,
            **self.triton_kwargs,
        )

        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0)
        context = self.dropout(context)

        # ---- Head mask (FIX 9) ----
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                hm = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            else:
                hm = head_mask
            context = context * hm

        context = context * mask_expanded
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)

        # FIX 2: for nested encoder, skip out_proj (LoRA disabled)
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(context)

        # ---- Shape sanity ----
        if out.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
            if out.shape[-1] < hidden_states.shape[-1]:
                out = F.pad(out, (0, hidden_states.shape[-1] - out.shape[-1]))
            else:
                out = out[..., : hidden_states.shape[-1]]

        return out.to(dtype), None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def _forward_causal(
        self,
        Q_feat: torch.Tensor,
        K_feat: torch.Tensor,
        V: torch.Tensor,
        B: int,
        H: int,
        d: int,
        N: int,
        device: torch.device,
        dtype: torch.dtype,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Exact causal FAVOR+ prefix scan."""
        M = Q_feat.shape[-1]
        chunk_size = max(16, min(64, N // 4))
        num_chunks = (N + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size

        S = torch.zeros(B, H, M, d, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
        z = torch.zeros(B, H, M, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
        num_list = []
        den_list = []

        for c in range(num_chunks):
            start = c * chunk_size
            end = min(start + chunk_size, N)
            L = end - start

            q_c = Q_feat[:, :, start:end].float()
            k_c = K_feat[:, :, start:end].float()
            v_c = V[:, :, start:end].float()

            causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, L, device=device, dtype=torch.float32))
            intra_attn = torch.matmul(q_c, k_c.transpose(-1, -2)) * causal_mask
            intra_num = torch.matmul(intra_attn, v_c)
            inter_num = torch.matmul(q_c, S)
            num_list.append(intra_num + inter_num)

            intra_den = intra_attn.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
            inter_den = torch.matmul(q_c, z.unsqueeze(-1))
            den_list.append(intra_den + inter_den)

            S = S + torch.matmul(k_c.transpose(-1, -2), v_c)
            z = z + k_c.sum(dim=2)

        num = torch.cat(num_list, dim=2)
        den = torch.cat(den_list, dim=2).clamp(min=self.eps)
        return (num / den).to(dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def _forward_bidirectional(
        self,
        Q_feat: torch.Tensor,
        K_feat: torch.Tensor,
        V: torch.Tensor,
        B: int,
        device: torch.device,
        dtype: torch.dtype,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Standard linear attention (non-causal)."""
        # FIX 9: only upcast to fp32 when needed (fp16 case)
        compute_dtype = torch.float32 if dtype == torch.float16 else dtype
        Kf = K_feat.to(compute_dtype)
        Vf = V.to(compute_dtype)
        Qf = Q_feat.to(compute_dtype)

        KV = torch.matmul(Kf.transpose(-2, -1), Vf)
        K_sum = Kf.sum(dim=-2, keepdim=True)
        num = torch.matmul(Qf, KV)
        den = torch.matmul(Qf, K_sum.transpose(-2, -1)) + self.eps
        return (num / den).to(dtype)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> bool:
    """Copy weights from source attention module.

    FIX 13: returns bool (was None).
    FIX 4: sets is_nested_encoder robustly.
    """
    if src is None:
        return False

    cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
    has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")

    # FIX 4: robust nested detection
    is_nested = False
    if (
        (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            and hasattr(src, "output")
            and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
        )
        or (
            cls_name
            in [
                "BertSelfAttention",
                "RobertaSelfAttention",
                "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                "ElectraSelfAttention",
                "DebertaSelfAttention",
            ]
            or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
        )
        and not has_out_lin
    ):
        is_nested = True

    # Only override if we didn't get explicit is_nested_encoder in __init__
    # We know we didn't if current value is False (default) and no source
    # was given... but actually we should always trust the source.
    # Let's just set it (unless caller explicitly passed one, in which
    # case we don't override — but we can't detect that here, so we
    # assume the source is authoritative).
    self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

    extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
        "q": (None, None),
        "k": (None, None),
        "v": (None, None),
        "o": (None, None),
    }

    # ---- 1. GPT-2 fused Conv1D ----
    if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
        c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
        base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
        c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
        if c_w is not None:
            c_w = c_w.data.clone()
            if (
                (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
            ):
                c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
            split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
            bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
            c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
            extracted["q"] = (
                c_w[:split_size, :],
                c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
            )
            extracted["k"] = (
                c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
            )
            extracted["v"] = (
                c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
            )

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if o_w is not None:
                o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (
                    o_w,
                    bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                )

    # ---- 2. RoBERTa/BERT nested ----
    elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
        s = src.self
        for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
            mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
            if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                extracted[proj_key] = (
                    mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                )
        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
            out_mod = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (
                out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
            )

    # ---- 3. Flat modules ----
    else:
        proj_candidates = {
            "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
            "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
            "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
            "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
        }
        for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
            for name in names:
                if hasattr(src, name):
                    mod = getattr(src, name)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        extracted[proj_key] = (
                            mod.weight.data.clone(),
                            mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                        )
                        break

    # ---- 4. Fallback via UniversalAdapter ----
    missing = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing:
        try:
            from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

            adapter = UniversalAdapter()
            for k in missing:
                w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[k] = (
                        w.clone(),
                        b.clone() if b is not None else None,
                    )
        except Exception:
            pass

    missing_after = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing_after:
        logger.warning(
            "PerformerAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections %s from %s.",
            missing_after,
            src.__class__.__name__,
        )
        return False

    # ---- 5. Copy into target modules ----
    target_map = {
        "q": self.query,
        "k": self.key,
        "v": self.value,
        "o": self.out_proj,
    }

    with torch.no_grad():
        for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is None:
                if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                    if target_mod.bias is not None:
                        nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                continue

            if w.ndim != 2:
                w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

            if (
                w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape
            ):
                w = w.t().contiguous()

            out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
            in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))

            target_mod.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                w[:out_d, :in_d].to(
                    device=target_mod.weight.device,
                    dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype,
                )
            )

            if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                min_b = min(target_mod.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                target_mod.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(
                    b[:min_b].to(
                        device=target_mod.bias.device,
                        dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype,
                    )
                )
            elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

            w_norm = float(torch.norm(target_mod.weight.data).item())
            if w_norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    "PerformerAttention weight verification notice for %s: "
                    "norm too small (%.2e)",
                    proj_key,
                    w_norm,
                )

    # ---- 6. Ensure base weights frozen, LoRA A/B trainable ----
    if self.lora_rank > 0:
        self.query.freeze_base_weights()
        self.key.freeze_base_weights()
        self.value.freeze_base_weights()
        if not self.is_nested_encoder:
            self.out_proj.freeze_base_weights()
        # If nested encoder, out_proj LoRA was already disabled

    return True

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("primal_attention")
class PrimalAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int = 64,
        pinv_iters: int = 2,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        if rank is None:
            rank = 64
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None)

        self.dim = getattr(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        self.num_heads = getattr(
            config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = getattr(
            config,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
        )
        self.head_dim = getattr(
            config,
            "head_dim",
            kwargs.get("head_dim", self.dim // self.num_heads),
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        # PR-FIX-03: guard self.rank against None
        _rank_candidate = None
        if comp_config is not None:
            _rank_candidate = getattr(comp_config, "rank", None)
        if _rank_candidate is None:
            _rank_candidate = kwargs.pop("rank", None)
        if _rank_candidate is None:
            _rank_candidate = rank
        self.rank = int(_rank_candidate) if _rank_candidate else 64

        self.pinv_iters = pinv_iters

        is_dec = getattr(config, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if config
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        # PR-FIX-07: chunked head processing threshold.
        # If rank is large, split heads into chunks of CHUNK_HEADS to bound memory.
        self.chunk_heads = int(kwargs.get("chunk_heads", 0))  # 0 = disabled

        # Projections
        self.query = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.key = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.value = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        self.q_proj = self.query
        self.k_proj = self.key
        self.v_proj = self.value

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa / BERT nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules (DistilBERT, GPT-2 style without c_attn)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]

        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"PrimalAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections "
                f"{missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.query,
            "k": self.key,
            "v": self.value,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]

                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue
                    else:
                        logger.warning(
                            "PrimalAttention.copy_weights: no weight for %s, "
                            "keeping initialized weights.",
                            proj_key,
                        )
                        continue

                # Shape alignment
                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "PrimalAttention.copy_weights: %s has near-zero Frobenius norm (%.2e).",
                        proj_key,
                        fnorm,
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.query, self.key, self.value, self.out_proj]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        Q = (
            self.query(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )
        K = (
            self.key(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )
        V = (
            self.value(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )

        # RoPE
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        # GQA expansion
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            num_reps = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            K = repeat_kv(K, num_reps)
            V = repeat_kv(V, num_reps)

        # Landmark computation
        num_landmarks = min(self.rank, seq_len)
        if seq_len % num_landmarks == 0:
            seg = seq_len // num_landmarks
            Q_land = Q.reshape(batch_size, self.num_heads, num_landmarks, seg, self.head_dim).mean(
                dim=3
            )
            K_land = K.reshape(batch_size, self.num_heads, num_landmarks, seg, self.head_dim).mean(
                dim=3
            )
        else:
            idx = torch.linspace(
                0,
                seq_len - 1,
                num_landmarks,
                dtype=torch.long,
                device=hidden_states.device,
            )
            Q_land = Q[:, :, idx, :]
            K_land = K[:, :, idx, :]

        scale = 1.0 / (self.head_dim**0.5)

        # PR-FIX-01 + PR-FIX-07: chunked head processing to bound peak memory.
        # The chunk_heads attribute (default 0) disables chunking.
        if self.chunk_heads > 0 and self.num_heads > self.chunk_heads:
            head_chunks = [
                (h0, min(h0 + self.chunk_heads, self.num_heads))
                for h0 in range(0, self.num_heads, self.chunk_heads)
            ]
        else:
            head_chunks = [(0, self.num_heads)]

        context_heads = []
        attn_for_return: torch.Tensor | None = None

        for h0, h1 in head_chunks:
            Qc = Q[:, h0:h1]
            Kc = K[:, h0:h1]
            Vc = V[:, h0:h1]
            Ql = Q_land[:, h0:h1]
            Kl = K_land[:, h0:h1]

            with torch.amp.autocast("cuda", enabled=False):
                F1 = torch.matmul(Qc.float(), Kl.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                F2 = torch.matmul(Ql.float(), Kl.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                F3 = torch.matmul(Ql.float(), Kc.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

                # PR-FIX-01: pseudoinverse + Schulz without gradient tracking.
                # This eliminates the ~20x backward memory of pinv and stabilizes
                # the iteration numerically (rcond clamps near-singular values).
                with torch.no_grad():
                    F2_f32 = F2.detach().to(torch.float32)
                    F2_pinv = torch.linalg.pinv(F2_f32, rcond=1e-4)
                    I = (
                        torch.eye(
                            num_landmarks,
                            device=hidden_states.device,
                            dtype=torch.float32,
                        )
                        .unsqueeze(0)
                        .unsqueeze(0)
                    )
                    X = F2_pinv
                    for _ in range(min(self.pinv_iters, 4)):
                        X = torch.matmul(X, 2.0 * I - torch.matmul(F2_f32, X))
                    X = X.detach()

                    # PR-FIX-02: free pinv intermediates.
                    del F2_pinv, I

                attn_c = torch.matmul(F1, torch.matmul(X, F3))

                del F1, F2, F3, F2_f32, X

            # Causal mask
            is_causal = self.is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)
            if is_causal:
                causal_mask = torch.triu(
                    torch.full((seq_len, seq_len), -1e4, device=attn_c.device),
                    diagonal=1,
                )
                attn_c = attn_c + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

            # Padding mask
            if attention_mask is not None:
                attn_c = apply_attention_mask(attn_c, attention_mask)

            probs_c = F.softmax(attn_c, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
            probs_c = torch.nan_to_num(probs_c, nan=0.0)
            probs_c = self.dropout(probs_c)

            # head_mask (per-head, so select chunk)
            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
                    hm = head_mask[h0:h1].view(1, h1 - h0, 1, 1).to(probs_c.dtype)
                    probs_c = probs_c * hm
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
                    hm = (
                        head_mask[:, h0:h1].view(head_mask.size(0), h1 - h0, 1, 1).to(probs_c.dtype)
                    )
                    probs_c = probs_c * hm
                elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                    hm = head_mask[:, h0:h1].to(probs_c.dtype)
                    probs_c = probs_c * hm

            ctx_c = torch.matmul(probs_c, Vc)
            context_heads.append(ctx_c)

            if output_attentions and attn_for_return is None:
                # PR-FIX-05: return softmax probs, not logits.
                attn_for_return = probs_c

        # Concatenate chunks along head axis: (B, H_total, N, D)
        context = torch.cat(context_heads, dim=1)
        context = (
            context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)
        )

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            return context, (attn_for_return if output_attentions else None)

        out = self.out_proj(context)
        return out, (attn_for_return if output_attentions else None)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("rala_attention")
class RALALinearAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank or 64)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None)

        self.hidden_size = getattr(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        if (
            (config is None or not hasattr(config, "hidden_size"))
            and "hidden_size" not in kwargs
            and pretrained_attn is not None
        ):
            for cand in ["q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "c_attn"]:
                mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, cand, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    w = mod.weight
                    self.hidden_size = w.shape[1] if w.ndim == 2 else self.hidden_size
                    break

        self.dim = self.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = getattr(
            config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = getattr(
            config,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
        )
        self.head_dim = getattr(
            config,
            "head_dim",
            kwargs.get("head_dim", self.dim // self.num_heads),
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.rank_r = getattr(
            comp_config,
            "rala_rank",
            getattr(config, "rala_rank", kwargs.get("rala_rank", 8)),
        )

        # RALA-FIX-01: rename trainable params with `lora_` prefix
        self.lora_U = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(self.num_heads, self.head_dim, self.rank_r) * 0.01)
        self.lora_V = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(self.num_heads, self.head_dim, self.rank_r) * 0.01)
        self.lora_rank_alpha = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0))

        # Decoder detection
        _mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi", "opt")
        self.is_decoder = (
            bool(getattr(config, "is_decoder", False))
            or bool(getattr(config, "causal", False))
            or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            or (pretrained_attn is not None and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn"))
            or bool(kwargs.get("is_causal", False))
        )
        if "is_decoder" in kwargs and kwargs["is_decoder"] is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(kwargs["is_decoder"])
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if config
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and source_attn is not None:
            p = next(source_attn.parameters(), None)
            if p is not None:
                target_dtype = p.dtype

        # Projections
        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )

        self.query = self.q_proj
        self.key = self.k_proj
        self.value = self.v_proj

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)
            try:
                src_device = next(source_attn.parameters()).device
                self.to(src_device)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if target_dtype is not None:
            self.to(target_dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight transfer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                "RALALinearAttention.copy_weights: missing %s from %s",
                missing_after,
                src.__class__.__name__,
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue
                    continue

                src_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item())
                if src_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "RALALinearAttention: source weight for %s has near-zero norm %.6f",
                        proj_key,
                        src_norm,
                    )

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                    and w.shape[1] == target_mod.weight.shape[1]
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                fnorm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item())
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "RALALinearAttention: %s has norm %.6f",
                        proj_key,
                        fnorm,
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
                mod.freeze_base_weights()
            # RALA-FIX-01: U, V, alpha trainable via lora_ prefix
            self.lora_U.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_V.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_rank_alpha.requires_grad = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        q = (
            self.q_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )
        k = (
            self.k_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )
        v = (
            self.v_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )

        # RoPE
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # GQA
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            k = repeat_kv(k, repeats)
            v = repeat_kv(v, repeats)

        q_prime = F.softplus(q)

        valid_mask = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, batch_size, seq_len, hidden_states.device)
        mask_4d = valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(k.dtype)
        k_prime = F.softplus(k) * mask_4d
        v_masked = v * mask_4d

        is_causal = self.is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)

        if is_causal:
            # State-based linear scan
            S = torch.zeros(
                batch_size,
                self.num_heads,
                self.head_dim,
                self.head_dim,
                dtype=q.dtype,
                device=hidden_states.device,
            )
            z = torch.zeros(
                batch_size,
                self.num_heads,
                self.head_dim,
                dtype=q.dtype,
                device=hidden_states.device,
            )

            num_list = []
            denom_list = []

            for i in range(seq_len):
                q_i = q_prime[:, :, i, :]
                k_i = k_prime[:, :, i, :]
                v_i = v_masked[:, :, i, :]

                S = S + torch.einsum("bhd,bhe->bhde", k_i, v_i)
                z = z + k_i

                num_i = torch.einsum("bhd,bhde->bhe", q_i, S)
                denom_i = torch.einsum("bhd,bhd->bh", q_i, z).unsqueeze(-1)

                num_list.append(num_i)
                denom_list.append(denom_i)

            num = torch.stack(num_list, dim=2)
            denom = torch.stack(denom_list, dim=2).clamp(min=1e-4)
            lin_out = torch.nan_to_num(num / denom)

            # RALA-FIX-01: use lora_U, lora_V (trainable)
            rank_mat = torch.einsum("hdr,her->hde", self.lora_U, self.lora_V)
            aug_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,hde->bhne", q_prime, rank_mat) / denom)
        else:
            kv = torch.einsum("bhnd,bhne->bhde", k_prime, v_masked)
            z = k_prime.sum(dim=2)
            denom = torch.einsum("bhnd,bhd->bhn", q_prime, z).unsqueeze(-1).clamp(min=1e-4)
            lin_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,bhde->bhne", q_prime, kv) / denom)

            rank_mat = torch.einsum("hdr,her->hde", self.lora_U, self.lora_V)
            aug_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,hde->bhne", q_prime, rank_mat) / denom)

        # RALA-FIX-04: removed dead scale_factor=1.0
        # RALA-FIX-01: use lora_rank_alpha (trainable)
        out = (lin_out + self.lora_rank_alpha * aug_out) * mask_4d

        # head_mask
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
                out = out * head_mask.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(out.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
                out = out * head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(out.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                out = out * head_mask.to(out.dtype)

        out = out.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            return out, None

        output = self.out_proj(out)
        return output, None

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module
