def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
    if src is None:
        return

    cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
    has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
    is_nested = False
    if (
        (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            and hasattr(src, "output")
            and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
        )
        or (
            cls_name
            in [
                "BertSelfAttention",
                "RobertaSelfAttention",
                "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                "ElectraSelfAttention",
                "DebertaSelfAttention",
            ]
            or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
        )
        and not has_out_lin
    ):
        is_nested = True
    self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

    extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
        "q": (None, None),
        "k": (None, None),
        "v": (None, None),
        "o": (None, None),
    }

    # 1. GPT-2 fused
    if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
        c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
        base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
        c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
        if c_w is not None:
            c_w = c_w.data.clone()
            if (
                c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
            ):
                c_w = c_w.t()
            split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
            bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
            c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
            extracted["q"] = (
                c_w[:split_size, :],
                c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
            )
            extracted["k"] = (
                c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
            )
            extracted["v"] = (
                c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
            )

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if o_w is not None:
                o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    o_w = o_w.t()
                bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (
                    o_w,
                    bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                )

    # 2. RoBERTa nested
    elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
        s = src.self
        for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
            mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
            if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                extracted[proj_key] = (
                    mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                )
        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
            out_mod = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (
                out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
            )

    # 3. Flat modules
    else:
        proj_candidates = {
            "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
            "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
            "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
            "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
        }
        for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
            for name in names:
                if hasattr(src, name):
                    mod = getattr(src, name)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        extracted[proj_key] = (
                            mod.weight.data.clone(),
                            mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                        )
                        break

    missing = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing:
        try:
            from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

            adapter = UniversalAdapter()
            for k in missing:
                w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

    missing_after = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing_after:
        logger.warning(
            "RALALinearAttention.copy_weights: missing %s from %s",
            missing_after,
            src.__class__.__name__,
        )

    target_map = {
        "q": self.q_proj,
        "k": self.k_proj,
        "v": self.v_proj,
        "o": self.out_proj,
    }

    with torch.no_grad():
        for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is None:
                if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                    if target_mod.bias is not None:
                        nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                    continue
                continue

            src_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item())
            if src_norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    "RALALinearAttention: source weight for %s has near-zero norm %.6f",
                    proj_key,
                    src_norm,
                )

            if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
            elif w.t().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t())
            elif (
                w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                and w.shape[1] == target_mod.weight.shape[1]
            ):
                repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
            else:
                min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                target_mod.weight.zero_()
                target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

            if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                    target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                elif (
                    b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                    target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                else:
                    min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                    target_mod.bias.zero_()
                    target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
            elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

            fnorm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item())
            if fnorm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    "RALALinearAttention: %s has norm %.6f",
                    proj_key,
                    fnorm,
                )

    if self.freeze_base:
        for mod in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
            mod.freeze_base_weights()
        # RALA-FIX-01: U, V, alpha trainable via lora_ prefix
        self.lora_U.requires_grad = True
        self.lora_V.requires_grad = True
        self.lora_rank_alpha.requires_grad = True

Universal fallback: copy pretrained weights from old attention layer.

def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool | None = False,
    output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
    return_dict: bool | None = True,
    position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
    *args: Any,
    **kwargs: Any,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
    batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

    q = (
        self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
    )
    k = (
        self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
    )
    v = (
        self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
    )

    # RoPE
    if position_embeddings is None:
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
    if position_embeddings is not None:
        cos, sin = position_embeddings
        q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

    # GQA
    if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
        repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
        k = repeat_kv(k, repeats)
        v = repeat_kv(v, repeats)

    q_prime = F.softplus(q)

    valid_mask = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, batch_size, seq_len, hidden_states.device)
    mask_4d = valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(k.dtype)
    k_prime = F.softplus(k) * mask_4d
    v_masked = v * mask_4d

    is_causal = self.is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)

    if is_causal:
        # State-based linear scan
        S = torch.zeros(
            batch_size,
            self.num_heads,
            self.head_dim,
            self.head_dim,
            dtype=q.dtype,
            device=hidden_states.device,
        )
        z = torch.zeros(
            batch_size,
            self.num_heads,
            self.head_dim,
            dtype=q.dtype,
            device=hidden_states.device,
        )

        num_list = []
        denom_list = []

        for i in range(seq_len):
            q_i = q_prime[:, :, i, :]
            k_i = k_prime[:, :, i, :]
            v_i = v_masked[:, :, i, :]

            S = S + torch.einsum("bhd,bhe->bhde", k_i, v_i)
            z = z + k_i

            num_i = torch.einsum("bhd,bhde->bhe", q_i, S)
            denom_i = torch.einsum("bhd,bhd->bh", q_i, z).unsqueeze(-1)

            num_list.append(num_i)
            denom_list.append(denom_i)

        num = torch.stack(num_list, dim=2)
        denom = torch.stack(denom_list, dim=2).clamp(min=1e-4)
        lin_out = torch.nan_to_num(num / denom)

        # RALA-FIX-01: use lora_U, lora_V (trainable)
        rank_mat = torch.einsum("hdr,her->hde", self.lora_U, self.lora_V)
        aug_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,hde->bhne", q_prime, rank_mat) / denom)
    else:
        kv = torch.einsum("bhnd,bhne->bhde", k_prime, v_masked)
        z = k_prime.sum(dim=2)
        denom = torch.einsum("bhnd,bhd->bhn", q_prime, z).unsqueeze(-1).clamp(min=1e-4)
        lin_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,bhde->bhne", q_prime, kv) / denom)

        rank_mat = torch.einsum("hdr,her->hde", self.lora_U, self.lora_V)
        aug_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,hde->bhne", q_prime, rank_mat) / denom)

    # RALA-FIX-04: removed dead scale_factor=1.0
    # RALA-FIX-01: use lora_rank_alpha (trainable)
    out = (lin_out + self.lora_rank_alpha * aug_out) * mask_4d

    # head_mask
    if head_mask is not None:
        if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
            out = out * head_mask.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(out.dtype)
        elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
            out = out * head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(out.dtype)
        elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
            out = out * head_mask.to(out.dtype)

    out = out.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)

    if self.is_nested_encoder:
        return out, None

    output = self.out_proj(out)
    return output, None

Must return (output, attention_weights_or_None).

**kwargs: Any)

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("rala_attention")
class RALALinearAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank or 64)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None)

        self.hidden_size = getattr(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        if (
            (config is None or not hasattr(config, "hidden_size"))
            and "hidden_size" not in kwargs
            and pretrained_attn is not None
        ):
            for cand in ["q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "c_attn"]:
                mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, cand, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    w = mod.weight
                    self.hidden_size = w.shape[1] if w.ndim == 2 else self.hidden_size
                    break

        self.dim = self.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = getattr(
            config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = getattr(
            config,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
        )
        self.head_dim = getattr(
            config,
            "head_dim",
            kwargs.get("head_dim", self.dim // self.num_heads),
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.rank_r = getattr(
            comp_config,
            "rala_rank",
            getattr(config, "rala_rank", kwargs.get("rala_rank", 8)),
        )

        # RALA-FIX-01: rename trainable params with `lora_` prefix
        self.lora_U = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(self.num_heads, self.head_dim, self.rank_r) * 0.01)
        self.lora_V = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(self.num_heads, self.head_dim, self.rank_r) * 0.01)
        self.lora_rank_alpha = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0))

        # Decoder detection
        _mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi", "opt")
        self.is_decoder = (
            bool(getattr(config, "is_decoder", False))
            or bool(getattr(config, "causal", False))
            or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            or (pretrained_attn is not None and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn"))
            or bool(kwargs.get("is_causal", False))
        )
        if "is_decoder" in kwargs and kwargs["is_decoder"] is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(kwargs["is_decoder"])
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if config
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and source_attn is not None:
            p = next(source_attn.parameters(), None)
            if p is not None:
                target_dtype = p.dtype

        # Projections
        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )

        self.query = self.q_proj
        self.key = self.k_proj
        self.value = self.v_proj

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)
            try:
                src_device = next(source_attn.parameters()).device
                self.to(src_device)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if target_dtype is not None:
            self.to(target_dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight transfer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                "RALALinearAttention.copy_weights: missing %s from %s",
                missing_after,
                src.__class__.__name__,
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue
                    continue

                src_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item())
                if src_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "RALALinearAttention: source weight for %s has near-zero norm %.6f",
                        proj_key,
                        src_norm,
                    )

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                    and w.shape[1] == target_mod.weight.shape[1]
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                fnorm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item())
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "RALALinearAttention: %s has norm %.6f",
                        proj_key,
                        fnorm,
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
                mod.freeze_base_weights()
            # RALA-FIX-01: U, V, alpha trainable via lora_ prefix
            self.lora_U.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_V.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_rank_alpha.requires_grad = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        q = (
            self.q_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )
        k = (
            self.k_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )
        v = (
            self.v_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )

        # RoPE
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # GQA
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            k = repeat_kv(k, repeats)
            v = repeat_kv(v, repeats)

        q_prime = F.softplus(q)

        valid_mask = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, batch_size, seq_len, hidden_states.device)
        mask_4d = valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(k.dtype)
        k_prime = F.softplus(k) * mask_4d
        v_masked = v * mask_4d

        is_causal = self.is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)

        if is_causal:
            # State-based linear scan
            S = torch.zeros(
                batch_size,
                self.num_heads,
                self.head_dim,
                self.head_dim,
                dtype=q.dtype,
                device=hidden_states.device,
            )
            z = torch.zeros(
                batch_size,
                self.num_heads,
                self.head_dim,
                dtype=q.dtype,
                device=hidden_states.device,
            )

            num_list = []
            denom_list = []

            for i in range(seq_len):
                q_i = q_prime[:, :, i, :]
                k_i = k_prime[:, :, i, :]
                v_i = v_masked[:, :, i, :]

                S = S + torch.einsum("bhd,bhe->bhde", k_i, v_i)
                z = z + k_i

                num_i = torch.einsum("bhd,bhde->bhe", q_i, S)
                denom_i = torch.einsum("bhd,bhd->bh", q_i, z).unsqueeze(-1)

                num_list.append(num_i)
                denom_list.append(denom_i)

            num = torch.stack(num_list, dim=2)
            denom = torch.stack(denom_list, dim=2).clamp(min=1e-4)
            lin_out = torch.nan_to_num(num / denom)

            # RALA-FIX-01: use lora_U, lora_V (trainable)
            rank_mat = torch.einsum("hdr,her->hde", self.lora_U, self.lora_V)
            aug_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,hde->bhne", q_prime, rank_mat) / denom)
        else:
            kv = torch.einsum("bhnd,bhne->bhde", k_prime, v_masked)
            z = k_prime.sum(dim=2)
            denom = torch.einsum("bhnd,bhd->bhn", q_prime, z).unsqueeze(-1).clamp(min=1e-4)
            lin_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,bhde->bhne", q_prime, kv) / denom)

            rank_mat = torch.einsum("hdr,her->hde", self.lora_U, self.lora_V)
            aug_out = torch.nan_to_num(torch.einsum("bhnd,hde->bhne", q_prime, rank_mat) / denom)

        # RALA-FIX-04: removed dead scale_factor=1.0
        # RALA-FIX-01: use lora_rank_alpha (trainable)
        out = (lin_out + self.lora_rank_alpha * aug_out) * mask_4d

        # head_mask
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
                out = out * head_mask.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(out.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
                out = out * head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(out.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                out = out * head_mask.to(out.dtype)

        out = out.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            return out, None

        output = self.out_proj(out)
        return output, None

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

class RPCAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any = None,
        rank: int = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank if rank is not None else 64)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        comp_config = kwargs.get("comp_config", getattr(config, "compression_config", None))

        self.dim = int(_cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)))
        self.num_heads = int(
            _cfg_get(config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12))
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = int(
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                _cfg_get(
                    config,
                    "num_kv_heads",
                    kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
                ),
            )
        )

        cfg_head_dim = _cfg_get(config, "head_dim", None)
        kw_head_dim = kwargs.get("head_dim")
        inferred = cfg_head_dim or kw_head_dim

        if inferred is None and pretrained_attn is not None:
            for attr in ("q_proj", "query", "q_lin"):
                if hasattr(pretrained_attn, attr):
                    layer_q = getattr(pretrained_attn, attr)
                    if hasattr(layer_q, "weight"):
                        inferred = layer_q.weight.shape[0] // max(1, self.num_heads)
                        break

        self.head_dim = int(
            inferred or (self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads) if self.num_heads > 0 else 64)
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        # ---- sketch rank ----
        raw_rank = kwargs.pop(
            "rank",
            _cfg_get(
                comp_config,
                "rank",
                _cfg_get(config, "rank", rank if rank is not None else 64),
            ),
        )
        self.sketch_rank = max(1, min(int(raw_rank), self.dim))

        # ---- projection rank for FactorizedLoRALinear ----
        raw_proj_rank = int(
            kwargs.get(
                "proj_rank",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "proj_rank", _cfg_get(config, "proj_rank", 64)),
            )
        )
        # RPC-13: clamp and log
        self.proj_rank = max(1, raw_proj_rank)

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            mt = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            self.is_decoder = (
                bool(_cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", False))
                or bool(kwargs.get("is_causal", False))
                or any(
                    mt.startswith(p)
                    for p in (
                        "gpt",
                        "qwen",
                        "llama",
                        "mistral",
                        "gemma",
                        "phi",
                        "opt",
                        "bloom",
                        "falcon",
                        "olmo",
                        "mpt",
                        "stablelm",
                    )
                )
                or (pretrained_attn is not None and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn"))
            )
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # ---- LoRA config ----
        self.lora_rank = int(
            kwargs.get(
                "lora_rank",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_rank", _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", 16)),
            )
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            kwargs.get(
                "lora_alpha",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_alpha", _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
        )

        # ---- projections ----
        FF = FactorizedLoRALinear
        self.W_q = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.W_k = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.W_v = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = FF(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )

        self.q_proj = self.W_q
        self.k_proj = self.W_k
        self.v_proj = self.W_v
        self.query = self.W_q
        self.key = self.W_k
        self.value = self.W_v

        # ---- RPC-14: seeded random projection buffers ----
        # RPC-19: non-persistent (recomputed on load with same seed)
        seed = int(kwargs.get("sketch_seed", 0xC0FFEE))
        gen = torch.Generator(device="cpu").manual_seed(seed)
        std = self.head_dim**-0.5
        proj_q = torch.randn(self.num_heads, self.head_dim, self.sketch_rank, generator=gen) * std
        proj_k = torch.randn(self.num_heads, self.head_dim, self.sketch_rank, generator=gen) * std
        self.register_buffer("proj_q", proj_q.float(), persistent=False)
        self.register_buffer("proj_k", proj_k.float(), persistent=False)

        # ---- RPC-15: configurable sketch scale ----
        # Default: 1/sqrt(head_dim) — matches the original attention scale
        # because sketching preserves the expected inner product.
        default_scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        self.sketch_scale = float(
            kwargs.get(
                "sketch_scale",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "sketch_scale", default_scale),
            )
        )

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # ---- RPC-18: storage accounting ----
        self._orig_storage_bytes = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes = 0

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

        logger.info(
            "RPCAttention: hidden=%d, heads=%d, head_dim=%d, sketch_rank=%d, "
            "proj_rank=%d, is_decoder=%s, lora_rank=%d, scale=%.4f",
            self.dim,
            self.num_heads,
            self.head_dim,
            self.sketch_rank,
            self.proj_rank,
            self.is_decoder,
            self.lora_rank,
            self.sketch_scale,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.W_q

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.W_k

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.W_v

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SVD init
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _svd_init(w: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor | None, proj: FactorizedLoRALinear) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = w.detach().float()
            if W.ndim != 2:
                W = W.reshape(W.size(0), -1)

            out_t, in_t = proj.out_features, proj.in_features
            if W.shape[0] != out_t or W.shape[1] != in_t:
                padded = torch.zeros(out_t, in_t, dtype=W.dtype, device=W.device)
                r = min(out_t, W.shape[0])
                c = min(in_t, W.shape[1])
                padded[:r, :c] = W[:r, :c]
                W = padded

            try:
                U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("RPC._svd_init SVD failed: %s", e)
                return

            r = min(proj.rank, U.size(1), Vh.size(0))
            proj.down.weight.data.zero_()
            proj.up.weight.data.zero_()
            proj.down.weight.data[:r, :].copy_(Vh[:r, :].to(proj.down.weight.dtype))
            US = U[:, :r] @ torch.diag(S[:r])
            proj.up.weight.data[:, :r].copy_(US.to(proj.up.weight.dtype))

            if b is not None and proj.up.bias is not None:
                n = min(b.numel(), proj.up.bias.numel())
                proj.up.bias.data[:n].copy_(b[:n].to(proj.up.bias.dtype))
            elif proj.up.bias is not None:
                proj.up.bias.data.zero_()

            # RPC-17: freeze base after SVD init.
            proj.freeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight copy
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        # RPC-18: track original storage
        try:
            for p in src.parameters():
                self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bmod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c, "bias", None))
                c_b = bmod.data.clone() if bmod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (c_w[:split], c_b[:split] if c_b is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split : 2 * split],
                    c_b[split : 2 * split] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split :],
                    c_b[2 * split :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                o = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bo = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(o, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (o_w, bo.data.clone() if bo is not None else None)

        # BERT / RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    extracted[key] = (
                        m.weight.data.clone(),
                        m.bias.data.clone() if m.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                om = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    om.weight.data.clone(),
                    om.bias.data.clone() if om.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # Flat modules
        else:
            cand = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in cand.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(src, n):
                        m = getattr(src, n)
                        if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            extracted[key] = (
                                m.weight.data.clone(),
                                m.bias.data.clone() if m.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                ua = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = ua.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"RPCAttention.copy_weights: missing {missing_after} from {cls_name}."
            )

        # ---- SVD init for each projection ----
        for key, target in (
            ("q", self.W_q),
            ("k", self.W_k),
            ("v", self.W_v),
            ("o", self.out_proj),
        ):
            w, b = extracted[key]
            if w is None:
                if key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    eye = torch.eye(
                        target.out_features,
                        target.in_features,
                        device=target.down.weight.device,
                        dtype=target.down.weight.dtype,
                    )
                    self._svd_init(eye, None, target)
                continue

            # GQA handling for k, v
            if key in ("k", "v"):
                out_t = target.out_features
                src_out = w.shape[0]
                if src_out > out_t and src_out % out_t == 0:
                    rep = src_out // out_t
                    w = w.view(out_t, rep, -1).mean(dim=1)
                    if b is not None:
                        b = b.view(out_t, rep).mean(dim=1)
                elif src_out < out_t and out_t % src_out == 0:
                    rep = out_t // src_out
                    w = w.repeat(rep, 1)
                    if b is not None:
                        b = b.repeat(rep)

            self._svd_init(w, b, target)

        # ---- storage accounting ----
        try:
            for m in (self.W_q, self.W_k, self.W_v, self.out_proj):
                self._comp_storage_bytes += m.storage_bytes()
            for buf in (self.proj_q, self.proj_k):
                self._comp_storage_bytes += buf.numel() * buf.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # RPC-06 / RPC-20: robust mask helper
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _prepare_valid_mask(
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
        B: int,
        L: int,
        device: torch.device,
    ) -> torch.Tensor | None:
        """
        Returns a bool mask of shape (B, L) where True = valid token.
        If the input mask cannot be interpreted, returns None (no masking).
        """
        if attention_mask is None:
            return None

        def _to_bool(t: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            if t.dtype == torch.bool:
                return t
            if t.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return t > -1e4
            return t > 0

        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask)
            if m.shape[-1] == L:
                return m
            return None
        if attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask).squeeze(1)
            if m.shape[-1] == L:
                return m
            return None
        if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask).any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
            if m.shape[-1] == L:
                return m
            return None

        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported attention_mask dim: {attention_mask.dim()}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        L_kv = kv_input.shape[1]

        # ---- Projections ----
        q = self.W_q(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        k = self.W_k(kv_input).view(B, L_kv, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        v = self.W_v(kv_input).view(B, L_kv, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

        # ---- RPC-08: RoPE with past_len ----
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            past_len = 0
            if past_key_value is not None:
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 1:
                    past_len = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
                elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                    past_len = past_key_value.key_cache[0].shape[-2]
            cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
            sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # ---- GQA ----
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        # ---- Sketching ----
        proj_q = self.proj_q.to(dtype=q.dtype, device=q.device)
        proj_k = self.proj_k.to(dtype=k.dtype, device=k.device)

        # (B, H, L, head_dim) @ (H, head_dim, r) -> (B, H, L, r)
        q_sketched = torch.einsum("bhld,hdr->bhlr", q, proj_q)
        k_sketched = torch.einsum("bhld,hdr->bhlr", k, proj_k)

        scores = (
            torch.matmul(
                q_sketched.float(),
                k_sketched.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous(),
            )
            * self.sketch_scale
        )

        # ---- RPC-07 / RPC-21: causal only for self-attention ----
        if self.is_decoder and not is_cross:
            diag = 1 + max(0, L_kv - L)
            causal = (
                torch.triu(
                    torch.full((L, L_kv), float("-inf"), device=device, dtype=torch.float32),
                    diagonal=diag,
                )
                .unsqueeze(0)
                .unsqueeze(0)
            )
            scores = scores + causal

        # ---- Attention mask ----
        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        valid_mask = self._prepare_valid_mask(eff_mask, B, L_kv, device)
        if valid_mask is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask[:, None, None, :], -1e4)

        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
        probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
            probs = probs * head_mask.to(probs.dtype)

        context = torch.matmul(probs, v)
        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        # ---- Output projection (RPC-10: assert shape) ----
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(context)

        if out.shape[-1] != D:
            raise RuntimeError(f"RPCAttention: output feature dim {out.shape[-1]} != input {D}")

        return out, (probs if output_attentions else None)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        """Report per-component storage."""

        def _sz(p):
            if p is None:
                return 0
            if isinstance(p, nn.Parameter):
                return p.numel() * p.element_size()
            if isinstance(p, torch.Tensor):
                return p.numel() * p.element_size()
            return 0

        out = {}
        for name, m in (
            ("W_q", self.W_q),
            ("W_k", self.W_k),
            ("W_v", self.W_v),
            ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
        ):
            out[name] = m.storage_bytes()
        out["proj_q"] = self.proj_q.numel() * self.proj_q.element_size()
        out["proj_k"] = self.proj_k.numel() * self.proj_k.element_size()
        out["total"] = sum(out.values())
        return out

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Report per-component storage."""

    def _sz(p):
        if p is None:
            return 0
        if isinstance(p, nn.Parameter):
            return p.numel() * p.element_size()
        if isinstance(p, torch.Tensor):
            return p.numel() * p.element_size()
        return 0

    out = {}
    for name, m in (
        ("W_q", self.W_q),
        ("W_k", self.W_k),
        ("W_v", self.W_v),
        ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
    ):
        out[name] = m.storage_bytes()
    out["proj_q"] = self.proj_q.numel() * self.proj_q.element_size()
    out["proj_k"] = self.proj_k.numel() * self.proj_k.element_size()
    out["total"] = sum(out.values())
    return out

class SparsePruningCallback(_HFTrainerCallback):
    """
    Periodically hard-thresholds the sparse residual of every
    SpectronAttention module in the model.
    """

    def __init__(self, model: Any, prune_freq: int = 200, final_sparsity: float = 0.7):
        self.model = model
        self.prune_freq = prune_freq
        self.final_sparsity = final_sparsity

    def on_step_end(self, args, state, control, **kwargs):
        if state.global_step > 0 and state.global_step % self.prune_freq == 0:
            for m in self.model.modules():
                if isinstance(m, SpectronAttention) and m.add_sparse:
                    m.prune_all_sparse(self.final_sparsity)

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("spectra_v2")
class SpectraV2Attention(BaseAttention):
    """
    MHA wrapper around PyTorch's scaled_dot_product_attention.
    Previously named spectra_v2 (deprecated alias).
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        model_config: Any | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        total_layers: int = 12,
        bert_mode: bool = False,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        cfg = config or model_config or kwargs.get("config")
        super().__init__(cfg, rank or 64)
        self.config = cfg
        comp_config = _cfg_get(cfg, "compression_config", kwargs.get("comp_config"))

        self.hidden_size = kwargs.get(
            "hidden_size",
            _cfg_get(cfg, "hidden_size", _cfg_get(cfg, "dim", _cfg_get(cfg, "d_model", 768))),
        )
        self.dim = self.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = kwargs.get(
            "num_heads",
            kwargs.get(
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(
                    cfg,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    _cfg_get(cfg, "num_heads", _cfg_get(cfg, "n_heads", 12)),
                ),
            ),
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = kwargs.get(
            "num_kv_heads",
            kwargs.get(
                "num_key_value_heads",
                _cfg_get(
                    cfg,
                    "num_key_value_heads",
                    _cfg_get(cfg, "num_kv_heads", self.num_heads),
                ),
            ),
        )
        self.head_dim = kwargs.get(
            "head_dim",
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "head_dim",
                self.dim // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64,
            ),
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.layer_idx = layer_idx
        self.total_layers = total_layers
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode

        is_dec = _cfg_get(cfg, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = _cfg_get(
            cfg,
            "lora_rank",
            _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
        )
        self.lora_alpha = _cfg_get(
            cfg,
            "lora_alpha",
            _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # Projections
        self.query = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.key = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.value = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        dropout_p = _cfg_get(cfg, "dropout", _cfg_get(cfg, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = (
            hasattr(src, "out_proj")
            or hasattr(src, "dense")
            or hasattr(src, "c_proj")
            or hasattr(src, "out_lin")
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_proj
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused c_attn / c_proj
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                extracted["o"] = _unpack_weight_bias(src.c_proj)

        # 2. DistilBERT style: q_lin, k_lin, v_lin, out_lin
        if hasattr(src, "q_lin") and src.q_lin is not None:
            extracted["q"] = _unpack_weight_bias(src.q_lin)
            extracted["k"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, "k_lin", None))
            extracted["v"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, "v_lin", None))
            extracted["o"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, "out_lin", None))

        # 3. BERT / RoBERTa style: query, key, value, dense
        if hasattr(src, "query") and src.query is not None and extracted["q"][0] is None:
            extracted["q"] = _unpack_weight_bias(src.query)
            extracted["k"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, "key", None))
            extracted["v"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, "value", None))
            extracted["o"] = _unpack_weight_bias(
                getattr(src, "dense", getattr(src, "out_proj", None))
            )

        # 4. Nested structures (RoBERTa / BERT container objects)
        if hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query") and extracted["q"][0] is None:
            s = src.self
            extracted["q"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(s, "query", None))
            extracted["k"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(s, "key", None))
            extracted["v"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(s, "value", None))
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                extracted["o"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src.output, "dense", None))

        # 5. Qwen / LLaMA / HF Attention style: q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj
        if hasattr(src, "q_proj") and src.q_proj is not None and extracted["q"][0] is None:
            extracted["q"] = _unpack_weight_bias(src.q_proj)
            extracted["k"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, "k_proj", None))
            extracted["v"] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, "v_proj", None))
            extracted["o"] = _unpack_weight_bias(
                getattr(src, "o_proj", getattr(src, "dense", getattr(src, "out_proj", None)))
            )

        # 6. UniversalAdapter fallback for custom names
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        target_map = {
            "q": self.query,
            "k": self.key,
            "v": self.value,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue
                    else:
                        logger.warning(
                            "Attention module copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights."
                        )
                        continue

                src_norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item()

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] > target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and w.shape[0] % target_mod.weight.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = w.shape[0] // target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    # For GQA: average the query head groups
                    w_grouped = w.view(target_mod.weight.shape[0], repeats, w.shape[1]).mean(dim=1)
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w_grouped)
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] > target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and b.shape[0] % target_mod.bias.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = b.shape[0] // target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        b_grouped = b.view(target_mod.bias.shape[0], repeats).mean(dim=1)
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b_grouped)
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
                if src_norm > 1e-3 and fnorm < 1e-4:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        f"SpectraV2Attention: copy_weights for {proj_key} produced near-zero norm "
                        f"({fnorm:.2e}) from a valid source ({src_norm:.2e}). Aborting to prevent silent "
                        f"model corruption."
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.query, self.key, self.value, self.out_proj]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

    _copy_pretrained = copy_weights

    @classmethod
    def from_distilbert_pretrained(
        cls, old_attn: nn.Module, model_config: Any | None = None, **kwargs: Any
    ) -> SpectraV2Attention:
        return cls(model_config=model_config, pretrained_attn=old_attn, **kwargs)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_states = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        N_k = kv_states.shape[1]

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask

        Q = self.query(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        K = self.key(kv_states).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        V = self.value(kv_states).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

        # Apply RoPE if provided
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        # Repeat KV for GQA
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            K = repeat_kv(K, repeats)
            V = repeat_kv(V, repeats)

        scale = self.head_dim**-0.5

        # Causal determination
        _is_causal = getattr(self, "is_decoder", False) and not is_cross and N > 1

        attn_weights = None

        if not output_attentions:
            try:
                _qs = Q.half() if Q.dtype == torch.float32 else Q
                _ks = K.half() if K.dtype == torch.float32 else K
                _vs = V.half() if V.dtype == torch.float32 else V

                attn_mask_for_sdpa = None
                if eff_mask is not None and not _is_causal:
                    if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                        attn_mask_for_sdpa = eff_mask.bool().view(
                            eff_mask.shape[0], 1, 1, eff_mask.shape[1]
                        )
                    elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                        attn_mask_for_sdpa = eff_mask.bool()

                _sdpa_out = torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    _qs,
                    _ks,
                    _vs,
                    attn_mask=attn_mask_for_sdpa,
                    is_causal=_is_causal,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                )

                _sdpa_out = _sdpa_out.to(hidden_states.dtype)

                # Apply head_mask
                if head_mask is not None and head_mask.dim() >= 1:
                    hm = head_mask.view(-1, self.num_heads)
                    if hm.shape[0] == 1:
                        hm = hm.expand(_sdpa_out.shape[0], -1)
                    _sdpa_out = _sdpa_out * hm.view(_sdpa_out.shape[0], self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(
                        _sdpa_out.dtype
                    )

                _sdpa_out = _sdpa_out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
                _sdpa_out = torch.nan_to_num(_sdpa_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

                context = _sdpa_out
                if self.is_nested_encoder:
                    if context.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
                        raise RuntimeError(
                            f"SpectraV2Attention: output dimension mismatch. "
                            f"Got {context.shape[-1]}, expected {hidden_states.shape[-1]}."
                        )
                    return context, None

                output = self.out_proj(context)
                if output.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        f"SpectraV2Attention: output dimension mismatch. "
                        f"Got {output.shape[-1]}, expected {hidden_states.shape[-1]}."
                    )
                return output, None

            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("SDPA path failed or fell back: %s", e)
                pass

        # Fallback path (manual attention)
        scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        if _is_causal:
            causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.full((N, N_k), -1e4, device=scores.device), diagonal=1)
            scores = scores + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        if eff_mask is not None:
            scores = apply_attention_mask(scores, eff_mask)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
        attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
                attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(
                    attn_weights.dtype
                )
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
                attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.view(
                    head_mask.size(0), self.num_heads, 1, 1
                ).to(attn_weights.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.to(attn_weights.dtype)

        context = (
            torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
            .contiguous()
            .view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
        )

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            if context.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"SpectraV2Attention: output dimension mismatch. "
                    f"Got {context.shape[-1]}, expected {hidden_states.shape[-1]}."
                )
            return context, (attn_weights if output_attentions else None)

        output = self.out_proj(context)
        if output.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"SpectraV2Attention: output dimension mismatch. "
                f"Got {output.shape[-1]}, expected {hidden_states.shape[-1]}."
            )
        return output, (attn_weights if output_attentions else None)
