@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("spectraformer_attention")
class SpectraformerAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Spectraformer Attention: Exact linear-time attention via random feature kernelization.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank or 64)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = _cfg_get(config, "compression_config", None) or kwargs.get("comp_config")

        self.hidden_size = (
            kwargs.get("hidden_size")
            or _cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", None)
            or _cfg_get(config, "d_model", None)
        )
        if self.hidden_size is None and pretrained_attn is not None:
            for cand in ["q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "c_attn"]:
                mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, cand, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    w = mod.weight
                    self.hidden_size = w.shape[1] if w.ndim == 2 else None
                    if self.hidden_size is not None:
                        break
        if self.hidden_size is None:
            self.hidden_size = 768
        self.dim = self.hidden_size

        self.num_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_heads")
            or _cfg_get(config, "num_attention_heads", None)
            or _cfg_get(config, "num_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_kv_heads")
            or _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                self.num_heads,
            )
        )
        self.head_dim = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "head_dim",
            kwargs.get("head_dim", self.hidden_size // self.num_heads),
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.rank = _cfg_get(
            comp_config,
            "spectraformer_rank",
            _cfg_get(config, "spectraformer_rank", kwargs.pop("rank", rank or 64)),
        )

        # Causal decoder detection
        is_dec = _cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        model_type = _cfg_get(config, "model_type", "").lower() if config else ""
        _mt = (model_type or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # LoRA config
        self.lora_rank = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "lora_rank",
            _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
        )
        self.lora_alpha = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "lora_alpha",
            _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # Aliases
        self.query = self.q_proj
        self.key = self.k_proj
        self.value = self.v_proj

        # Persistent orthogonal random feature matrix R: [head_dim, rank]
        # Generated once with QR decomposition for maximum feature variance coverage
        torch.manual_seed(42)
        H = torch.randn(self.head_dim, self.rank)
        Q_ortho, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
        self.register_buffer("R", Q_ortho.float())

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", kwargs.get("dropout", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        """
        Robust weight copying across GPT-2, RoBERTa/BERT nested, Qwen/LLaMA, and DistilBERT.
        Validates Frobenius norm >= 1e-4.
        """
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa nested encoder
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"SpectraformerAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue

                src_norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item()
                if src_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"Source weight for {proj_key} has near-zero norm {src_norm:.6f} < 1e-4"
                    )

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                    and w.shape[1] == target_mod.weight.shape[1]
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "Compressor copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights."
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

        # Preserve existing LoRA adapters from source projections
        for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
            sub_name = {"q": "query", "k": "key", "v": "value", "o": "dense"}.get(proj_key, "")
            src_sub = getattr(src, sub_name, None)
            if src_sub is None and hasattr(src, f"{proj_key}_proj"):
                src_sub = getattr(src, f"{proj_key}_proj")
            if src_sub is None and hasattr(src, f"{proj_key}_lin"):
                src_sub = getattr(src, f"{proj_key}_lin")

            if src_sub is not None and hasattr(src_sub, "lora_A") and hasattr(src_sub, "lora_B"):
                if target_mod.lora_A is not None and hasattr(src_sub.lora_A, "weight"):
                    try:
                        target_mod.lora_A.data.copy_(src_sub.lora_A.weight.data)
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
                if target_mod.lora_B is not None and hasattr(src_sub.lora_B, "weight"):
                    try:
                        target_mod.lora_B.data.copy_(src_sub.lora_B.weight.data)
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")

        return True

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        """
        Forward pass with linear random feature kernel:
        1. Linear projection to Q, K, V
        2. Optional RoPE embedding
        3. GQA head expansion
        4. Random orthogonal feature projection with ReLU activation
        5. Linear attention context computation (recurrent for causal, global for bidirectional)
        """
        if hidden_states is None:
            hidden_states = kwargs.get("hidden_states") or (args[0] if args else None)
        if hidden_states is None:
            raise ValueError("SpectraformerAttention forward requires hidden_states")
        if attention_mask is None:
            attention_mask = kwargs.get("attention_mask")
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape

        q = (
            self.q_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        k = (
            self.k_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.v_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # Apply RoPE if provided
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # GQA head expansion
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

        # Ensure R matches head_dim and rank
        R = self.R.to(dtype=hidden_states.dtype, device=hidden_states.device)
        if R.shape[0] != self.head_dim or R.shape[1] != self.rank:
            g = torch.Generator(
                device=(hidden_states.device if hidden_states.device.type != "cpu" else "cpu")
            ).manual_seed(42)
            H = torch.randn(
                self.head_dim,
                self.rank,
                device=hidden_states.device,
                dtype=torch.float32,
                generator=g,
            )
            R, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
            R = R.to(dtype=hidden_states.dtype)
            if hasattr(self, "R") and isinstance(self.R, torch.Tensor) and self.R.shape == R.shape:
                self.R.data.copy_(R)
            else:
                self.R = R

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        # Q_proj, K_proj: (B, H, N, r) - positive ELU+1 feature map with single scale on Q
        Q_proj = (F.elu(torch.matmul(q, R)) + 1.0) * scale
        K_proj = F.elu(torch.matmul(k, R)) + 1.0

        # Mask padding in K_proj
        if attention_mask is not None:
            valid_tok = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, hidden_states.device)
            v_mask_4d = valid_tok.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype=hidden_states.dtype)
            K_proj = K_proj * v_mask_4d
            v = v * v_mask_4d

        if self.is_decoder:
            # Recurrent linear causal attention: exact vectorized computation
            causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(N, N, device=Q_proj.device, dtype=torch.bool))
            aff = torch.matmul(
                Q_proj.float(), K_proj.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
            )  # (B, H, N, N)
            aff = aff.masked_fill(~causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), 0.0)

            num = torch.matmul(aff, v.float())  # (B, H, N, d)
            den = aff.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-8  # (B, H, N, 1)
            context = (num / den).to(hidden_states.dtype)
        else:
            # Bidirectional linear attention: exact O(N·r·d) computation
            KV = torch.matmul(
                K_proj.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous().float(), v.float()
            )  # (B, H, r, d)
            Z = (
                K_proj.float().sum(dim=2, keepdim=True).transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
            )  # (B, H, r, 1)

            num = torch.matmul(Q_proj.float(), KV)  # (B, H, N, d)
            den = torch.matmul(Q_proj.float(), Z) + 1e-8  # (B, H, N, 1)
            context = (num / den).to(hidden_states.dtype)

        if head_mask is not None:
            hm = head_mask.to(context.device)
            if hm.dim() == 1:
                hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 2:
                hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], 1, 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 3:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
            context = context * hm

        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(context)

        return (output, None)

Spectraformer Attention: Exact linear-time attention via random feature kernelization.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
    """
    Robust weight copying across GPT-2, RoBERTa/BERT nested, Qwen/LLaMA, and DistilBERT.
    Validates Frobenius norm >= 1e-4.
    """
    if src is None:
        return

    cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
    has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
    is_nested = False
    if (
        (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            and hasattr(src, "output")
            and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
        )
        or (
            cls_name
            in [
                "BertSelfAttention",
                "RobertaSelfAttention",
                "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                "ElectraSelfAttention",
                "DebertaSelfAttention",
            ]
            or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
        )
        and not has_out_lin
    ):

    self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        "q": (None, None),
        "k": (None, None),
        "v": (None, None),
        "o": (None, None),
    }

    # 1. GPT-2 fused c_attn
    if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
        c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
        base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
        c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
        if c_w is not None:
            c_w = c_w.data.clone()
            if (
                (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
            ):
                c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()

            bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
            c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
            extracted["q"] = (
                c_w[:split_size, :],
                c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
            )
            extracted["k"] = (
                c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
            )
            extracted["v"] = (
                c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
            )

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if o_w is not None:
                o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (
                    o_w,
                    bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                )

    # 2. RoBERTa nested encoder
    elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
        s = src.self
        for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
            mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
            if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                extracted[proj_key] = (
                    mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                )
        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
            out_mod = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (
                out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
            )

    # 3. Flat modules (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
    else:
        proj_candidates = {
            "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
            "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
            "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
            "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
        }
        for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
            for name in names:
                if hasattr(src, name):
                    mod = getattr(src, name)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        extracted[proj_key] = (
                            mod.weight.data.clone(),
                            mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                        )
                        break

    missing = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing:
        try:
            from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

            adapter = UniversalAdapter()
            for k in missing:
                w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

    missing_after = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing_after:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"SpectraformerAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
        )

    target_map = {
        "q": self.q_proj,
        "k": self.k_proj,
        "v": self.v_proj,
        "o": self.out_proj,
    }

    with torch.no_grad():
        for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is None:
                if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                    if target_mod.bias is not None:
                        nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                    continue

            src_norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item()
            if src_norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"Source weight for {proj_key} has near-zero norm {src_norm:.6f} < 1e-4"
                )

            if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
            elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
            elif (
                w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                and w.shape[1] == target_mod.weight.shape[1]
            ):
                repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
            else:
                min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                target_mod.weight.zero_()
                target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

            if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                    target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                elif (
                    b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                    target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                else:
                    min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                    target_mod.bias.zero_()
                    target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
            elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

            fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
            if fnorm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    "Compressor copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights."
                )

    if self.freeze_base:
        for mod in [self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj]:
            mod.freeze_base_weights()

    # Preserve existing LoRA adapters from source projections
    for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
        sub_name = {"q": "query", "k": "key", "v": "value", "o": "dense"}.get(proj_key, "")
        src_sub = getattr(src, sub_name, None)
        if src_sub is None and hasattr(src, f"{proj_key}_proj"):
            src_sub = getattr(src, f"{proj_key}_proj")
        if src_sub is None and hasattr(src, f"{proj_key}_lin"):
            src_sub = getattr(src, f"{proj_key}_lin")

        if src_sub is not None and hasattr(src_sub, "lora_A") and hasattr(src_sub, "lora_B"):
            if target_mod.lora_A is not None and hasattr(src_sub.lora_A, "weight"):
                try:
                    target_mod.lora_A.data.copy_(src_sub.lora_A.weight.data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
            if target_mod.lora_B is not None and hasattr(src_sub.lora_B, "weight"):
                try:
                    target_mod.lora_B.data.copy_(src_sub.lora_B.weight.data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")

    return True

Robust weight copying across GPT-2, RoBERTa/BERT nested, Qwen/LLaMA, and DistilBERT. Validates Frobenius norm >= 1e-4.

def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool | None = False,
    output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
    return_dict: bool | None = True,
    position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
    *args: Any,
    **kwargs: Any,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
    """
    Forward pass with linear random feature kernel:
    1. Linear projection to Q, K, V
    2. Optional RoPE embedding
    3. GQA head expansion
    4. Random orthogonal feature projection with ReLU activation
    5. Linear attention context computation (recurrent for causal, global for bidirectional)
    """
    if hidden_states is None:
        hidden_states = kwargs.get("hidden_states") or (args[0] if args else None)
    if hidden_states is None:
        raise ValueError("SpectraformerAttention forward requires hidden_states")
    if attention_mask is None:
        attention_mask = kwargs.get("attention_mask")
    B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape

    q = (
        self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )
    k = (
        self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )
    v = (
        self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )

    # Apply RoPE if provided
    if position_embeddings is None:
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
    if position_embeddings is not None:
        cos, sin = position_embeddings
        q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

    # GQA head expansion
    if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
        k = repeat_kv(k, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
        v = repeat_kv(v, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

    # Ensure R matches head_dim and rank
    R = self.R.to(dtype=hidden_states.dtype, device=hidden_states.device)
    if R.shape[0] != self.head_dim or R.shape[1] != self.rank:
        g = torch.Generator(
            device=(hidden_states.device if hidden_states.device.type != "cpu" else "cpu")
        ).manual_seed(42)
        H = torch.randn(
            self.head_dim,
            self.rank,
            device=hidden_states.device,
            dtype=torch.float32,
            generator=g,
        )
        R, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
        R = R.to(dtype=hidden_states.dtype)
        if hasattr(self, "R") and isinstance(self.R, torch.Tensor) and self.R.shape == R.shape:
            self.R.data.copy_(R)
        else:
            self.R = R

    scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
    # Q_proj, K_proj: (B, H, N, r) - positive ELU+1 feature map with single scale on Q
    Q_proj = (F.elu(torch.matmul(q, R)) + 1.0) * scale
    K_proj = F.elu(torch.matmul(k, R)) + 1.0

    # Mask padding in K_proj
    if attention_mask is not None:
        valid_tok = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, hidden_states.device)
        v_mask_4d = valid_tok.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype=hidden_states.dtype)
        K_proj = K_proj * v_mask_4d
        v = v * v_mask_4d

    if self.is_decoder:
        # Recurrent linear causal attention: exact vectorized computation
        causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(N, N, device=Q_proj.device, dtype=torch.bool))
        aff = torch.matmul(
            Q_proj.float(), K_proj.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
        )  # (B, H, N, N)
        aff = aff.masked_fill(~causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), 0.0)

        num = torch.matmul(aff, v.float())  # (B, H, N, d)
        den = aff.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-8  # (B, H, N, 1)
        context = (num / den).to(hidden_states.dtype)
    else:
        # Bidirectional linear attention: exact O(N·r·d) computation
        KV = torch.matmul(
            K_proj.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous().float(), v.float()
        )  # (B, H, r, d)
        Z = (
            K_proj.float().sum(dim=2, keepdim=True).transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
        )  # (B, H, r, 1)

        num = torch.matmul(Q_proj.float(), KV)  # (B, H, N, d)
        den = torch.matmul(Q_proj.float(), Z) + 1e-8  # (B, H, N, 1)
        context = (num / den).to(hidden_states.dtype)

    if head_mask is not None:
        hm = head_mask.to(context.device)
        if hm.dim() == 1:
            hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 2:
            hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], 1, 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 3:
            hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
        context = context * hm

    context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)

    if self.is_nested_encoder:
        output = context
    else:
        output = self.out_proj(context)

    return (output, None)

Forward pass with linear random feature kernel: 1. Linear projection to Q, K, V 2. Optional RoPE embedding 3. GQA head expansion 4. Random orthogonal feature projection with ReLU activation 5. Linear attention context computation (recurrent for causal, global for bidirectional)

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("spectron", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class SpectronAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        old_attn: Any | None = None,
        dropout: float = 0.1,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        ranks: dict[str, int] | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        add_sparse: bool = False,
        sparsity_level: float = 0.7,
        whitening: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        seed: int = 42,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        model_config = config or kwargs.get("model_config")
        super().__init__(model_config)

        torch.manual_seed(seed)

        self.hidden_size = int(
            _cfg_get(model_config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        )
        self.num_heads = int(
            _cfg_get(
                model_config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(model_config, "num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)),
            )
        )
        self.head_dim = int(
            _cfg_get(
                model_config,
                "head_dim",
                kwargs.get("head_dim", self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads)),
            )
        )

        cfg_kv = _cfg_get(
            model_config,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            _cfg_get(model_config, "num_kv_heads", None),
        )
        if cfg_kv is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv)
        elif kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(kwargs["num_kv_heads"])
        else:
            self.num_kv_heads = self.num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.q_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_head_size = self.q_head_size

        # SP-FIX-04: explicit decoder detection
        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            _mt = str(_cfg_get(model_config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            _dec_pfx = (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
            self.is_decoder = (
                bool(_cfg_get(model_config, "is_decoder", False))
                or bool(_cfg_get(model_config, "causal", False))
                or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _dec_pfx)
                or (old_attn is not None and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"))
            )

        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # Ranks (with global clamp)
        default_r = int(_cfg_get(model_config, "svd_rank", _cfg_get(model_config, "rank", 64)))
        default_r = max(1, min(default_r, self.hidden_size, self.all_head_size))
        self.ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        for k in ("q", "k", "v", "o"):
            val = ranks.get(k, default_r) if ranks else default_r
            self.ranks[k] = max(1, min(int(val), self.hidden_size, self.all_head_size))

        self.add_sparse = bool(add_sparse)
        self.sparsity_level = float(sparsity_level)
        self.lora_rank = max(0, int(lora_rank)) if lora_rank else 0
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (lora_alpha / self.lora_rank) if self.lora_rank > 0 else 1.0

        # Whitening factors per projection (from calibration)
        self.whitening = whitening or {}

        # ---- factorised base projections (SVD) ----
        self.q_down = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.ranks["q"], bias=False)
        self.q_up = nn.Linear(self.ranks["q"], self.q_head_size, bias=True)
        self.k_down = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.ranks["k"], bias=False)
        self.k_up = nn.Linear(self.ranks["k"], self.kv_head_size, bias=True)
        self.v_down = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.ranks["v"], bias=False)
        self.v_up = nn.Linear(self.ranks["v"], self.kv_head_size, bias=True)
        self.o_down = nn.Linear(self.q_head_size, self.ranks["o"], bias=False)
        self.o_up = nn.Linear(self.ranks["o"], self.hidden_size, bias=True)

        for mod in (
            self.q_down,
            self.q_up,
            self.k_down,
            self.k_up,
            self.v_down,
            self.v_up,
            self.o_down,
            self.o_up,
        ):
            nn.init.orthogonal_(mod.weight)
            if mod.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(mod.bias)
            mod.weight.requires_grad = False
            if mod.bias is not None:
                mod.bias.requires_grad = False

        # ---- LoRA adapters ----
        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.q_lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.hidden_size))
            self.q_lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.q_head_size, self.lora_rank))
            self.k_lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.hidden_size))
            self.k_lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.kv_head_size, self.lora_rank))
            self.v_lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.hidden_size))
            self.v_lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.kv_head_size, self.lora_rank))
            self.o_lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.q_head_size))
            self.o_lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.hidden_size, self.lora_rank))
            for p_a in (self.q_lora_A, self.k_lora_A, self.v_lora_A, self.o_lora_A):
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(p_a, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("q_lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("q_lora_B", None)
            self.register_parameter("k_lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("k_lora_B", None)
            self.register_parameter("v_lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("v_lora_B", None)
            self.register_parameter("o_lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("o_lora_B", None)

        # ---- optional sparse residuals (init from zero) ----
        if self.add_sparse:
            self.q_sparse = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.q_head_size, self.hidden_size))
            self.k_sparse = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.kv_head_size, self.hidden_size))
            self.v_sparse = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.kv_head_size, self.hidden_size))
            self.o_sparse = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.hidden_size, self.q_head_size))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("q_sparse", None)
            self.register_parameter("k_sparse", None)
            self.register_parameter("v_sparse", None)
            self.register_parameter("o_sparse", None)

        dropout_p = _cfg_get(
            model_config,
            "dropout",
            _cfg_get(
                model_config,
                "hidden_dropout_prob",
                _cfg_get(model_config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", dropout),
            ),
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(float(dropout_p) if dropout_p is not None else 0.1)

        src_attn = old_attn or kwargs.get("pretrained_attn")
        if src_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SP-FIX-01: activation-aware factorization
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _factorise_and_set(
        self,
        W: torch.Tensor,
        bias: torch.Tensor | None,
        down: nn.Linear,
        up: nn.Linear,
        rank: int,
        whitening_L: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Factories W into down/up via activation-aware SVD:

            if whitening_L is None:
                U Σ V^T = SVD(W)
                down = √Σ V^T
                up   = U √Σ
            else:
                U Σ V^T = SVD(W L)
                down = √Σ V^T L^{-1}
                up   = U √Σ
        """
        with torch.no_grad():
            W_f = W.float()
            d_out, d_in = W_f.shape
            r = min(rank, d_out, d_in, down.out_features, up.in_features)
            r = max(r, 1)

            L = None
            if whitening_L is not None and whitening_L.shape == (d_in, d_in):
                L = whitening_L.float().to(W_f.device)

            if L is not None:
                eps = 1e-6 * torch.trace(L @ L.t()).item() / max(1, d_in)
                eye = torch.eye(d_in, device=W_f.device, dtype=W_f.dtype)
                try:
                    L_reg = L + eps * eye  # small regularization
                    W_eff = W_f @ L_reg
                    U, s, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_eff, full_matrices=False)
                    L_inv = torch.linalg.inv(L_reg)
                    Vt = Vt @ L_inv  # bring back to original coordinate
                except Exception as e:
                    log.debug("Whitening failed on factorise (%s); fallback.", e)
                    U, s, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_f, full_matrices=False)
            else:
                U, s, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_f, full_matrices=False)

            sqrt_s = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(s[:r], min=1e-12))
            down_w = (sqrt_s.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, :]).to(down.weight.device, down.weight.dtype)
            up_w = (U[:, :r] * sqrt_s.unsqueeze(0)).to(up.weight.device, up.weight.dtype)

            down.weight.data.zero_()
            up.weight.data.zero_()
            down.weight.data[:r, :].copy_(down_w)
            up.weight.data[:, :r].copy_(up_w)
            if bias is not None and up.bias is not None:
                up.bias.data.copy_(bias.to(up.bias.device, up.bias.dtype))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights with activation-aware factorization
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q, b_q = None, None
            w_k, b_k = None, None
            w_v, b_v = None, None
            w_o, b_o = None, None

            src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
            has_out_proj = any(
                hasattr(old_attn, a)
                for a in (
                    "dense",
                    "out_proj",
                    "c_proj",
                    "out_lin",
                    "o_proj",
                    "wo",
                    "o",
                    "proj",
                )
            )
            if src_cls_name in (
                "BertSelfAttention",
                "RobertaSelfAttention",
                "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                "ElectraSelfAttention",
                "DebertaSelfAttention",
            ) or (hasattr(old_attn, "query") and not has_out_proj):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True

            if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True
                src = old_attn.self
                if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                    w_o, b_o = _unpack_weight_bias(old_attn.output.dense)
            else:
                src = old_attn

            # GPT-2 fused c_attn
            if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                c_w, c_b = _unpack_weight_bias(src.c_attn)
                if c_w is not None:
                    if c_w.shape[0] == self.hidden_size and c_w.shape[1] != self.hidden_size:
                        c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                    q_dim = self.q_head_size
                    k_dim = self.hidden_size
                    v_dim = self.hidden_size
                    w_q = c_w[:q_dim, :]
                    w_k = c_w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :]
                    w_v = c_w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :]
                    if c_b is not None:
                        b_q = c_b[:q_dim]
                        b_k = c_b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim]
                        b_v = c_b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim]

                out_w, out_b = _unpack_weight_bias(src.c_proj)
                if out_w is not None:
                    if out_w.shape[0] == self.hidden_size and out_w.shape[1] != self.hidden_size:
                        out_w = out_w.t().contiguous()
                    w_o, b_o = out_w, out_b

            # BERT / RoBERTa nested
            elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
                w_q, b_q = _unpack_weight_bias(src.query)
                w_k, b_k = _unpack_weight_bias(src.key)
                w_v, b_v = _unpack_weight_bias(src.value)
                if w_o is None:
                    for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                        if hasattr(src, attr):
                            w_o, b_o = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, attr))
                            break

            # Qwen / LLaMA
            elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
                w_q, b_q = _unpack_weight_bias(src.q_proj)
                w_k, b_k = _unpack_weight_bias(src.k_proj)
                w_v, b_v = _unpack_weight_bias(src.v_proj)
                if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                    w_o, b_o = _unpack_weight_bias(src.o_proj)

            # DistilBERT
            elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
                w_q, b_q = _unpack_weight_bias(src.q_lin)
                w_k, b_k = _unpack_weight_bias(src.k_lin)
                w_v, b_v = _unpack_weight_bias(src.v_lin)
                if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                    w_o, b_o = _unpack_weight_bias(src.out_lin)

            # UniversalAdapter fallback
            if w_q is None or w_k is None or w_v is None:
                try:
                    adapter = UniversalAdapter(old_attn)
                    extracted = adapter.extract_qkv_out()
                    if extracted:
                        w_q = extracted.get("q_weight")
                        b_q = extracted.get("q_bias")
                        w_k = extracted.get("k_weight")
                        b_k = extracted.get("k_bias")
                        w_v = extracted.get("v_weight")
                        b_v = extracted.get("v_bias")
                        if w_o is None:
                            w_o = extracted.get("out_weight")
                            b_o = extracted.get("out_bias")
                except Exception as e:
                    log.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

            missing = []
            if w_q is None:
                missing.append("query")
            if w_k is None:
                missing.append("key")
            if w_v is None:
                missing.append("value")
            if missing:
                logger.warning("Spectron: missing %s; keeping random init.", missing)
                return

            def _orient(w, out_dim, in_dim):
                if w.shape == (out_dim, in_dim):
                    return w
                if w.shape == (in_dim, out_dim):
                    return w.t().contiguous()
                return w

            # ---- activation-aware factorization using self.whitening ----
            w_q = _orient(w_q, self.q_head_size, self.hidden_size)
            self._factorise_and_set(
                w_q,
                b_q,
                self.q_down,
                self.q_up,
                self.ranks["q"],
                whitening_L=self.whitening.get("q", None),
            )

            # K with GQA pooling
            if w_k.shape[0] == self.kv_head_size:
                w_k = _orient(w_k, self.kv_head_size, self.hidden_size)
                self._factorise_and_set(
                    w_k,
                    b_k,
                    self.k_down,
                    self.k_up,
                    self.ranks["k"],
                    whitening_L=self.whitening.get("k", None),
                )
            else:
                rep = max(1, w_k.shape[0] // self.kv_head_size)
                w_k_pooled = (
                    w_k.view(self.num_kv_heads, rep, self.head_dim, -1)
                    .mean(dim=1)
                    .view(self.kv_head_size, -1)
                )
                b_k_pooled = (
                    b_k.view(self.num_kv_heads, rep, self.head_dim).mean(dim=1).view(-1)
                    if b_k is not None
                    else None
                )
                self._factorise_and_set(
                    w_k_pooled,
                    b_k_pooled,
                    self.k_down,
                    self.k_up,
                    self.ranks["k"],
                    whitening_L=self.whitening.get("k", None),
                )

            # V with GQA pooling
            if w_v.shape[0] == self.kv_head_size:
                w_v = _orient(w_v, self.kv_head_size, self.hidden_size)
                self._factorise_and_set(
                    w_v,
                    b_v,
                    self.v_down,
                    self.v_up,
                    self.ranks["v"],
                    whitening_L=self.whitening.get("v", None),
                )
            else:
                rep = max(1, w_v.shape[0] // self.kv_head_size)
                w_v_pooled = (
                    w_v.view(self.num_kv_heads, rep, self.head_dim, -1)
                    .mean(dim=1)
                    .view(self.kv_head_size, -1)
                )
                b_v_pooled = (
                    b_v.view(self.num_kv_heads, rep, self.head_dim).mean(dim=1).view(-1)
                    if b_v is not None
                    else None
                )
                self._factorise_and_set(
                    w_v_pooled,
                    b_v_pooled,
                    self.v_down,
                    self.v_up,
                    self.ranks["v"],
                    whitening_L=self.whitening.get("v", None),
                )

            # Output projection
            if w_o is not None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
                w_o = _orient(w_o, self.hidden_size, self.q_head_size)
                self._factorise_and_set(
                    w_o,
                    b_o,
                    self.o_down,
                    self.o_up,
                    self.ranks["o"],
                    whitening_L=self.whitening.get("o", None),
                )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _linear_proj(
        self,
        x: torch.Tensor,
        down: nn.Linear,
        up: nn.Linear,
        lora_A: nn.Parameter | None,
        lora_B: nn.Parameter | None,
        sparse_w: nn.Parameter | None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        out = up(down(x))
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and lora_A is not None and lora_B is not None:
            a = lora_A.to(x.dtype)
            b = lora_B.to(x.dtype)
            out = out + F.linear(F.linear(x, a), b) * self.scaling
        if self.add_sparse and sparse_w is not None:
            out = out + F.linear(x, sparse_w.to(x.dtype))
        return out

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        kv_len = kv_input.shape[1]

        Q = self._linear_proj(
            hidden_states,
            self.q_down,
            self.q_up,
            self.q_lora_A,
            self.q_lora_B,
            self.q_sparse,
        )
        K = self._linear_proj(
            kv_input,
            self.k_down,
            self.k_up,
            self.k_lora_A,
            self.k_lora_B,
            self.k_sparse,
        )
        V = self._linear_proj(
            kv_input,
            self.v_down,
            self.v_up,
            self.v_lora_A,
            self.v_lora_B,
            self.v_sparse,
        )

        Q = Q.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = K.view(B, kv_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = V.view(B, kv_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # RoPE
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            past_len = 0
            if past_key_value is not None:
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 1:
                    past_len = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
                elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                    past_len = past_key_value.key_cache[0].shape[-2]
            cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + N, :]
            sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + N, :]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        # GQA
        if self.num_kv_heads < self.num_heads:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)

        # SP-FIX-04: correct causal condition
        is_causal = self.is_decoder and not is_cross and N > 1

        # SP-FIX-05: removed padding/truncation hack
        scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        if is_causal:
            causal = (
                torch.triu(
                    torch.full(
                        (N, kv_len),
                        float("-inf"),
                        device=scores.device,
                        dtype=torch.float32,
                    ),
                    diagonal=1 + max(0, kv_len - N),
                )
                .unsqueeze(0)
                .unsqueeze(0)
            )
            scores = scores + causal

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        if eff_mask is not None:
            if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                scores = scores + (eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2).float() - 1.0) * 10000.0
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 3:
                scores = scores + eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                scores = scores + eff_mask.float()

        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
        attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

        if head_mask is not None:
            hm = head_mask.to(attn_weights.dtype)
            if hm.dim() == 1:
                hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 2:
                hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], hm.shape[1], 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 3:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
            attn_weights = attn_weights * hm

        context = (
            torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
            .view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
        )
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self._linear_proj(
                context,
                self.o_down,
                self.o_up,
                self.o_lora_A,
                self.o_lora_B,
                self.o_sparse,
            )

        return output, (attn_weights if output_attentions else None)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def total_sparse_l1(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Sum of L1 norms of all sparse residuals. Add to training loss."""
        if not self.add_sparse:
            return torch.zeros((), device=self.q_down.weight.device)
        total = torch.zeros((), device=self.q_down.weight.device)
        for s in (self.q_sparse, self.k_sparse, self.v_sparse, self.o_sparse):
            if s is not None:
                total = total + s.abs().sum()
        return total

    def prune_all_sparse(self, sparsity: float) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            for s in (self.q_sparse, self.k_sparse, self.v_sparse, self.o_sparse):
                if s is None or s.numel() == 0:
                    continue
                keep = int(s.numel() * (1.0 - sparsity))
                if keep <= 0:
                    s.zero_()
                    continue
                if keep >= s.numel():
                    continue
                flat = s.abs().view(-1)
                kth = torch.kthvalue(flat, s.numel() - keep).values
                s.mul_(s.abs() >= kth)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.
