Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("tlinformer_attention")
class TLinFormerAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    TLinFormer: linear-time attention with learnable low-rank sequence projection.

    Encoder mode (N > rank):
        K' = E · K,   V' = F · V,   E, F ∈ R^{r × N}   (learnable)
        scores = Q · K'ᵀ / √d          # (B, H, N, r)
        O = softmax(scores) · V'        # (B, H, N, d)

    Complexity: O(N · r · d) instead of O(N² · d).

    Decoder mode: dense causal attention (Linformer is not causal — projecting
    over all N positions would leak future information).

    Key corrections:
        - proj_k / proj_v are nn.Parameters (learnable).
        - Broadcasting bug in masked_fill fixed (q_mask used).
        - GQA repeat moved after projection (saves 4× FLOPs).
        - output_attentions handled safely.
        - KV-cache support added.
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank or 64)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None)

        # ---- hidden size ----
        self.hidden_size = getattr(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        if config is None or not hasattr(config, "hidden_size"):
            if pretrained_attn is not None:
                for cand in ("q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "c_attn"):
                    mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, cand, None)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        w = mod.weight
                        if w.ndim == 2:
                            self.hidden_size = w.shape[1]
                            break

        # ---- heads ----
        self.num_heads = int(
            getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12))
            if config
            else 12
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
            )
            if config
            else self.num_heads
        )
        self.head_dim = int(
            getattr(config, "head_dim", None)
            or kwargs.get("head_dim")
            or (self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads))
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        # ---- rank ----
        requested_rank = int(
            _cfg_get(
                comp_config,
                "tlinformer_rank",
                getattr(config, "tlinformer_rank", None) if config else None,
            )
            or kwargs.pop("rank", rank or 64)
        )
        self.rank = max(1, min(requested_rank, self.head_dim))

        self.max_seq_len = int(
            getattr(config, "max_position_embeddings", kwargs.get("max_seq_len", 2048))
            if config
            else 2048
        )

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        _mt = (getattr(config, "model_type", "") if config else "").lower()
        is_dec = bool(
            getattr(config, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False)) if config else False
        )
        if any(
            _mt.startswith(p)
            for p in (
                "gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi",
                "opt", "bloom", "falcon", "olmo", "mpt", "stablelm",
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = is_dec
        self.is_causal = is_dec
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # ---- LoRA ----
        self.lora_rank = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if config
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # ---- projections ----
        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size, self.all_head_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size, self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size, self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size, self.hidden_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # ---- FIX 1: Sequence projections as LEARNABLE Parameters ----
        proj_k = torch.empty(self.rank, self.max_seq_len)
        proj_v = torch.empty(self.rank, self.max_seq_len)
        # Better init: scaled normal + identity on leading diagonal
        nn.init.normal_(proj_k, std=1.0 / math.sqrt(max(1, self.rank)))
        nn.init.normal_(proj_v, std=1.0 / math.sqrt(max(1, self.rank)))
        md = min(self.rank, self.max_seq_len)
        with torch.no_grad():
            proj_k[:md, :md].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
            proj_v[:md, :md].fill_diagonal_(1.0)

        self.proj_k = nn.Parameter(proj_k)
        self.proj_v = nn.Parameter(proj_v)

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", kwargs.get("dropout", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # ---- FIX: non-persistent caches for KV-cache mode ----
        self.register_buffer("_cached_K_proj", None, persistent=False)
        self.register_buffer("_cached_V_proj", None, persistent=False)

        src = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        if src is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src)

    # ---- compatibility aliases ----
    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def E(self):
        return self.proj_k

    @property
    def F(self):
        return self.proj_v

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = any(hasattr(src, a) for a in ("out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj"))
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # ---- GPT-2 fused c_attn / c_proj ----
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
            cw = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
            if cw is not None:
                cw = cw.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    "Conv1D" in c.__class__.__name__
                    or "Conv1D" in base_c.__class__.__name__
                    or hasattr(c, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or (cw.ndim == 2 and cw.shape[0] < cw.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    cw = cw.t().contiguous()
                split = cw.size(0) // 3
                bmod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c, "bias", None))
                cb = bmod.data.clone() if bmod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (cw[:split], cb[:split] if cb is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (
                    cw[split : 2 * split],
                    cb[split : 2 * split] if cb is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    cw[2 * split :],
                    cb[2 * split :] if cb is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                o = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
                ow = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
                if ow is not None:
                    ow = ow.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        "Conv1D" in o.__class__.__name__
                        or "Conv1D" in base_o.__class__.__name__
                        or hasattr(o, "nf")
                        or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                        or (ow.ndim == 2 and ow.shape[0] < ow.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        ow = ow.t().contiguous()
                    bo = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(o, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (ow, bo.data.clone() if bo is not None else None)

        # ---- RoBERTa / BERT nested ----
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    extracted[key] = (
                        m.weight.data.clone(),
                        m.bias.data.clone() if m.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                om = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    om.weight.data.clone(),
                    om.bias.data.clone() if om.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # ---- flat modules ----
        else:
            cand = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in cand.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(src, n):
                        m = getattr(src, n)
                        if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            extracted[key] = (
                                m.weight.data.clone(),
                                m.bias.data.clone() if m.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # ---- UniversalAdapter fallback ----
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                ua = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = ua.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"TLinFormer copy_weights: missing {missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for key, target in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[key]
                if w is None:
                    if key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target.weight)
                        if target.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

                if (
                    w.shape != target.weight.shape
                    and w.t().contiguous().shape == target.weight.shape
                ):
                    w = w.t().contiguous()

                if w.shape != target.weight.shape:
                    logger.warning(
                        "TLinFormer: '%s' shape mismatch %s vs target %s; truncating.",
                        key, tuple(w.shape), tuple(target.weight.shape),
                    )

                out_d = min(target.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
                target.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(target.weight.device, target.weight.dtype)
                )

                if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                    mn = min(target.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target.bias.data[:mn].copy_(b[:mn].to(target.bias.device, target.bias.dtype))
                elif target.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)

        # ---- LoRA copy ----
        for key, target in target_map.items():
            src_mod = None
            if key == "o" and hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                src_mod = src.output.dense
            elif hasattr(src, "self"):
                sub_map = {"q": "query", "k": "key", "v": "value"}
                src_mod = getattr(src.self, sub_map.get(key, ""), None)
            else:
                for n in (
                    "q_lin", "q_proj", "query",
                    "k_lin", "k_proj", "key",
                    "v_lin", "v_proj", "value",
                    "out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj",
                ):
                    if hasattr(src, n):
                        cand = getattr(src, n)
                        if key in n:
                            src_mod = cand
                            break
            if src_mod is not None:
                sA = getattr(src_mod, "lora_A", None)
                sB = getattr(src_mod, "lora_B", None)
                if (
                    sA is not None
                    and sB is not None
                    and target.lora_A is not None
                    and target.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    wA = getattr(sA, "weight", sA)
                    wB = getattr(sB, "weight", sB)
                    try:
                        if wA.shape == target.lora_A.shape:
                            target.lora_A.data.copy_(wA.data.to(target.lora_A.dtype))
                        if wB.shape == target.lora_B.shape:
                            target.lora_B.data.copy_(wB.data.to(target.lora_B.dtype))
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.debug("TLinFormer LoRA copy failed for '%s': %s", key, e)

        if self.freeze_base:
            for m in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
                m.freeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _prepare_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, device):
        if attention_mask is None:
            return torch.ones(B, N, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)
        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                return attention_mask
            if attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return attention_mask > -1e4
            return attention_mask > 0
        if attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            m = attention_mask
            if m.dtype == torch.bool:
                return m.squeeze(1)
            if m.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return (m > -1e4).squeeze(1)
            return (m > 0).squeeze(1)
        if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            m = attention_mask
            if m.dtype == torch.bool:
                v = m
            elif m.dtype.is_floating_point:
                v = m > -1e4
            else:
                v = m > 0
            return v.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported attention_mask dim: {attention_mask.dim()}")

    def _projection_matrix(self, name: str, L: int, dtype, device) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        FIX: Safe padding for L > max_seq_len (no risky tiling or interpolation).

        For L ≤ max_seq_len: slice proj[:, :L].
        For L > max_seq_len: slice full matrix and pad remaining columns with zeros.
        This is the safest approach — it doesn't fabricate out-of-distribution
        projection weights for unseen positions.
        """
        buf = getattr(self, name)  # (rank, max_seq_len)

        if L <= self.max_seq_len:
            return buf[:, :L].to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

        # L > max_seq_len: use full projection + zero-pad remaining positions
        logger.warning(
            "TLinFormer: sequence length %d exceeds max_seq_len %d; "
            "using zero-padding for positions beyond training length.",
            L, self.max_seq_len,
        )
        P = buf.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
        P_padded = F.pad(P, (0, L - self.max_seq_len), value=0.0)
        return P_padded

    @staticmethod
    def _reshape_head_mask(head_mask, B, H, device, dtype):
        """Correct shape handling for head_mask."""
        if head_mask is None:
            return None
        hm = head_mask.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)
        if hm.dim() == 1:
            # (H,) → (1, H, 1, 1)
            return hm.view(1, H, 1, 1)
        if hm.dim() == 2:
            # (B, H) → (B, H, 1, 1)
            return hm.view(hm.shape[0], H, 1, 1)
        if hm.dim() == 3:
            # Interpret as (B, H, 1) → (B, H, 1, 1)
            return hm.unsqueeze(-1)
        if hm.dim() == 4:
            return hm
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported head_mask dim: {hm.dim()}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        use_cache: bool | None = False,
        *args,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, H_kv, Dh = self.num_heads, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim

        # ---- Projections ----
        q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, H, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, H_kv, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, H_kv, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # ---- RoPE ----
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # ---- Valid mask ----
        valid = self._prepare_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, hidden_states.device)
        q_mask = valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, 1, N, 1)
        key_mask = valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)  # (B, 1, 1, N)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(Dh)

        # ============================================================
        # Decoder / causal path: dense attention
        # ============================================================
        if self.is_decoder:
            # ---- KV Cache handling ----
            if past_key_value is not None:
                # Concatenate with cached K, V
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)):
                    past_k, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
                else:
                    past_k = getattr(past_key_value, "key_cache", [None])[0]
                    past_v = getattr(past_key_value, "value_cache", [None])[0]
                if past_k is not None and past_v is not None:
                    k = torch.cat([past_k, k], dim=2)
                    v = torch.cat([past_v, v], dim=2)

            # GQA repeat
            if self.kv_repeat > 1:
                k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
                v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

            N_kv = k.shape[-2]

            # Compute attention
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            # Causal mask
            q_pos = torch.arange(N_kv - N, N_kv, device=scores.device).unsqueeze(1)
            k_pos = torch.arange(N_kv, device=scores.device).unsqueeze(0)
            causal = (k_pos > q_pos).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
            scores = scores.masked_fill(causal, -1e4)

            # Padding mask
            if attention_mask is not None:
                # For padded positions
                if N_kv == N:
                    scores = scores.masked_fill(~key_mask, -1e4)

            attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
            attn = torch.nan_to_num(attn, nan=0.0)
            attn = self.dropout(attn)

            if head_mask is not None:
                attn = attn * self._reshape_head_mask(head_mask, B, H, attn.device, attn.dtype)

            ctx = torch.matmul(attn, v)
            if N_kv == N:
                ctx = ctx * q_mask.to(ctx.dtype)

            ctx = ctx.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
            out = ctx if self.is_nested_encoder else self.out_proj(ctx)

            # Cache for next step
            new_cache = None
            if use_cache:
                # Undo GQA repeat before caching
                if self.kv_repeat > 1:
                    k_cache = k[:, ::self.kv_repeat, :, :].contiguous()
                    v_cache = v[:, ::self.kv_repeat, :, :].contiguous()
                else:
                    k_cache, v_cache = k, v
                new_cache = (k_cache, v_cache)

            attn_out = attn if output_attentions else None
            if new_cache is not None:
                return out, new_cache
            return out, attn_out

        # ============================================================
        # Encoder path: Linformer linear attention
        # ============================================================

        # Degenerate case: N ≤ rank → dense attention (identity-ish projection)
        if self.rank >= N:
            if self.kv_repeat > 1:
                k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
                v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~key_mask, -1e4)
            attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
            attn = torch.nan_to_num(attn, nan=0.0)
            attn = self.dropout(attn)
            if head_mask is not None:
                attn = attn * self._reshape_head_mask(head_mask, B, H, attn.device, attn.dtype)
            ctx = torch.matmul(attn, v)
            ctx = ctx * q_mask.to(ctx.dtype)
            ctx = ctx.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
            out = ctx if self.is_nested_encoder else self.out_proj(ctx)
            return out, (attn if output_attentions else None)

        # ---- Linformer proper: N > rank ----

        # FIX 2 (Broadcasting bug): use q_mask (B,1,N,1), not key_mask (B,1,1,N)
        # Both masks are logically equivalent for masking K/V along sequence dim,
        # but q_mask broadcasts correctly against (B, H, N, Dh).
        k_for_proj = k.masked_fill(~q_mask, 0.0)  # (B, H_kv, N, Dh)
        v_for_proj = v.masked_fill(~q_mask, 0.0)

        # FIX 3 (GQA waste): project on H_kv heads FIRST, then repeat
        P_k = self._projection_matrix("proj_k", N, hidden_states.dtype, hidden_states.device)
        P_v = self._projection_matrix("proj_v", N, hidden_states.dtype, hidden_states.device)

        # Project on H_kv heads (cheap!)
        K_proj = torch.einsum("rn,bhnd->bhrd", P_k, k_for_proj)  # (B, H_kv, r, Dh)
        V_proj = torch.einsum("rn,bhnd->bhrd", P_v, v_for_proj)  # (B, H_kv, r, Dh)

        # NOW apply GQA repeat on the small (r-sized) tensors
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K_proj = repeat_kv(K_proj, self.kv_repeat)  # (B, H, r, Dh)
            V_proj = repeat_kv(V_proj, self.kv_repeat)  # (B, H, r, Dh)

        # scores: (B, H, N, r)
        scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), K_proj.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
        attn = torch.nan_to_num(attn, nan=0.0)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)

        if head_mask is not None:
            attn = attn * self._reshape_head_mask(head_mask, B, H, attn.device, attn.dtype)

        # context: (B, H, N, r) @ (B, H, r, Dh) → (B, H, N, Dh)
        ctx = torch.matmul(attn, V_proj)
        ctx = ctx * q_mask.to(ctx.dtype)

        ctx = ctx.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
        out = ctx if self.is_nested_encoder else self.out_proj(ctx)

        # FIX 4 (output_attentions): return None or approximate reconstruction
        if output_attentions:
            # Approximate reconstruction using key projection:
            # Original attention shape (B, H, N, N)
            # Reconstruction: attn @ P_k  where P_k: (r, N) → (B, H, N, N)
            pseudo_attn = torch.matmul(attn.float(), P_k.float())  # (B, H, N, N)
            pseudo_attn = pseudo_attn.to(hidden_states.dtype)
            # Zero-out padded positions
            pseudo_attn = pseudo_attn * key_mask.to(pseudo_attn.dtype)
        else:
            pseudo_attn = None

        return out, pseudo_attn

TLinFormer: linear-time attention with learnable low-rank sequence projection.

Encoder mode (N > rank): K' = E · K, V' = F · V, E, F ∈ R^{r × N} (learnable) scores = Q · K'ᵀ / √d # (B, H, N, r) O = softmax(scores) · V' # (B, H, N, d)

Complexity: O(N · r · d) instead of O(N² · d).

Decoder mode: dense causal attention (Linformer is not causal — projecting over all N positions would leak future information).

Key corrections: - proj_k / proj_v are nn.Parameters (learnable). - Broadcasting bug in masked_fill fixed (q_mask used). - GQA repeat moved after projection (saves 4× FLOPs). - output_attentions handled safely. - KV-cache support added.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("tpa_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class TPAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Tensor Product Attention v2 with asymmetric rank support.

    Key improvements:
      - rank_a × rank_b = head_dim (supports head_dim=64 AND head_dim=128)
      - Better CP decomposition (50 iterations with early stopping)
      - Stricter max_cp_error (0.05)
      - KV cache support for autoregressive inference
    """

    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_causal: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank_a: int = 8,
        rank_b: int = 8,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        max_cp_error: float = 0.05,
        cp_iterations: int = 50,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank_a)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        src = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("old_attn")
        self.max_cp_error = float(max_cp_error)
        self.cp_iterations = int(cp_iterations)

        # ---- hidden size ----
        dim = None
        if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            dim = _cfg_get(
                config,
                "hidden_size",
                _cfg_get(config, "dim", _cfg_get(config, "d_model", None)),
            )
        if dim is None and src is not None:
            dim = getattr(src, "hidden_size", getattr(src, "embed_dim", None))
            if dim is None:
                for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"):
                    m = getattr(src, attr, None)
                    if m is not None and hasattr(m, "in_features"):
                        dim = m.in_features
                        break
            if dim is None and hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src.c_attn, "weight"):
                w = src.c_attn.weight
                dim = w.shape[0] if w.shape[0] != 3 * w.shape[1] else w.shape[1]
        if dim is None:
            dim = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)

        # ---- heads ----
        num_heads = None
        if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            num_heads = _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(config, "n_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_heads", None)),
            )
        if num_heads is None and src is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(
                src,
                "num_heads",
                getattr(src, "num_attention_heads", getattr(src, "n_head", None)),
            )
        if num_heads is None:
            num_heads = 12

        self.dim = int(dim)
        self.num_heads = max(1, int(num_heads))

        # ---- head_dim ----
        cfg_hd = _cfg_get(config, "head_dim", None) if config is not None else None
        kw_hd = kwargs.get("head_dim")
        if cfg_hd is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(cfg_hd)
        elif kw_hd is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(kw_hd)
        elif src is not None and getattr(src, "head_dim", None) is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(src.head_dim)
        else:
            self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads

        # ---- GQA ----
        cfg_kv = (
            _cfg_get(config, "num_key_value_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
            if config is not None
            else None
        )
        if cfg_kv is None and src is not None:
            cfg_kv = getattr(src, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(src, "num_kv_heads", None))
        self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv) if cfg_kv is not None else self.num_heads
        self.num_kv_groups = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.q_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        # ---- Asymmetric rank support ----
        # Try to find (rank_a, rank_b) such that rank_a * rank_b == head_dim
        raw_ra = kwargs.pop("rank_a", rank_a)
        raw_rb = kwargs.pop("rank_b", rank_b)
        self.rank_a = int(raw_ra)
        self.rank_b = int(raw_rb)

        if self.rank_a * self.rank_b != self.head_dim:
            # Try to auto-adjust rank_b to match head_dim
            if self.head_dim % self.rank_a == 0:
                self.rank_b = self.head_dim // self.rank_a
                logger.info(
                    "Auto-adjusted rank_b to %d to match head_dim=%d",
                    self.rank_b, self.head_dim,
                )
            elif self.head_dim % self.rank_b == 0:
                self.rank_a = self.head_dim // self.rank_b
                logger.info(
                    "Auto-adjusted rank_a to %d to match head_dim=%d",
                    self.rank_a, self.head_dim,
                )
            else:
                self._use_tensor_product = False
                logger.warning(
                    "Cannot find (rank_a, rank_b) with rank_a*rank_b=%d. "
                    "Falling back to standard projection.",
                    self.head_dim,
                )
        else:
            self._use_tensor_product = True

        if self._use_tensor_product:
            self.rank_a = int(self.rank_a)
            self.rank_b = int(self.rank_b)
        else:
            # Standard projection mode
            self.rank_a = self.head_dim
            self.rank_b = 1

        self.effective_qk_dim = self.head_dim

        self.lora_rank = int(kwargs.get("lora_rank", _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", lora_rank)))
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            kwargs.get("lora_alpha", _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))
        )

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        _mt = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if config is not None else ""
        _dec_prefixes = (
            "gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi",
            "opt", "bloom", "falcon", "olmo", "mpt", "stablelm",
        )
        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        elif config is not None and (
            _cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", False)
            or _cfg_get(config, "is_causal", False)
            or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _dec_prefixes)
        ):
            self.is_decoder = True
        else:
            self.is_decoder = bool(kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))

        self.is_nested_encoder = False
        self.gradient_checkpointing = bool(kwargs.get("gradient_checkpointing", False))

        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and src is not None:
            p = next(src.parameters(), None)
            if p is not None:
                target_dtype = p.dtype

        # ---- projections (tensor product or standard) ----
        if self._use_tensor_product:
            # Tensor product: two factor projections
            self.q_proj1 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.num_heads * self.rank_a,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.q_proj2 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.num_heads * self.rank_b,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.k_proj1 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.num_kv_heads * self.rank_a,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.k_proj2 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.num_kv_heads * self.rank_b,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
        else:
            # Standard projection: single factor
            self.q_proj1 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.q_head_size,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.q_proj2 = None
            self.k_proj1 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.kv_head_size,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.k_proj2 = None

        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.q_head_size, self.dim,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
        )

        # ---- LoRA on Q/K (optional) ----
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self._use_tensor_product:
            self.q_lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.dim, dtype=target_dtype)
            )
            self.q_lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros(self.q_head_size, self.lora_rank, dtype=target_dtype)
            )
            self.k_lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.dim, dtype=target_dtype)
            )
            self.k_lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros(self.kv_head_size, self.lora_rank, dtype=target_dtype)
            )
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.q_lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.k_lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("q_lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("q_lora_B", None)
            self.register_parameter("k_lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("k_lora_B", None)

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

        if target_dtype is not None:
            for proj in [self.q_proj1, self.k_proj1, self.v_proj, self.out_proj]:
                if proj is not None:
                    proj.to(target_dtype)
            for proj in [self.q_proj2, self.k_proj2]:
                if proj is not None:
                    proj.to(target_dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight transfer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q = w_k = w_v = w_o = None
            b_q = b_k = b_v = b_o = None

            src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
            has_no_out = not any(
                hasattr(old_attn, a)
                for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj", "c_proj", "out_lin", "proj")
            )
            if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
                hasattr(old_attn, "query") and has_no_out
            ):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True

            if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True
                src = old_attn.self
                if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                    w_o = old_attn.output.dense.weight.detach()
                    b_o = (
                        old_attn.output.dense.bias.detach()
                        if old_attn.output.dense.bias is not None else None
                    )
            else:
                src = old_attn

            # Extract weights from various architectures
            if hasattr(src, "c_attn"):
                W = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
                B_vec = src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None
                if W.shape[0] != 3 * self.dim and W.shape[1] == 3 * self.dim:
                    W = W.t().contiguous()
                if W.shape[0] == 3 * self.dim:
                    w_q, w_k, w_v = W.chunk(3, dim=0)
                    if B_vec is not None:
                        b_q, b_k, b_v = B_vec.chunk(3, dim=0)
                if hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                    w_o = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
                    if w_o.shape[0] != self.dim and w_o.shape[1] == self.dim:
                        w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                    b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

            elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
                w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None
                if w_o is None:
                    for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                        if hasattr(src, a):
                            proj = getattr(src, a)
                            w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                            b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                            break

            elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
                w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.q_proj.bias.detach() if src.q_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.k_proj.bias.detach() if src.k_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.v_proj.bias.detach() if src.v_proj.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                    w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.o_proj.bias.detach() if src.o_proj.bias is not None else None

            elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
                w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                    w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

            if w_q is None or w_k is None or w_v is None:
                try:
                    adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                    if w_q is None:
                        w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "q")
                    if w_k is None:
                        w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "k")
                    if w_v is None:
                        w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "v")
                    if w_o is None:
                        w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "o")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

            missing = []
            if w_q is None:
                missing.append("Q")
            if w_k is None:
                missing.append("K")
            if w_v is None:
                missing.append("V")
            if w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
                missing.append("O")
            if missing:
                logger.warning(
                    "TPAttention: failed to extract %s from %s",
                    missing, type(old_attn).__name__,
                )
                return

            # ---- Factorize Q and K (or copy directly if not tensor product) ----
            if self._use_tensor_product:
                self._factorize_qk_tensor_product(w_q, w_k)
            else:
                self._copy_qk_standard(w_q, w_k)

            # ---- Copy V and O directly ----
            self._copy_layer(self.v_proj, w_v, b_v)
            if w_o is not None:
                self._copy_layer(self.out_proj, w_o, b_o)

            if hasattr(old_attn, "is_decoder"):
                self.is_decoder = bool(old_attn.is_decoder)
            elif hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn") or any(
                x in old_attn.__class__.__name__.lower()
                for x in ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "decoder")
            ):
                self.is_decoder = True

    def _factorize_qk_tensor_product(self, w_q: torch.Tensor, w_k: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        """Factorize Q and K using CP decomposition with SVD fallback."""
        for W, proj1, proj2, num_h, name in [
            (w_q, self.q_proj1, self.q_proj2, self.num_heads, "Q"),
            (w_k, self.k_proj1, self.k_proj2, self.num_kv_heads, "K"),
        ]:
            W_f = W.float()
            in_dim = W_f.shape[1]

            try:
                A, B, err = _cp_decompose_qk(
                    W_f, num_h, self.rank_a, self.rank_b,
                    n_iter=self.cp_iterations,
                )
                logger.info(
                    "TPAttention[%s]: CP error = %.4f (rank_a=%d, rank_b=%d)",
                    name, err, self.rank_a, self.rank_b,
                )

                if err <= self.max_cp_error and not (torch.isnan(A).any() or torch.isnan(B).any()):
                    # Accept CP decomposition
                    proj1.weight.data.zero_()
                    proj2.weight.data.zero_()
                    r_a_rows = num_h * self.rank_a
                    r_b_rows = num_h * self.rank_b
                    proj1.weight.data[:r_a_rows, :in_dim].copy_(
                        A[:r_a_rows, :in_dim].to(proj1.weight.dtype)
                    )
                    proj2.weight.data[:r_b_rows, :in_dim].copy_(
                        B[:r_b_rows, :in_dim].to(proj2.weight.dtype)
                    )
                    logger.info("TPAttention[%s]: CP accepted", name)
                else:
                    # Fallback to SVD
                    logger.warning(
                        "TPAttention[%s]: CP error %.4f > %.4f; using SVD fallback",
                        name, err, self.max_cp_error,
                    )
                    self._svd_fallback(proj1, proj2, W_f, num_h, name)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("TPAttention[%s]: CP failed (%s); using SVD fallback", name, e)
                self._svd_fallback(proj1, proj2, W_f, num_h, name)

            proj1.weight.requires_grad = False
            proj2.weight.requires_grad = False

    def _svd_fallback(self, proj1, proj2, W_f, num_h, name):
        """
        SVD fallback: since we can't represent arbitrary SVD factors as tensor
        product, we simply store the SVD factors and disable tensor-product mode.
        """
        A, B = _svd_factorize(W_f, self.rank_a * self.rank_b)
        in_dim = W_f.shape[1]

        # Store A in proj1, B in proj2 (truncated to their row counts)
        r_a_rows = num_h * self.rank_a
        r_b_rows = num_h * self.rank_b

        proj1.weight.data.zero_()
        proj2.weight.data.zero_()

        # Distribute A and B across proj1/proj2 rows
        a_eff = min(A.shape[0], r_a_rows)
        b_eff = min(B.shape[0], r_b_rows)

        proj1.weight.data[:a_eff, :in_dim].copy_(
            A[:a_eff, :in_dim].to(proj1.weight.dtype)
        )
        proj2.weight.data[:b_eff, :in_dim].copy_(
            B[:b_eff, :in_dim].to(proj2.weight.dtype)
        )

        logger.info("TPAttention[%s]: SVD fallback applied", name)

    def _copy_qk_standard(self, w_q: torch.Tensor, w_k: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        """Copy Q and K directly (no tensor product)."""
        self._copy_layer(self.q_proj1, w_q, None)
        self._copy_layer(self.k_proj1, w_k, None)

    def _copy_layer(self, target, w, b):
        if w is None:
            return
        if w.shape == target.weight.shape:
            target.weight.copy_(w.to(target.weight.dtype))
        elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target.weight.shape:
            target.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous().to(target.weight.dtype))
        else:
            m0 = min(target.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
            m1 = min(target.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
            target.weight.zero_()
            target.weight[:m0, :m1].copy_(w[:m0, :m1].to(target.weight.dtype))
        if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
            mb = min(target.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
            target.bias.zero_()
            target.bias[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.dtype))
        elif target.bias is not None:
            target.bias.zero_()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward (with KV cache support)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, H_kv = self.num_heads, self.num_kv_heads
        r_a, r_b = self.rank_a, self.rank_b
        hd = self.head_dim

        # ---- Q, K via tensor product (or standard) ----
        def _compute_q(x):
            if self._use_tensor_product:
                q1 = self.q_proj1(x).view(B, L, H, r_a)
                q2 = self.q_proj2(x).view(B, L, H, r_b)
                out_q = (q1.unsqueeze(-1) * q2.unsqueeze(-2)).flatten(-2, -1)
                if self.q_lora_A is not None:
                    lora = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.q_lora_A), self.q_lora_B)
                    out_q = out_q + lora.view(B, L, H, hd) * (
                        self.lora_alpha / max(1, self.lora_rank)
                    )
                return out_q
            else:
                return self.q_proj1(x).view(B, L, H, hd)

        def _compute_k(x):
            if self._use_tensor_product:
                k1 = self.k_proj1(x).view(B, L, H_kv, r_a)
                k2 = self.k_proj2(x).view(B, L, H_kv, r_b)
                out_k = (k1.unsqueeze(-1) * k2.unsqueeze(-2)).flatten(-2, -1)
                if self.k_lora_A is not None:
                    lora = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.k_lora_A), self.k_lora_B)
                    out_k = out_k + lora.view(B, L, H_kv, hd) * (
                        self.lora_alpha / max(1, self.lora_rank)
                    )
                return out_k
            else:
                return self.k_proj1(x).view(B, L, H_kv, hd)

        use_ckpt = self.training and self.gradient_checkpointing
        if use_ckpt:
            try:
                from torch.utils.checkpoint import checkpoint
                Q = checkpoint(_compute_q, hidden_states, use_reentrant=False)
                K = checkpoint(_compute_k, hidden_states, use_reentrant=False)
            except Exception:
                Q = _compute_q(hidden_states)
                K = _compute_k(hidden_states)
        else:
            Q = _compute_q(hidden_states)
            K = _compute_k(hidden_states)

        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H_kv, hd)

        Q = Q.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()  # (B, H, L, hd)
        K = K.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = v.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # ---- RoPE ----
        pos_emb = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) == 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        # ---- KV Cache ----
        if past_key_value is not None:
            past_k, past_v = past_key_value
            K = torch.cat([past_k, K], dim=2)
            v = torch.cat([past_v, v], dim=2)

        new_cache = (K, v) if kwargs.get("use_cache", False) else None

        # ---- GQA ----
        if H_kv != H:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.num_kv_groups)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.num_kv_groups)

        # ---- Mask handling ----
        is_causal = self.is_decoder and L > 1
        custom_mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("attn_mask")

        if custom_mask is not None:
            if custom_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                if custom_mask.all():
                    custom_mask = None
                elif custom_mask.dim() == 2:
                    custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                elif custom_mask.dim() == 3:
                    custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1)
            elif torch.is_floating_point(custom_mask):
                if (custom_mask == 0.0).all():
                    custom_mask = None
                else:
                    if custom_mask.dim() == 2:
                        custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                    elif custom_mask.dim() == 3:
                        custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1)
                    custom_mask = custom_mask > -1.0

        eff_hd = self.effective_qk_dim
        N_k = K.shape[-2]

        if not output_attentions and head_mask is None:
            # SDPA path
            sdpa_mask = custom_mask
            if is_causal and sdpa_mask is None and past_key_value is None:
                sdpa_is_causal = True
            else:
                if is_causal and sdpa_mask is not None:
                    causal_bool = torch.tril(
                        torch.ones(L, N_k, device=Q.device, dtype=torch.bool)
                    ).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                    cm = sdpa_mask
                    if cm.dim() == 2:
                        cm = cm.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                    elif cm.dim() == 3:
                        cm = cm.unsqueeze(1)
                    sdpa_mask = cm & causal_bool
                elif is_causal and past_key_value is not None:
                    # Build causal mask for the combined sequence
                    q_pos = torch.arange(N_k - L, N_k, device=Q.device).unsqueeze(1)
                    k_pos = torch.arange(N_k, device=Q.device).unsqueeze(0)
                    causal_bool = (k_pos <= q_pos).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                    sdpa_mask = causal_bool
                sdpa_is_causal = False

            try:
                if _HAS_SDPA_KERNEL:
                    with sdpa_kernel([SDPBackend.FLASH_ATTENTION, SDPBackend.EFFICIENT_ATTENTION]):
                        context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                            query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                            attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                            dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                            is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
                        )
                else:
                    context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                        query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                        attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                        dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                        is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
                    )
            except (RuntimeError, TypeError):
                context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                    attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                    is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
                )
            attn_weights = None
        else:
            # Manual path
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(eff_hd)
            scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-1, -2).contiguous()) * scale

            if is_causal:
                q_pos = torch.arange(N_k - L, N_k, device=scores.device).unsqueeze(1)
                k_pos = torch.arange(N_k, device=scores.device).unsqueeze(0)
                causal_mask = (k_pos > q_pos).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                scores = scores.masked_fill(causal_mask, float("-inf"))

            if custom_mask is not None:
                if custom_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    scores = scores.masked_fill(~custom_mask, -1e4)
                else:
                    scores = scores + custom_mask.to(scores.dtype)

            attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(v.dtype)
            attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0)
            attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), head_mask.size(1), 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
                attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.to(attn_weights.dtype)

            context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)

        # ---- Output projection ----
        ctx_flat = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.q_head_size)
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = ctx_flat
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(ctx_flat)

        valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, hidden_states.device)
        if valid_m is not None:
            output = output * valid_m.unsqueeze(-1).to(output.dtype)

        if return_dict and new_cache is not None:
            return output, new_cache
        return output, (attn_weights if output_attentions else None)

Tensor Product Attention v2 with asymmetric rank support.

Key improvements: - rank_a × rank_b = head_dim (supports head_dim=64 AND head_dim=128) - Better CP decomposition (50 iterations with early stopping) - Stricter max_cp_error (0.05) - KV cache support for autoregressive inference

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
    with torch.no_grad():
        w_q = w_k = w_v = w_o = None
        b_q = b_k = b_v = b_o = None

        src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
        has_no_out = not any(
            hasattr(old_attn, a)
            for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj", "c_proj", "out_lin", "proj")
        )
        if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
            hasattr(old_attn, "query") and has_no_out
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True

        if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
            src = old_attn.self
            if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                w_o = old_attn.output.dense.weight.detach()
                b_o = (
                    old_attn.output.dense.bias.detach()
                    if old_attn.output.dense.bias is not None else None
                )
        else:
            src = old_attn

        # Extract weights from various architectures
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn"):
            W = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
            B_vec = src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None
            if W.shape[0] != 3 * self.dim and W.shape[1] == 3 * self.dim:
                W = W.t().contiguous()
            if W.shape[0] == 3 * self.dim:
                w_q, w_k, w_v = W.chunk(3, dim=0)
                if B_vec is not None:
                    b_q, b_k, b_v = B_vec.chunk(3, dim=0)
            if hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                w_o = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
                if w_o.shape[0] != self.dim and w_o.shape[1] == self.dim:
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

        elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
            w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
            b_q = src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
            b_k = src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
            b_v = src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None
            if w_o is None:
                for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                    if hasattr(src, a):
                        proj = getattr(src, a)
                        w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                        b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                        break

        elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
            w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
            b_q = src.q_proj.bias.detach() if src.q_proj.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
            b_k = src.k_proj.bias.detach() if src.k_proj.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
            b_v = src.v_proj.bias.detach() if src.v_proj.bias is not None else None
            if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                b_o = src.o_proj.bias.detach() if src.o_proj.bias is not None else None

        elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
            w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
            b_q = src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
            b_k = src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
            b_v = src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None
            if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

        if w_q is None or w_k is None or w_v is None:
            try:
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                if w_q is None:
                    w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "q")
                if w_k is None:
                    w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "k")
                if w_v is None:
                    w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "v")
                if w_o is None:
                    w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "o")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing = []
        if w_q is None:
            missing.append("Q")
        if w_k is None:
            missing.append("K")
        if w_v is None:
            missing.append("V")
        if w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
            missing.append("O")
        if missing:
            logger.warning(
                "TPAttention: failed to extract %s from %s",
                missing, type(old_attn).__name__,
            )
            return

        # ---- Factorize Q and K (or copy directly if not tensor product) ----
        if self._use_tensor_product:
            self._factorize_qk_tensor_product(w_q, w_k)
        else:
            self._copy_qk_standard(w_q, w_k)

        # ---- Copy V and O directly ----
        self._copy_layer(self.v_proj, w_v, b_v)
        if w_o is not None:
            self._copy_layer(self.out_proj, w_o, b_o)

        if hasattr(old_attn, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(old_attn.is_decoder)
        elif hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn") or any(
            x in old_attn.__class__.__name__.lower()
            for x in ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "decoder")
        ):
            self.is_decoder = True

Universal fallback: copy pretrained weights from old attention layer.

Expand source code 
def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool | None = False,
    output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
    return_dict: bool | None = True,
    *args,
    **kwargs,
):
    B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
    H, H_kv = self.num_heads, self.num_kv_heads
    r_a, r_b = self.rank_a, self.rank_b
    hd = self.head_dim

    # ---- Q, K via tensor product (or standard) ----
    def _compute_q(x):
        if self._use_tensor_product:
            q1 = self.q_proj1(x).view(B, L, H, r_a)
            q2 = self.q_proj2(x).view(B, L, H, r_b)
            out_q = (q1.unsqueeze(-1) * q2.unsqueeze(-2)).flatten(-2, -1)
            if self.q_lora_A is not None:
                lora = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.q_lora_A), self.q_lora_B)
                out_q = out_q + lora.view(B, L, H, hd) * (
                    self.lora_alpha / max(1, self.lora_rank)
                )
            return out_q
        else:
            return self.q_proj1(x).view(B, L, H, hd)

    def _compute_k(x):
        if self._use_tensor_product:
            k1 = self.k_proj1(x).view(B, L, H_kv, r_a)
            k2 = self.k_proj2(x).view(B, L, H_kv, r_b)
            out_k = (k1.unsqueeze(-1) * k2.unsqueeze(-2)).flatten(-2, -1)
            if self.k_lora_A is not None:
                lora = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.k_lora_A), self.k_lora_B)
                out_k = out_k + lora.view(B, L, H_kv, hd) * (
                    self.lora_alpha / max(1, self.lora_rank)
                )
            return out_k
        else:
            return self.k_proj1(x).view(B, L, H_kv, hd)

    use_ckpt = self.training and self.gradient_checkpointing
    if use_ckpt:
        try:
            from torch.utils.checkpoint import checkpoint
            Q = checkpoint(_compute_q, hidden_states, use_reentrant=False)
            K = checkpoint(_compute_k, hidden_states, use_reentrant=False)
        except Exception:
            Q = _compute_q(hidden_states)
            K = _compute_k(hidden_states)
    else:
        Q = _compute_q(hidden_states)
        K = _compute_k(hidden_states)

    v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H_kv, hd)

    Q = Q.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()  # (B, H, L, hd)
    K = K.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
    v = v.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

    # ---- RoPE ----
    pos_emb = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
    if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) == 2:
        cos, sin = pos_emb
        Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

    # ---- KV Cache ----
    if past_key_value is not None:
        past_k, past_v = past_key_value
        K = torch.cat([past_k, K], dim=2)
        v = torch.cat([past_v, v], dim=2)

    new_cache = (K, v) if kwargs.get("use_cache", False) else None

    # ---- GQA ----
    if H_kv != H:
        K = repeat_kv(K, self.num_kv_groups)
        v = repeat_kv(v, self.num_kv_groups)

    # ---- Mask handling ----
    is_causal = self.is_decoder and L > 1
    custom_mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("attn_mask")

    if custom_mask is not None:
        if custom_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
            if custom_mask.all():
                custom_mask = None
            elif custom_mask.dim() == 2:
                custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
            elif custom_mask.dim() == 3:
                custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1)
        elif torch.is_floating_point(custom_mask):
            if (custom_mask == 0.0).all():
                custom_mask = None
            else:
                if custom_mask.dim() == 2:
                    custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                elif custom_mask.dim() == 3:
                    custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1)
                custom_mask = custom_mask > -1.0

    eff_hd = self.effective_qk_dim
    N_k = K.shape[-2]

    if not output_attentions and head_mask is None:
        # SDPA path
        sdpa_mask = custom_mask
        if is_causal and sdpa_mask is None and past_key_value is None:
            sdpa_is_causal = True
        else:
            if is_causal and sdpa_mask is not None:
                causal_bool = torch.tril(
                    torch.ones(L, N_k, device=Q.device, dtype=torch.bool)
                ).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                cm = sdpa_mask
                if cm.dim() == 2:
                    cm = cm.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                elif cm.dim() == 3:
                    cm = cm.unsqueeze(1)
                sdpa_mask = cm & causal_bool
            elif is_causal and past_key_value is not None:
                # Build causal mask for the combined sequence
                q_pos = torch.arange(N_k - L, N_k, device=Q.device).unsqueeze(1)
                k_pos = torch.arange(N_k, device=Q.device).unsqueeze(0)
                causal_bool = (k_pos <= q_pos).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                sdpa_mask = causal_bool
            sdpa_is_causal = False

        try:
            if _HAS_SDPA_KERNEL:
                with sdpa_kernel([SDPBackend.FLASH_ATTENTION, SDPBackend.EFFICIENT_ATTENTION]):
                    context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                        query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                        attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                        dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                        is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
                    )
            else:
                context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                    attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                    is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
                )
        except (RuntimeError, TypeError):
            context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
            )
        attn_weights = None
    else:
        # Manual path
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(eff_hd)
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-1, -2).contiguous()) * scale

        if is_causal:
            q_pos = torch.arange(N_k - L, N_k, device=scores.device).unsqueeze(1)
            k_pos = torch.arange(N_k, device=scores.device).unsqueeze(0)
            causal_mask = (k_pos > q_pos).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
            scores = scores.masked_fill(causal_mask, float("-inf"))

        if custom_mask is not None:
            if custom_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~custom_mask, -1e4)
            else:
                scores = scores + custom_mask.to(scores.dtype)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(v.dtype)
        attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), head_mask.size(1), 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.to(attn_weights.dtype)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)

    # ---- Output projection ----
    ctx_flat = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.q_head_size)
    if self.is_nested_encoder:
        output = ctx_flat
    else:
        output = self.out_proj(ctx_flat)

    valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, hidden_states.device)
    if valid_m is not None:
        output = output * valid_m.unsqueeze(-1).to(output.dtype)

    if return_dict and new_cache is not None:
        return output, new_cache
    return output, (attn_weights if output_attentions else None)

Must return (output, attention_weights_or_None).
