@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("tpa_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class TPAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Tensor Product Attention v2 with asymmetric rank support.

    Key improvements:
      - rank_a × rank_b = head_dim (supports head_dim=64 AND head_dim=128)
      - Better CP decomposition (50 iterations with early stopping)
      - Stricter max_cp_error (0.05)
      - KV cache support for autoregressive inference
    """

    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_causal: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank_a: int = 8,
        rank_b: int = 8,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        max_cp_error: float = 0.05,
        cp_iterations: int = 50,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank_a)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        src = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("old_attn")
        self.max_cp_error = float(max_cp_error)
        self.cp_iterations = int(cp_iterations)

        # ---- hidden size ----
        dim = None
        if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            dim = _cfg_get(
                config,
                "hidden_size",
                _cfg_get(config, "dim", _cfg_get(config, "d_model", None)),
            )
        if dim is None and src is not None:
            dim = getattr(src, "hidden_size", getattr(src, "embed_dim", None))
            if dim is None:
                for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"):
                    m = getattr(src, attr, None)
                    if m is not None and hasattr(m, "in_features"):
                        dim = m.in_features
                        break
            if dim is None and hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src.c_attn, "weight"):
                w = src.c_attn.weight
                dim = w.shape[0] if w.shape[0] != 3 * w.shape[1] else w.shape[1]
        if dim is None:
            dim = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)

        # ---- heads ----
        num_heads = None
        if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            num_heads = _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(config, "n_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_heads", None)),
            )
        if num_heads is None and src is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(
                src,
                "num_heads",
                getattr(src, "num_attention_heads", getattr(src, "n_head", None)),
            )
        if num_heads is None:
            num_heads = 12

        self.dim = int(dim)
        self.num_heads = max(1, int(num_heads))

        # ---- head_dim ----
        cfg_hd = _cfg_get(config, "head_dim", None) if config is not None else None
        kw_hd = kwargs.get("head_dim")
        if cfg_hd is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(cfg_hd)
        elif kw_hd is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(kw_hd)
        elif src is not None and getattr(src, "head_dim", None) is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(src.head_dim)
        else:
            self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads

        # ---- GQA ----
        cfg_kv = (
            _cfg_get(config, "num_key_value_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
            if config is not None
            else None
        )
        if cfg_kv is None and src is not None:
            cfg_kv = getattr(src, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(src, "num_kv_heads", None))
        self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv) if cfg_kv is not None else self.num_heads
        self.num_kv_groups = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.q_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        # ---- Asymmetric rank support ----
        # Try to find (rank_a, rank_b) such that rank_a * rank_b == head_dim
        raw_ra = kwargs.pop("rank_a", rank_a)
        raw_rb = kwargs.pop("rank_b", rank_b)
        self.rank_a = int(raw_ra)
        self.rank_b = int(raw_rb)

        if self.rank_a * self.rank_b != self.head_dim:
            # Try to auto-adjust rank_b to match head_dim
            if self.head_dim % self.rank_a == 0:
                self.rank_b = self.head_dim // self.rank_a
                logger.info(
                    "Auto-adjusted rank_b to %d to match head_dim=%d",
                    self.rank_b, self.head_dim,
                )
            elif self.head_dim % self.rank_b == 0:
                self.rank_a = self.head_dim // self.rank_b
                logger.info(
                    "Auto-adjusted rank_a to %d to match head_dim=%d",
                    self.rank_a, self.head_dim,
                )
            else:
                self._use_tensor_product = False
                logger.warning(
                    "Cannot find (rank_a, rank_b) with rank_a*rank_b=%d. "
                    "Falling back to standard projection.",
                    self.head_dim,
                )
        else:
            self._use_tensor_product = True

        if self._use_tensor_product:
            self.rank_a = int(self.rank_a)
            self.rank_b = int(self.rank_b)
        else:
            # Standard projection mode
            self.rank_a = self.head_dim
            self.rank_b = 1

        self.effective_qk_dim = self.head_dim

        self.lora_rank = int(kwargs.get("lora_rank", _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", lora_rank)))
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            kwargs.get("lora_alpha", _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))
        )

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        _mt = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if config is not None else ""
        _dec_prefixes = (
            "gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi",
            "opt", "bloom", "falcon", "olmo", "mpt", "stablelm",
        )
        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        elif config is not None and (
            _cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", False)
            or _cfg_get(config, "is_causal", False)
            or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _dec_prefixes)
        ):
            self.is_decoder = True
        else:
            self.is_decoder = bool(kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))

        self.is_nested_encoder = False
        self.gradient_checkpointing = bool(kwargs.get("gradient_checkpointing", False))

        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and src is not None:
            p = next(src.parameters(), None)
            if p is not None:
                target_dtype = p.dtype

        # ---- projections (tensor product or standard) ----
        if self._use_tensor_product:
            # Tensor product: two factor projections
            self.q_proj1 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.num_heads * self.rank_a,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.q_proj2 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.num_heads * self.rank_b,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.k_proj1 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.num_kv_heads * self.rank_a,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.k_proj2 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.num_kv_heads * self.rank_b,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
        else:
            # Standard projection: single factor
            self.q_proj1 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.q_head_size,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.q_proj2 = None
            self.k_proj1 = LoRALinear(
                self.dim, self.kv_head_size,
                bias=False, lora_rank=0, freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
            )
            self.k_proj2 = None

        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.q_head_size, self.dim,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, dtype=target_dtype,
        )

        # ---- LoRA on Q/K (optional) ----
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self._use_tensor_product:
            self.q_lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.dim, dtype=target_dtype)
            )
            self.q_lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros(self.q_head_size, self.lora_rank, dtype=target_dtype)
            )
            self.k_lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.dim, dtype=target_dtype)
            )
            self.k_lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros(self.kv_head_size, self.lora_rank, dtype=target_dtype)
            )
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.q_lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.k_lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("q_lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("q_lora_B", None)
            self.register_parameter("k_lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("k_lora_B", None)

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

        if target_dtype is not None:
            for proj in [self.q_proj1, self.k_proj1, self.v_proj, self.out_proj]:
                if proj is not None:
                    proj.to(target_dtype)
            for proj in [self.q_proj2, self.k_proj2]:
                if proj is not None:
                    proj.to(target_dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight transfer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q = w_k = w_v = w_o = None
            b_q = b_k = b_v = b_o = None

            src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
            has_no_out = not any(
                hasattr(old_attn, a)
                for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj", "c_proj", "out_lin", "proj")
            )
            if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
                hasattr(old_attn, "query") and has_no_out
            ):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True

            if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True
                src = old_attn.self
                if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                    w_o = old_attn.output.dense.weight.detach()
                    b_o = (
                        old_attn.output.dense.bias.detach()
                        if old_attn.output.dense.bias is not None else None
                    )
            else:
                src = old_attn

            # Extract weights from various architectures
            if hasattr(src, "c_attn"):
                W = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
                B_vec = src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None
                if W.shape[0] != 3 * self.dim and W.shape[1] == 3 * self.dim:
                    W = W.t().contiguous()
                if W.shape[0] == 3 * self.dim:
                    w_q, w_k, w_v = W.chunk(3, dim=0)
                    if B_vec is not None:
                        b_q, b_k, b_v = B_vec.chunk(3, dim=0)
                if hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                    w_o = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
                    if w_o.shape[0] != self.dim and w_o.shape[1] == self.dim:
                        w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                    b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

            elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
                w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None
                if w_o is None:
                    for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                        if hasattr(src, a):
                            proj = getattr(src, a)
                            w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                            b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                            break

            elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
                w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.q_proj.bias.detach() if src.q_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.k_proj.bias.detach() if src.k_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.v_proj.bias.detach() if src.v_proj.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                    w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.o_proj.bias.detach() if src.o_proj.bias is not None else None

            elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
                w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                    w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

            if w_q is None or w_k is None or w_v is None:
                try:
                    adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                    if w_q is None:
                        w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "q")
                    if w_k is None:
                        w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "k")
                    if w_v is None:
                        w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "v")
                    if w_o is None:
                        w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "o")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

            missing = []
            if w_q is None:
                missing.append("Q")
            if w_k is None:
                missing.append("K")
            if w_v is None:
                missing.append("V")
            if w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
                missing.append("O")
            if missing:
                logger.warning(
                    "TPAttention: failed to extract %s from %s",
                    missing, type(old_attn).__name__,
                )
                return

            # ---- Factorize Q and K (or copy directly if not tensor product) ----
            if self._use_tensor_product:
                self._factorize_qk_tensor_product(w_q, w_k)
            else:
                self._copy_qk_standard(w_q, w_k)

            # ---- Copy V and O directly ----
            self._copy_layer(self.v_proj, w_v, b_v)
            if w_o is not None:
                self._copy_layer(self.out_proj, w_o, b_o)

            if hasattr(old_attn, "is_decoder"):
                self.is_decoder = bool(old_attn.is_decoder)
            elif hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn") or any(
                x in old_attn.__class__.__name__.lower()
                for x in ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "decoder")
            ):
                self.is_decoder = True

    def _factorize_qk_tensor_product(self, w_q: torch.Tensor, w_k: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        """Factorize Q and K using CP decomposition with SVD fallback."""
        for W, proj1, proj2, num_h, name in [
            (w_q, self.q_proj1, self.q_proj2, self.num_heads, "Q"),
            (w_k, self.k_proj1, self.k_proj2, self.num_kv_heads, "K"),
        ]:
            W_f = W.float()
            in_dim = W_f.shape[1]

            try:
                A, B, err = _cp_decompose_qk(
                    W_f, num_h, self.rank_a, self.rank_b,
                    n_iter=self.cp_iterations,
                )
                logger.info(
                    "TPAttention[%s]: CP error = %.4f (rank_a=%d, rank_b=%d)",
                    name, err, self.rank_a, self.rank_b,
                )

                if err <= self.max_cp_error and not (torch.isnan(A).any() or torch.isnan(B).any()):
                    # Accept CP decomposition
                    proj1.weight.data.zero_()
                    proj2.weight.data.zero_()
                    r_a_rows = num_h * self.rank_a
                    r_b_rows = num_h * self.rank_b
                    proj1.weight.data[:r_a_rows, :in_dim].copy_(
                        A[:r_a_rows, :in_dim].to(proj1.weight.dtype)
                    )
                    proj2.weight.data[:r_b_rows, :in_dim].copy_(
                        B[:r_b_rows, :in_dim].to(proj2.weight.dtype)
                    )
                    logger.info("TPAttention[%s]: CP accepted", name)
                else:
                    # Fallback to SVD
                    logger.warning(
                        "TPAttention[%s]: CP error %.4f > %.4f; using SVD fallback",
                        name, err, self.max_cp_error,
                    )
                    self._svd_fallback(proj1, proj2, W_f, num_h, name)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("TPAttention[%s]: CP failed (%s); using SVD fallback", name, e)
                self._svd_fallback(proj1, proj2, W_f, num_h, name)

            proj1.weight.requires_grad = False
            proj2.weight.requires_grad = False

    def _svd_fallback(self, proj1, proj2, W_f, num_h, name):
        """
        SVD fallback: since we can't represent arbitrary SVD factors as tensor
        product, we simply store the SVD factors and disable tensor-product mode.
        """
        A, B = _svd_factorize(W_f, self.rank_a * self.rank_b)
        in_dim = W_f.shape[1]

        # Store A in proj1, B in proj2 (truncated to their row counts)
        r_a_rows = num_h * self.rank_a
        r_b_rows = num_h * self.rank_b

        proj1.weight.data.zero_()
        proj2.weight.data.zero_()

        # Distribute A and B across proj1/proj2 rows
        a_eff = min(A.shape[0], r_a_rows)
        b_eff = min(B.shape[0], r_b_rows)

        proj1.weight.data[:a_eff, :in_dim].copy_(
            A[:a_eff, :in_dim].to(proj1.weight.dtype)
        )
        proj2.weight.data[:b_eff, :in_dim].copy_(
            B[:b_eff, :in_dim].to(proj2.weight.dtype)
        )

        logger.info("TPAttention[%s]: SVD fallback applied", name)

    def _copy_qk_standard(self, w_q: torch.Tensor, w_k: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        """Copy Q and K directly (no tensor product)."""
        self._copy_layer(self.q_proj1, w_q, None)
        self._copy_layer(self.k_proj1, w_k, None)

    def _copy_layer(self, target, w, b):
        if w is None:
            return
        if w.shape == target.weight.shape:
            target.weight.copy_(w.to(target.weight.dtype))
        elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target.weight.shape:
            target.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous().to(target.weight.dtype))
        else:
            m0 = min(target.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
            m1 = min(target.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
            target.weight.zero_()
            target.weight[:m0, :m1].copy_(w[:m0, :m1].to(target.weight.dtype))
        if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
            mb = min(target.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
            target.bias.zero_()
            target.bias[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.dtype))
        elif target.bias is not None:
            target.bias.zero_()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward (with KV cache support)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, H_kv = self.num_heads, self.num_kv_heads
        r_a, r_b = self.rank_a, self.rank_b
        hd = self.head_dim

        # ---- Q, K via tensor product (or standard) ----
        def _compute_q(x):
            if self._use_tensor_product:
                q1 = self.q_proj1(x).view(B, L, H, r_a)
                q2 = self.q_proj2(x).view(B, L, H, r_b)
                out_q = (q1.unsqueeze(-1) * q2.unsqueeze(-2)).flatten(-2, -1)
                if self.q_lora_A is not None:
                    lora = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.q_lora_A), self.q_lora_B)
                    out_q = out_q + lora.view(B, L, H, hd) * (
                        self.lora_alpha / max(1, self.lora_rank)
                    )
                return out_q
            else:
                return self.q_proj1(x).view(B, L, H, hd)

        def _compute_k(x):
            if self._use_tensor_product:
                k1 = self.k_proj1(x).view(B, L, H_kv, r_a)
                k2 = self.k_proj2(x).view(B, L, H_kv, r_b)
                out_k = (k1.unsqueeze(-1) * k2.unsqueeze(-2)).flatten(-2, -1)
                if self.k_lora_A is not None:
                    lora = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.k_lora_A), self.k_lora_B)
                    out_k = out_k + lora.view(B, L, H_kv, hd) * (
                        self.lora_alpha / max(1, self.lora_rank)
                    )
                return out_k
            else:
                return self.k_proj1(x).view(B, L, H_kv, hd)

        use_ckpt = self.training and self.gradient_checkpointing
        if use_ckpt:
            try:
                from torch.utils.checkpoint import checkpoint
                Q = checkpoint(_compute_q, hidden_states, use_reentrant=False)
                K = checkpoint(_compute_k, hidden_states, use_reentrant=False)
            except Exception:
                Q = _compute_q(hidden_states)
                K = _compute_k(hidden_states)
        else:
            Q = _compute_q(hidden_states)
            K = _compute_k(hidden_states)

        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H_kv, hd)

        Q = Q.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()  # (B, H, L, hd)
        K = K.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = v.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # ---- RoPE ----
        pos_emb = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) == 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        # ---- KV Cache ----
        if past_key_value is not None:
            past_k, past_v = past_key_value
            K = torch.cat([past_k, K], dim=2)
            v = torch.cat([past_v, v], dim=2)

        new_cache = (K, v) if kwargs.get("use_cache", False) else None

        # ---- GQA ----
        if H_kv != H:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.num_kv_groups)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.num_kv_groups)

        # ---- Mask handling ----
        is_causal = self.is_decoder and L > 1
        custom_mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("attn_mask")

        if custom_mask is not None:
            if custom_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                if custom_mask.all():
                    custom_mask = None
                elif custom_mask.dim() == 2:
                    custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                elif custom_mask.dim() == 3:
                    custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1)
            elif torch.is_floating_point(custom_mask):
                if (custom_mask == 0.0).all():
                    custom_mask = None
                else:
                    if custom_mask.dim() == 2:
                        custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                    elif custom_mask.dim() == 3:
                        custom_mask = custom_mask.unsqueeze(1)
                    custom_mask = custom_mask > -1.0

        eff_hd = self.effective_qk_dim
        N_k = K.shape[-2]

        if not output_attentions and head_mask is None:
            # SDPA path
            sdpa_mask = custom_mask
            if is_causal and sdpa_mask is None and past_key_value is None:
                sdpa_is_causal = True
            else:
                if is_causal and sdpa_mask is not None:
                    causal_bool = torch.tril(
                        torch.ones(L, N_k, device=Q.device, dtype=torch.bool)
                    ).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                    cm = sdpa_mask
                    if cm.dim() == 2:
                        cm = cm.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                    elif cm.dim() == 3:
                        cm = cm.unsqueeze(1)
                    sdpa_mask = cm & causal_bool
                elif is_causal and past_key_value is not None:
                    # Build causal mask for the combined sequence
                    q_pos = torch.arange(N_k - L, N_k, device=Q.device).unsqueeze(1)
                    k_pos = torch.arange(N_k, device=Q.device).unsqueeze(0)
                    causal_bool = (k_pos <= q_pos).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                    sdpa_mask = causal_bool
                sdpa_is_causal = False

            try:
                if _HAS_SDPA_KERNEL:
                    with sdpa_kernel([SDPBackend.FLASH_ATTENTION, SDPBackend.EFFICIENT_ATTENTION]):
                        context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                            query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                            attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                            dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                            is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
                        )
                else:
                    context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                        query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                        attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                        dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                        is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
                    )
            except (RuntimeError, TypeError):
                context = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    query=Q, key=K, value=v,
                    attn_mask=sdpa_mask,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                    is_causal=sdpa_is_causal,
                )
            attn_weights = None
        else:
            # Manual path
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(eff_hd)
            scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-1, -2).contiguous()) * scale

            if is_causal:
                q_pos = torch.arange(N_k - L, N_k, device=scores.device).unsqueeze(1)
                k_pos = torch.arange(N_k, device=scores.device).unsqueeze(0)
                causal_mask = (k_pos > q_pos).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                scores = scores.masked_fill(causal_mask, float("-inf"))

            if custom_mask is not None:
                if custom_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    scores = scores.masked_fill(~custom_mask, -1e4)
                else:
                    scores = scores + custom_mask.to(scores.dtype)

            attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(v.dtype)
            attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0)
            attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), head_mask.size(1), 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
                attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.to(attn_weights.dtype)

            context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)

        # ---- Output projection ----
        ctx_flat = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.q_head_size)
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = ctx_flat
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(ctx_flat)

        valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, hidden_states.device)
        if valid_m is not None:
            output = output * valid_m.unsqueeze(-1).to(output.dtype)

        if return_dict and new_cache is not None:
            return output, new_cache
        return output, (attn_weights if output_attentions else None)

Tensor Product Attention v2 with asymmetric rank support.

Key improvements: - rank_a × rank_b = head_dim (supports head_dim=64 AND head_dim=128) - Better CP decomposition (50 iterations with early stopping) - Stricter max_cp_error (0.05) - KV cache support for autoregressive inference

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("triton_cosformer", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True)
class TritonCosformerAttention(BaseAttention):
    r"""
    Triton-Accelerated Cosformer Linear Attention with Full Autograd Differentiability,
    RoPE support, Pretrained Weight Loading, and Masked Attention.
    """

    triton_kernel = "linear_attn"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any = None,
        embed_dim: int | None = None,
        num_heads: int | None = None,
        num_kv_heads: int | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool = False,
        eps: float = 1e-6,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.config = config
        self.is_decoder = bool(
            is_decoder
            or getattr(config, "is_decoder", False)
            or getattr(config, "is_causal", False)
        )
        self.eps = eps
        self.triton_kwargs = {
            "phi": "elu+1",
            "eps": self.eps,
            "cos_weight": None,
            "sin_weight": None,
        }

        self.embed_dim = (
            embed_dim or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or getattr(config, "hidden_size", 768)
        )
        self.num_heads = (
            num_heads
            or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            or getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = (
            num_kv_heads
            or kwargs.get("num_kv_heads")
            or getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads)
        )
        self.head_dim = kwargs.get("head_dim") or (self.embed_dim // self.num_heads)

        # BUG 5 FIX: fixed max_length from config
        self.max_length = getattr(config, "max_position_embeddings", 2048) or 2048

        # Linear projections
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(self.embed_dim, self.embed_dim, bias=True)
        self.kv_dim = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(self.embed_dim, self.kv_dim, bias=True)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(self.embed_dim, self.kv_dim, bias=True)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.embed_dim, self.embed_dim, bias=True)

        for m in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
            nn.init.xavier_uniform_(m.weight)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)

        # BUG 3 FIX: Support pretrained attention weight copying during initialization
        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        """Transfer pretrained projection weights from GPT-2, RoBERTa/BERT, or standard linear layers."""
        if src is None:
            return

        getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__

        # 1. GPT-2: Fused c_attn (Conv1D) + c_proj
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                # If Conv1D or shape is [in, out], transpose to standard PyTorch [out, in]
                if (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or hasattr(c_attn_mod, "nf")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()

                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None

                self.q_proj.weight.data.copy_(c_w[:split_size, :])
                self.k_proj.weight.data.copy_(c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :])
                self.v_proj.weight.data.copy_(c_w[2 * split_size :, :])

                if c_b is not None:
                    if self.q_proj.bias is not None:
                        self.q_proj.bias.data.copy_(c_b[:split_size])
                    if self.k_proj.bias is not None:
                        self.k_proj.bias.data.copy_(c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size])
                    if self.v_proj.bias is not None:
                        self.v_proj.bias.data.copy_(c_b[2 * split_size :])

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    self.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(o_w)
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    if bias_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                        self.out_proj.bias.data.copy_(bias_o.data)

        # 2. RoBERTa / BERT nested self-attention
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            mapping = [("q_proj", "query"), ("k_proj", "key"), ("v_proj", "value")]
            for target_name, sub_name in mapping:
                target_mod = getattr(self, target_name)
                src_mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                base_src = getattr(src_mod, "base_layer", src_mod)
                if base_src is not None and hasattr(base_src, "weight"):
                    target_mod.weight.data.copy_(base_src.weight.data)
                    if base_src.bias is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                        target_mod.bias.data.copy_(base_src.bias.data)

            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                base_out = getattr(out_mod, "base_layer", out_mod)
                self.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(base_out.weight.data)
                if base_out.bias is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    self.out_proj.bias.data.copy_(base_out.bias.data)

        # 3. Standard flat linear projections (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q_proj": ["q_proj", "query", "q_lin", "W_q", "wq"],
                "k_proj": ["k_proj", "key", "k_lin", "W_k", "wk"],
                "v_proj": ["v_proj", "value", "v_lin", "W_v", "wv"],
                "out_proj": ["out_proj", "dense", "out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj", "wo"],
            }
            for target_name, candidates in proj_candidates.items():
                target_mod = getattr(self, target_name)
                for cand in candidates:
                    if hasattr(src, cand):
                        src_mod = getattr(src, cand)
                        base_mod = getattr(src_mod, "base_layer", src_mod)
                        if base_mod is not None and hasattr(base_mod, "weight"):
                            w = base_mod.weight.data
                            if (
                                w.ndim == 2
                                and w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                                and w.t().shape == target_mod.weight.shape
                            ):
                                w = w.t()
                            target_mod.weight.data.copy_(w)
                            if (
                                getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                                and target_mod.bias is not None
                            ):
                                target_mod.bias.data.copy_(base_mod.bias.data)
                            break

    # Alias for API uniformity
    copy_weights_from_pretrained = copy_weights

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        position_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, Any | None]:
        B, N, C = hidden_states.shape

        # RoPE from kwargs if not explicit
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")

        # 1. Project Q, K, V
        q = (
            self.q_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        k = (
            self.k_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.v_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # 2. BUG 4 FIX: Apply Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) if provided
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids=position_ids)

        # 3. GQA expansion if num_kv_heads < num_heads
        if self.num_kv_heads != self.num_heads:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

        # 4. BUG 2 FIX: Mask handling for padding tokens on K and V
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                # Shape (B, N) -> (B, 1, N, 1)
                mask = attention_mask[:, None, :, None].to(q.dtype)
                if (mask < 0).any():
                    mask = (mask > -1000.0).to(q.dtype)
                k = k * mask
                v = v * mask
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                if attention_mask.shape[-2] == 1:
                    mask = attention_mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1)[:, None, :, None].to(q.dtype)
                else:
                    mask = attention_mask[:, 0, -1, :][:, None, :, None].to(q.dtype)
                if (mask < 0).any():
                    mask = (mask > -1000.0).to(q.dtype)
                k = k * mask
                v = v * mask

        # 5. Differentiable forward pass using cosformer's own autograd function.
        # NOTE: We call _TritonCosformerAutogradFunction.apply() directly instead
        # of routing through triton_dispatch → linear_attn.fwd, because:
        #   - linear_attn.fwd early-exits to PyTorch fallback when cos_weight is set
        #   - That path was broken (ctx.save_for_backward not called before return)
        #   - This module has its own complete Triton + PyTorch fallback built in.
        out = _TritonCosformerAutogradFunction.apply(
            q, k, v, self.is_decoder, self.eps, self.max_length
        )

        out = out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, C)
        out = self.out_proj(out)
        return out, None

def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
    """Transfer pretrained projection weights from GPT-2, RoBERTa/BERT, or standard linear layers."""
    if src is None:
        return

    getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__

            self.k_proj.weight.data.copy_(c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :])
            self.v_proj.weight.data.copy_(c_w[2 * split_size :, :])

            if c_b is not None:
                if self.q_proj.bias is not None:
                    self.q_proj.bias.data.copy_(c_b[:split_size])
                if self.k_proj.bias is not None:
                    self.k_proj.bias.data.copy_(c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size])
                if self.v_proj.bias is not None:
                    self.v_proj.bias.data.copy_(c_b[2 * split_size :])

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if o_w is not None:

                    c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"

                    or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                    or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()

                bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                if bias_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    self.out_proj.bias.data.copy_(bias_o.data)

    elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
        s = src.self
        mapping = [("q_proj", "query"), ("k_proj", "key"), ("v_proj", "value")]
        for target_name, sub_name in mapping:
            target_mod = getattr(self, target_name)
            src_mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
            base_src = getattr(src_mod, "base_layer", src_mod)
            if base_src is not None and hasattr(base_src, "weight"):
                target_mod.weight.data.copy_(base_src.weight.data)
                if base_src.bias is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    target_mod.bias.data.copy_(base_src.bias.data)

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
            out_mod = src.output.dense
            base_out = getattr(out_mod, "base_layer", out_mod)
            self.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(base_out.weight.data)
            if base_out.bias is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                self.out_proj.bias.data.copy_(base_out.bias.data)

    # 3. Standard flat linear projections (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
    else:
        proj_candidates = {
            "q_proj": ["q_proj", "query", "q_lin", "W_q", "wq"],
            "k_proj": ["k_proj", "key", "k_lin", "W_k", "wk"],
            "v_proj": ["v_proj", "value", "v_lin", "W_v", "wv"],
            "out_proj": ["out_proj", "dense", "out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj", "wo"],
        }
        for target_name, candidates in proj_candidates.items():
            target_mod = getattr(self, target_name)
            for cand in candidates:
                if hasattr(src, cand):
                    src_mod = getattr(src, cand)
                    base_mod = getattr(src_mod, "base_layer", src_mod)
                    if base_mod is not None and hasattr(base_mod, "weight"):
                        w = base_mod.weight.data
                        if (
                            w.ndim == 2
                            and w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                            and w.t().shape == target_mod.weight.shape
                        ):
                            w = w.t()
                        target_mod.weight.data.copy_(w)
                        if (
                            getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                            and target_mod.bias is not None
                        ):
                            target_mod.bias.data.copy_(base_mod.bias.data)
                        break

Transfer pretrained projection weights from GPT-2, RoBERTa/BERT, or standard linear layers.

class UniversalAdapter
Expand source code 
class UniversalAdapter:
    """Universal adapter supporting BERT, DistilBERT, RoBERTa, LLaMA, GPT2, DeBERTa, etc."""

    @staticmethod
    def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any, proj: str) -> tuple:
        """Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

        Handles:
        - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module
        - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly
        - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj
        - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin
        - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight
        - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]
        """
        target = getattr(old_attn, "self", old_attn)
        candidate_names = CANONICAL_PROJECTION_ALIASES.get(proj, [])

        def _unwrap(mod: Any) -> Any:
            """Unwrap LoRALinear / merged adapter layers to their base_layer."""
            if mod is None:
                return mod
            # LoRALinear stores the original frozen layer as base_layer
            if hasattr(mod, "base_layer") and hasattr(mod.base_layer, "weight"):
                return mod.base_layer
            return mod

        def _extract(mod: Any):
            """Return (weight_data, bias) from a module, or None if not a valid weight-holder."""
            mod = _unwrap(mod)
            if mod is None:
                return None
            if not hasattr(mod, "weight") or mod.weight is None:
                return None
            import torch
            import torch.nn as nn

            w = mod.weight
            # Must be either an nn.Parameter or a plain Tensor (Conv1D)
            if not (isinstance(w, nn.Parameter) or isinstance(w, torch.Tensor)):
                return None
            w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
            # Projections must strictly be 2D weight matrices (excludes 1D norms, 3D convolutions, etc.)
            if w.ndim != 2:
                return None
            # Conv1D stores weights transposed: [in_features, out_features]
            is_conv1d = (mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D") or hasattr(mod, "nf")
            if is_conv1d or (w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]):
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            bias = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
            bias = bias.data if (bias is not None and hasattr(bias, "data")) else bias
            # Qwen3.5 Gated Q: query is fused with gate (dim 0 has 2 * num_heads * head_dim)
            if proj == "q" and (
                hasattr(old_attn, "q_norm")
                or old_attn.__class__.__name__ == "Qwen3_5Attention"
                or hasattr(old_attn, "k_norm")
            ):
                cfg = getattr(old_attn, "config", None)
                tc = getattr(cfg, "text_config", cfg)
                h_dim = getattr(tc, "head_dim", 256)
                n_heads = getattr(tc, "num_attention_heads", getattr(old_attn, "num_heads", 8))
                if w.shape[0] == n_heads * h_dim * 2:
                    w = (
                        w.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim, -1)[:, 0, :, :]
                        .reshape(n_heads * h_dim, -1)
                        .contiguous()
                    )
                    if bias is not None:
                        bias = (
                            bias.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim)[:, 0, :]
                            .reshape(n_heads * h_dim)
                            .contiguous()
                        )
            return w, bias

        # 1. Search in target (old_attn.self or old_attn)
        for name in candidate_names:
            if hasattr(target, name):
                result = _extract(getattr(target, name))
                if result is not None:
                    return result

        # 2. Search in old_attn.output (BERT / RoBERTa output projection 'o')
        if proj == "o" and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
            out_mod = old_attn.output
            if hasattr(out_mod, "dense"):
                result = _extract(out_mod.dense)
                if result is not None:
                    return result
            result = _extract(out_mod)
            if result is not None:
                return result

        # 3. Search directly in old_attn (catches cases where there's no .self sub-module)
        for name in candidate_names:
            if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                result = _extract(getattr(old_attn, name))
                if result is not None:
                    return result

        # 4. Fallback for Fused GPT-2 c_attn (must precede positional fallback)
        if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
            fused_result = _extract(old_attn.c_attn)
            if fused_result is not None:
                w, b = fused_result
                split_w = w.size(0) // 3
                split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:split_w, :], (b[:split_b] if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :], (
                        b[split_b : 2 * split_b] if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[2 * split_w :, :], (b[2 * split_b :] if b is not None else None)

        # 4b. Fallback for Qwen3.5 / fused in_proj_qkv
        if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "in_proj_qkv"):
            fused_result = _extract(old_attn.in_proj_qkv)
            if fused_result is not None:
                w, b = fused_result
                k_dim = getattr(old_attn, "key_dim", getattr(old_attn, "k_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
                v_dim = getattr(old_attn, "value_dim", getattr(old_attn, "v_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
                q_dim = w.size(0) - k_dim - v_dim if (k_dim + v_dim < w.size(0)) else k_dim
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:q_dim, :], (b[:q_dim] if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :], (
                        b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim] if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :], (
                        b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim] if b is not None else None
                    )

        # 4c. PyTorch nn.MultiheadAttention in_proj_weight / separate proj_weight
        for obj in (target, old_attn):
            if (
                proj in ("q", "k", "v")
                and hasattr(obj, "in_proj_weight")
                and obj.in_proj_weight is not None
            ):
                w = (
                    obj.in_proj_weight.data
                    if hasattr(obj.in_proj_weight, "data")
                    else obj.in_proj_weight
                )
                b = getattr(obj, "in_proj_bias", None)
                b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                split_w = w.size(0) // 3
                split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:split_w, :].clone(), (b[:split_b].clone() if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :].clone(), (
                        b[split_b : 2 * split_b].clone() if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[2 * split_w :, :].clone(), (
                        b[2 * split_b :].clone() if b is not None else None
                    )

            if proj in ("q", "k", "v"):
                attr_w = f"{proj}_proj_weight"
                if hasattr(obj, attr_w) and getattr(obj, attr_w) is not None:
                    w = getattr(obj, attr_w)
                    w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
                    b = getattr(obj, f"{proj}_proj_bias", None)
                    b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                    return w.clone(), (b.clone() if b is not None else None)

        # 5. Positional fallback: scan all Linear children of target in declaration order.
        #    Only valid if at least 3 children exist (the typical pattern for BERT/RoBERTa variants).
        ordered_proj_map = {"q": 0, "k": 1, "v": 2}
        if proj in ordered_proj_map:
            children_with_weight = [
                (n, m) for n, m in target.named_children() if _extract(m) is not None
            ]
            if len(children_with_weight) >= 3:
                idx = ordered_proj_map[proj]
                if idx < len(children_with_weight):
                    return _extract(children_with_weight[idx][1])

        # 6. Fallback for 'o' projection when replacing leaf self-attention without local out_proj
        # (e.g. RoBERTa/BERT RobertaSelfAttention where output.dense resides in the parent container).
        # Returning an Identity matrix ensures:
        #   (a) custom modules don't crash during weight copying, and
        #   (b) context passes through transparently to parent.output.dense without double projection!
        if proj == "o":
            for mod in target.modules():
                if (
                    hasattr(mod, "weight")
                    and isinstance(getattr(mod, "weight", None), torch.Tensor)
                    and mod.weight.ndim == 2
                ):
                    d = mod.weight.shape[0]
                    eye_w = torch.eye(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                    zero_b = torch.zeros(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                    return eye_w, zero_b

        raise ValueError(
            f"Could not extract '{proj}' projection weights from {type(old_attn).__name__}"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def get_rotary_emb(old_attn: Any) -> Any | None:
        """Return rotary embeddings if present."""
        return getattr(old_attn, "rotary_emb", None)

    @staticmethod
    def get_kv_heads(config: Any) -> int:
        """Return number of key-value heads for GQA/MQA."""
        return getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", 12))

@staticmethod
def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any, proj: str) -> tuple:
    """Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

    Handles:
    - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module
    - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly
    - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj
    - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin
    - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight
    - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]
    """
    target = getattr(old_attn, "self", old_attn)
    candidate_names = CANONICAL_PROJECTION_ALIASES.get(proj, [])

    def _unwrap(mod: Any) -> Any:
        """Unwrap LoRALinear / merged adapter layers to their base_layer."""
        if mod is None:
            return mod
        # LoRALinear stores the original frozen layer as base_layer
        if hasattr(mod, "base_layer") and hasattr(mod.base_layer, "weight"):
            return mod.base_layer
        return mod

    def _extract(mod: Any):
        """Return (weight_data, bias) from a module, or None if not a valid weight-holder."""
        mod = _unwrap(mod)
        if mod is None:
            return None
        if not hasattr(mod, "weight") or mod.weight is None:
            return None
        import torch
        import torch.nn as nn

        w = mod.weight
        # Must be either an nn.Parameter or a plain Tensor (Conv1D)
        if not (isinstance(w, nn.Parameter) or isinstance(w, torch.Tensor)):
            return None
        w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
        # Projections must strictly be 2D weight matrices (excludes 1D norms, 3D convolutions, etc.)
        if w.ndim != 2:
            return None
        # Conv1D stores weights transposed: [in_features, out_features]
        is_conv1d = (mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D") or hasattr(mod, "nf")
        if is_conv1d or (w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]):
            w = w.t().contiguous()
        bias = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
        bias = bias.data if (bias is not None and hasattr(bias, "data")) else bias
        # Qwen3.5 Gated Q: query is fused with gate (dim 0 has 2 * num_heads * head_dim)
        if proj == "q" and (
            hasattr(old_attn, "q_norm")
            or old_attn.__class__.__name__ == "Qwen3_5Attention"
            or hasattr(old_attn, "k_norm")
        ):
            cfg = getattr(old_attn, "config", None)
            tc = getattr(cfg, "text_config", cfg)
            h_dim = getattr(tc, "head_dim", 256)
            n_heads = getattr(tc, "num_attention_heads", getattr(old_attn, "num_heads", 8))
            if w.shape[0] == n_heads * h_dim * 2:
                w = (
                    w.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim, -1)[:, 0, :, :]
                    .reshape(n_heads * h_dim, -1)
                    .contiguous()
                )
                if bias is not None:
                    bias = (
                        bias.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim)[:, 0, :]
                        .reshape(n_heads * h_dim)
                        .contiguous()
                    )
        return w, bias

    # 1. Search in target (old_attn.self or old_attn)
    for name in candidate_names:
        if hasattr(target, name):
            result = _extract(getattr(target, name))
            if result is not None:
                return result

    # 2. Search in old_attn.output (BERT / RoBERTa output projection 'o')
    if proj == "o" and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
        out_mod = old_attn.output
        if hasattr(out_mod, "dense"):
            result = _extract(out_mod.dense)
            if result is not None:
                return result
        result = _extract(out_mod)
        if result is not None:
            return result

    # 3. Search directly in old_attn (catches cases where there's no .self sub-module)
    for name in candidate_names:
        if hasattr(old_attn, name):
            result = _extract(getattr(old_attn, name))
            if result is not None:
                return result

    # 4. Fallback for Fused GPT-2 c_attn (must precede positional fallback)
    if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
        fused_result = _extract(old_attn.c_attn)
        if fused_result is not None:
            w, b = fused_result
            split_w = w.size(0) // 3
            split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:split_w, :], (b[:split_b] if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :], (
                    b[split_b : 2 * split_b] if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[2 * split_w :, :], (b[2 * split_b :] if b is not None else None)

    # 4b. Fallback for Qwen3.5 / fused in_proj_qkv
    if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "in_proj_qkv"):
        fused_result = _extract(old_attn.in_proj_qkv)
        if fused_result is not None:
            w, b = fused_result
            k_dim = getattr(old_attn, "key_dim", getattr(old_attn, "k_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
            v_dim = getattr(old_attn, "value_dim", getattr(old_attn, "v_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
            q_dim = w.size(0) - k_dim - v_dim if (k_dim + v_dim < w.size(0)) else k_dim
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:q_dim, :], (b[:q_dim] if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :], (
                    b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim] if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :], (
                    b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim] if b is not None else None
                )

    # 4c. PyTorch nn.MultiheadAttention in_proj_weight / separate proj_weight
    for obj in (target, old_attn):
        if (
            proj in ("q", "k", "v")
            and hasattr(obj, "in_proj_weight")
            and obj.in_proj_weight is not None
        ):
            w = (
                obj.in_proj_weight.data
                if hasattr(obj.in_proj_weight, "data")
                else obj.in_proj_weight
            )
            b = getattr(obj, "in_proj_bias", None)
            b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
            split_w = w.size(0) // 3
            split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:split_w, :].clone(), (b[:split_b].clone() if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :].clone(), (
                    b[split_b : 2 * split_b].clone() if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[2 * split_w :, :].clone(), (
                    b[2 * split_b :].clone() if b is not None else None
                )

        if proj in ("q", "k", "v"):
            attr_w = f"{proj}_proj_weight"
            if hasattr(obj, attr_w) and getattr(obj, attr_w) is not None:
                w = getattr(obj, attr_w)
                w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
                b = getattr(obj, f"{proj}_proj_bias", None)
                b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                return w.clone(), (b.clone() if b is not None else None)

    # 5. Positional fallback: scan all Linear children of target in declaration order.
    #    Only valid if at least 3 children exist (the typical pattern for BERT/RoBERTa variants).
    ordered_proj_map = {"q": 0, "k": 1, "v": 2}
    if proj in ordered_proj_map:
        children_with_weight = [
            (n, m) for n, m in target.named_children() if _extract(m) is not None
        ]
        if len(children_with_weight) >= 3:
            idx = ordered_proj_map[proj]
            if idx < len(children_with_weight):
                return _extract(children_with_weight[idx][1])

    # 6. Fallback for 'o' projection when replacing leaf self-attention without local out_proj
    # (e.g. RoBERTa/BERT RobertaSelfAttention where output.dense resides in the parent container).
    # Returning an Identity matrix ensures:
    #   (a) custom modules don't crash during weight copying, and
    #   (b) context passes through transparently to parent.output.dense without double projection!
    if proj == "o":
        for mod in target.modules():
            if (
                hasattr(mod, "weight")
                and isinstance(getattr(mod, "weight", None), torch.Tensor)
                and mod.weight.ndim == 2
            ):
                d = mod.weight.shape[0]
                eye_w = torch.eye(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                zero_b = torch.zeros(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                return eye_w, zero_b

    raise ValueError(
        f"Could not extract '{proj}' projection weights from {type(old_attn).__name__}"
    )

Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

Handles: - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]

Class variables

var constexpr

Special type indicating an unconstrained type.

  • Any is compatible with every type.
  • Any assumed to have all methods.
  • All values assumed to be instances of Any.

Note that all the above statements are true from the point of view of static type checkers. At runtime, Any should not be used with instance checks.

class triton

class _DummyTriton:
    @staticmethod
    def jit(*args, **kwargs):
        def decorator(fn):
            return fn
        if len(args) == 1 and callable(args[0]):
            return args[0]
        return decorator

    @staticmethod
    def autotune(*args, **kwargs):
        def decorator(fn):
            return fn
        if len(args) == 1 and callable(args[0]):
            return args[0]
        return decorator

    @staticmethod
    def Config(*args, **kwargs):
        return {}

    @staticmethod
    def cdiv(a, b):
        return (a + b - 1) // b

    @staticmethod
    def next_power_of_2(n):
        return 1 if n == 0 else 2 ** (int(n) - 1).bit_length()

class SDPBackend (...)

An enum-like class that contains the different backends for scaled dot product attention.

… warning:: This class is in beta and subject to change.

This backend class is designed to be used with the sdpa_kernel context manager.See :func: torch.nn.attention.sdpa_kernel for more details.
